nāṟumuṭi aḻuttaniṉ naṭunarōṭu cēṟi"

- naṟṟiṇai : 60 : 1-8





uḻavar makaḷiṉ aṉṟāṭak kaṭamai, uṇavu camaittal - ākum. avaḷ kātalaṉ, avaḷ peṟṟōriṉ viruntōmpum paṇpukku ēṟpa, ammaṉaipukum viruntiṉaruḷ oruvaṉākac ceṉṟu, taṉ kātaliyaik kaṇṭu makiḻvatōṭu, uḻavarvāḻ ciṟṟūrkaḷiṉ maṉai vāḻkkaiyiṉ māṇpu ettakaiyatu eṉpatai nām aṟiyavum ceytuḷḷāṉ.

vaḷaintu nīṇṭa kompukaḷaiyuṭaiya erumaiyiṉ taḷarnta naṭaiyuṭaiyavāya iḷam kaṉṟukaḷ vīṭṭut tūṇkaḷ tōṟum kaṭṭappaṭṭuk kāṇpataṟkuk kaḷip pūṭṭum nalla maṉaiyiṉ kaṇ, vaḷainta kuṇṭalaṅ kaḷaik kātil aṇintu koṇṭirukkum, ceḻitta uṭal nalam uṭaiyavaḷākiya nam kātali, ciṟiya kal mōtiram ceṟikkappaṭṭa melliya viralkaḷ civantu pōkumāṟu, vāḻaiyiṉ kuḷirnta iḷam taṇṭai , ciṟuciṟu tuṇṭukaḷāka naṟukki aṭicil ākkutalāl, pukai paṭintu civanta kaṇkaḷai uṭaiyaḷāki, aḻaku uṇṭākumāṟu, piṟaivaṭivāṉa neṟṟiyil uṇṭākiyirukkum ciṟuciṟu tuḷikaḷāṉa viyarvaiyait taṉṉuṭaiya aḻakiya cēlaittalaippāl tuṭaittuk koṇṭu , camaiyal muṭintum, kaṇavaṉ vantilaṉē eṉṟa niṉaippāl nam mītu koṇṭa ciṉattuṭaṉ aṭṭiṟ cālaiyil irukkiṟāḷ. viruntiṉarāy vara virumpuvār eṉṉōṭu varuvārkaḷāka; viruntiṉar. muṉṉē, aḻakiya māmai niṟam vāynta eṉ kātali, eṉṉaic ciṉantu kaṇ civantu kāṭci taramāṭṭāḷ, muḷpōlum ciṟiya paṟkaḷ ciṟitē tōṉṟap puṉṉakaiyē. kāṭṭuvaḷ; avaḷiṉ ciritē mukattai nāṉum kaṇṭu makiḻvēṉ”

"taṭamaruppu erumai maṭanaṭaik kuḻavi

tūṇtoṟum yātta, kāṇtaku nalil,

koṭuṅkuḻai peyta ceḻuñcey pēḻai ,

ciṟutāḻ ceṟitta melviral cēppa,

vāḻai īrntaṭi vallitiṉ vakai ip

pukai uṇṭu amarnta kaṇṇaṉ, takaipeṟap

piṟainutal poṟitta ciṟunuṇ palviyar

antukil talaiyil tuṭaiyiṉaḷ, nappulantu

aṭṭilōḷē, ammā arivai!

emakkē, varukatil viruntē; civappāḷaṉṟu;

ciṟiya muḷ eyiṟu tōṉṟa

muṟuval koṇṭa mukam kāṇ kammē"

- naṟṟiṇai - 120





ciṟṟūr āṭcimuṟai paṇṭaiya nāṭkaḷil, tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vāḻkkai muṟai, muḻukka muḻukka ciṟṟūrkaḷaic cārntē iruntatu. ciṟṟūrkaḷ ippoḻutaik kāṭṭilum, aṉṟu taṉṉiṟaivu peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉa. ciṟṟūr āṭcimuṟai, eḷitil viḷaṅkik koḷḷalākāc caṭṭaṅkaḷai etirnōkkiyirukkavillai. caṭṭaṅkaḷ mūlam, makkaḷ etai etirpārttaṉarō, atai, ammakkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷē vaḻaṅkiviṭṭaṉa. ētēṉum ōr iṭaiyūṟu, oru vaḻakku ēṟpaṭṭu viṭṭāl, ūrpperiyavarkaḷ, tāḻttappaṭṭa, piṟpaṭuttappaṭṭa makkaḷ iṉṟum ceyvatupōl, ūrnaṭuvē niṟkum marattaṭiyil, potuvāka, ālamarattaṭiyil kūṭiyiruntu, oru kalayam kaḷḷukku, avvaḻakkiṉait tīrttuviṭuvar. attaku maraṅkaḷiṉ aṭiyil irukkum tiṟantaveḷi, potiyil, potiyam, potuvil, maṉṟu, maṉṟam" eṉṟellām aḻaikkappaṭum. maṉṟam, eṉṟāl potuvāka ūrp potuviṭam eṉappaṭum. ūrkkālnaṭaikaḷ āṅkē maṭakki vaikkappaṭum. kaṉṟukaḷai aḻaikkum 'ammā eṉum kural eḻuppiyavāṟē maṉṟil pukuntu niṟaintuviṭum. ('kaṉṟupayir kural maṉṟu niṟaipukutarum" (akanāṉūṟu: 14:11) itē vari, kuṟiñcippāṭṭilum, evvita māṟṟamum iṉṟi iṭam peṟṟuḷatu. (kuṟiñcippāṭṭu : 278) kālnaṭaikaḷ, varicai varicaiyāka maṉṟuḷ nuḻaivataiyum, kollēṟu : eṉum kāḷaikaḷ, ammaṉṟattil tirivataiyum puṟanāṉūṟṟu varikaḷ uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. maṉṟu niṟaiyum nirai (puṟam : 387 : 24), "kollēṟu tiritarumaṉṟam" (puṟam : 309:4) maram ceṭi, koṭikaḷ aṟṟa, mēṭu paḷḷam illāta tiṟanta veḷikaḷum, potu nikaḻccikaḷukkāka otukki vaikkap paṭum, attaku iṭaṅkaḷum maṉṟam eṉa aḻaikkappaṭum. āṅku makaḷir celvatum, kuravai pōlum kūttāṭuvatum ceyvar. "meṉtōḷ makaḷir maṉṟam pēṇār" (puṟam : 375:12) maṉṟu toṟum niṉṟa kuravai, (maturaikkāñci : 615) periyāḻvār tirumoḻi, maṉṟil kuravai āṭiya tirumālaik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. ‘'maṉṟil kuravai piṇainta māl” (1 : 2 : 2:12)

itu pōlum tiṟanta veḷikaḷil, avvannilattukkē uriya maraṅkaḷ vaḷarkkappaṭum pāṇṭiyarkku uriya maṉṟattil vēmpu vaḷarkkappaṭṭu, taṅkaḷ talaimālaikku vēṇṭum taḻai ammarattiliruntu koyyappaṭum. pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ. ūr maṉṟattē irukkum vēmpiṉ periya kiḷaikaḷil uḷḷa taḷirkaḷai uḻiñaik koṭiyōṭu kalantu kaṭṭiya talai mālai aṇintu pōrukkup puṟappaṭṭāṉ.

maṉṟa vēmpiṉ mācciṉai oṇtaḷir

neṭuṅkoṭi uḻiñaip pavaroṭumiṭaintu

ceṟiyat toṭutta tēmpāy kaṇṇi

- puṟam : 76 : 4-6





vēmpu niṟkum ūrmaṉṟattil uḷḷa kuḷattuk kuḷir nīril mūḻki ammarattu oḷḷiya taḻaikaḷai aṇintu koṇṭāṉ: “mūtūr vāyil paṉikkayam maṇṇi, maṉṟa vēmpiṉ oṇkuḻai malaintu” (puṟam: 79 : 1-2) palvēṟu nilaṅkaḷaic cērnta maṉṟaṅkaḷil, avvannilattukku uriyavāy niṟkum palvēṟu maraṅkaḷāvaṉa. 1. palā : “maṉṟap palaviṉ mācciṉai,” ‘'maṉṟappalaviṉ mālvarai” (puṟam : 128-1: 476:5) 2. viḷām: ‘'maṉṟa viḷaviṉ maṉaivīḻ veḷḷil” : (puṟam: 181:1) 3. ilantai : “iratti nīṭiya akaṉṟalai maṉṟam ; “ . “maṟukuṭaṉ kamaḻum matukai maṉṟattu alantalai iratti alaṅkupaṭu nīḻal” (puṟam: 34:12, 325 : 10-11) puṉṉai : ‘'maṉṟap puṉṉai mācciṉai “ (puṟam : 49:8)

potiyil, maṉṟam eṉṟa coṟkaḷ, ūrpperiyavarkaḷ oṉṟu kūṭiyiruntu, ūr nikaḻccikaḷaip pēcit tīrkkum marattu nīḻal, potuvāka ālamarattu nīḻal eṉṟum poruḷ paṭum. ammaram, ūrmukappil niṟkum. piṟkālattavarāya māmūlaṉārum attaku marattaṭi maṉṟam, iṉṟu iruppatu, pōlavē, tam kālattum iruntataik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ‘'muṉṉūrp potiyil” (puṟam: 390:19) “toṉmutu ālattu arumpaṇaip potiyil" (akam: 251:8) nakaraṅkaḷ eḻuntatum, aracarkaḷ, mutalil oru potukkuṭicaiyai, aṭuttu, oru potu vīṭṭai allatu nakar araṅkaik kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ. avaiyum paṇṭaip peyarilēyē aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉa. atil, kantu allatu kantam eṉappaṭum marak kiḷai naṭappaṭṭu, ammarattuṇṭil iṭam peṟṟiruppatākak karutappaṭum kaṭavuḷ, taṇṇumai muḻakkattiṟkiṭaiyē vaḻipaṭap peṟum. "potuvil tūṅkum viciyuṟu taṇṇumai" (puṟam : 89:7). ivvaraṅkukaḷil, āriyarkaḷiṭaiyē, nikaḻntatu pōlavē, paṇṭaik kālattil camaya nikaḻccikaḷōṭu naṭaipeṟṟatāṉa cūtāṭṭa nikaḻcciyum naṭaipeṟṟatu. kīḻvarum pāṭṭu, avvāṟu pāḻuṟṟuk kaiviṭappaṭṭa oru kōyil paṟṟiya viḷakkamām. "vīṭutōṟum mīṉ cuṭutalāl pulāl nāṟṟattōṭu kalantu eḻum pukaiyiṉ nīṇṭa oḻuṅku, vayalarukē niṟkum marutamarattu vaḷainta kōṭukaḷil ceṉṟu cūḻntu koḷḷum periya varuvāy ippōtu illaiyākip pōkavē, muḻavu mutalāyiṉa muḻaṅka, paliyiṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭa kaṭavuḷ ellām, tām kuṭiyirunta kantakaṅkaḷai viṭṭup pōyviṭṭaṉa. ataṉāl pāḻuṟṟup pōṉa ūr maṉṟaṅkaḷil paṇṭellām, naraitiraiyuṟṟa mutiyōr ōyvāka amarntu cūtāṭuṅkāl, cūtāṭukaruvikaḷ vīḻntu vīḻntu paṇṇiviṭṭa kuḻikaḷ, uṭalellām puḷḷikaḷaik koṇṭa kāṭṭuk kōḻikaḷ iṭṭa muṭṭaikaḷāl niṟaintuviṭum"

"mīṉcuṭu pukaiyiṉ pulavunāṟu neṭuṅkoṭi

vayaluḻai marutiṉ. vāṅkuciṉai valakkum

perunal yāṇariṉ orīi, iṉiyē

kalikeḻu kaṭavuḷ kantam kaiviṭap,

palikaṇ māṟiya pāḻpaṭu potiyil

naraimū tāḷar nāyiṭak kuḻinta

vallil nallakam niṟaiyap palpoṟik

kāṉa vāraṇam īṉam"

puṟam - 52-9-16;





itu pōlavē pāḻuṟṟup pōṉa potiyilil uḷḷa paḷḷaṅkaḷiliruntu koṇṭuvanta taṇṇīrai naṟṟiṇai kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. ‘'maṉṟattuk kalluṭaip paṭuviṟ kaluḻi” (39 :3-4) atē tokai nūlil, ‘'muruṅkai marattil, vēṉiṟ kālattil kāyntu muṟṟiya neṟṟu pōṉṟa aḻaku illāta viralkaḷaiyuṭaiya , accam ūṭṭum, pēroli eḻuppum vāyaiyuṭaiya pēy, vaḷappattaip paṇṭu uṭaiyatāki irunta ūriṉkaṇ uḷḷa, teyvattai vaḻipaṭṭu iṭum paliccōṟṟai uṇṇuvāṉ virumpip pāḻmaṉṟam nōkki mōti eḻum”, tuṉpamikka mālaippoḻutu viḷakkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. “vēṉil murukkiṉ viḷai tuṇar aṉṉa

māṇā virala valvāyp pēey

mallal mūtūr malarppali uṇīiya

maṉṟam pōḻum puṉkaṇ mālai

naṟṟiṇai : 73 : 1-4





mēlum piṟpaṭṭa kālaṅkaḷil, ūrmaṉṟamākap periya māḷikaikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭu, maṉṉarkaḷaip pāṭip paricil peṟuvāṉ vēṇṭivarum pulavarkaḷum piṟa iravalarkaḷum taṅkum iṭamākavum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. marattālāṉa pala kampaṅkaḷaik koṇṭa nakar araṅkiṉ oru pakkam, paḷḷiyākap payaṉpaṭṭataip puṟanāṉūṟu kāṭṭukiṟatu, "palkāl potiyil oruciṟai paḷḷi āka” (puṟam : 375 : 2-3) "maṉṟupaṭu paricilar” (puṟam:135: 17) nakaril uḷḷa, maṉṟam, potiyil pōlum iṭaṅkaḷellām piṟkālattil, kōyil teyvattirumēṉikaḷiṉ uṟaiviṭaṅkaḷākiviṭṭaṉa. maṉṟamum potiyil pōlum iṭaṅkaḷellām piṟkālattil, kōyil teyvat tirumēṉikaḷiṉ uṟaiviṭaṅkaḷākiviṭṭaṉa. "maṉṟamum potiyilum kantuṭai nilaiyilum" (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai : 226) vaḻakkaṟukkum naṭuvar maṉṟamākavum viḷaṅkiya uṟaiyūr nakar araṅkam, puṟanāṉūṟṟil (220 : 7) “mūtūr maṉṟam” eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu, eṉṟālum urai āciriyar oruvar, maṉṟam eṉpataṟkuk kutiraikaḷ ōṭum ceṇṭuveḷi" eṉap poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷār.

aṟam vaḻaṅkum ūrpperiyavarkaḷ kūṭum ālamarattilum, ūr mutiyōr ōyvu koḷḷum paḻaiya kuṭilkaḷilum naṭappaṭṭa kantu eṉum marakkaḻikaḷilum iṭam peṟṟirunta kaṭavuḷ etu eṉṉum viṉā viṭai kāṇamāṭṭātē niṟkiṟatu. ivviṉāviṟku viṭai kāṇpataṟku muṉṉar, nām āyntu koṇṭirukkum ikkālattiṟku mikamika muṟpaṭṭa kālamākiya kaṟkāla kaṭṭattil, aiyattiṟku iṭam iṉṟi vaḻipaṭuvataṟkāka eṉṟē kal liṅkaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu kuṟippiṭal poruntum. ulōkak kālam (vaṭa intiyāvil ceppuk kālam, teṉ intiyāvil irumpukkālam toṭaṅkiya piṉṉarum kal iliṅka vaṭivaṅkaḷ toṭarntu vaḻipaṭṭu varalāyiṉa. liṅkam pōl tōṟṟam aḷikkum. cila poruṭkaḷ, hārappā, mōkaṉ jōtarōkkaḷil tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil iraṇṭu vakaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. oṉṟu uyarattil arai aṅkulattiliruntu ōr aṭivarai vēṟupaṭum caturaṅka viḷaiyāṭṭukkāykaḷ pōlum vaṭivuṭaiyaṉa; iraṇṭu, kuṟukkē arai aṅkulattiliruntu 3 aṭi allatu 4 aṭi uyaram uḷḷa vaṭṭakkal vaṭivuṭaiyaṉa. "avaṟṟuḷ cila kāṇappaṭṭa cūḻnilaiyōṭu, atu toṭarpāṉa poruḷ kaḷiṉ tokutiyiṉaiyum iṇaittup pārkkumpōtu, avvuṇmaikaḷ vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaikku uriya, yātō cila poruḷkaḷ aṅku iruntaṉa eṉṟa muṭiviṟkuc ciṟu cantēkattiṟkum iṭam vaikkavillai. avvaṭṭakkal, mesapaṭōmiyāvil kāṇakkiṭaitta oru poruḷōṭu orumaippāṭuṭaiyatāṉa ceṇṭu allatu taṇṭāyutattiṉ talaiyāka irukkalām eṉṟa karuttum kūṟappaṭṭatu. āṉāl nūlāciriyariṉ karuttuppaṭi, palapaṭiyālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷak kūṭiya viḷakkam, vaṭṭakkal, yōṉi ākum. caturaṅkakkāy pōlum poruḷ liṅkam ākum eṉpatē ām. yōṉiyum liṅkamum, vaṭakōṭi mutal teṉkōṭivarai, kīḻkkōṭi mutal mēṟkōṭi varaiyāṉa intiyā muḻuvatum, civaṉaik kuṟikka ellōrkkum viḷaṅkum vaṭivamākum. liṅka vaḻipāṭu, āriyar varukaikku mikavum muṟpaṭṭa, naṉmikap paḻaiya vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaiyām eṉpatil evvita viṉāviṟkum iṭam illai.'’ eṉkiṟār tiruvāḷar jāṉ mārṣel avarkaḷ. (Archaeological Survey of India. Annual Report. 1925-26, page:79)

teṉṉintiyāvil, ki.pi. 500kku muṟpaṭṭa kālattaic cērnta pāṭalkaḷil, civaṉ, ālamar celvaṉākavē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷāṉ eṉpatu īṇṭuk kuṟippiṭal poruntum, puṟanāṉūṟu pāṭṭu oṉṟu, ālamarattaik kaṭavuḷukku uriyatākak kūṟukiṟatu. ‘kaṭavuḷ ālam'’ (1:7) vēṟucila pāṭalkaḷ, civaṉai, ālil uṟaiyum iṟaivaṉām eṉat teḷivāka uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. “ālamar celvaṉ'’ (ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭai: 1:97; kalittokai : 81:9:83:45), "ālkeḻu kaṭavuḷ” (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai : 256). ippāṭalkaḷ, āriyak karuttukaḷ, tamiḻppāṭalkaḷiṭaiyē iṭam peṟat toṭaṅkiya kālattaic cērntavai eṉṟālum kantu eṉappaṭum kampattil iṭam peṟṟa kaṭavuḷum, ālamarattil iṭam peṟṟa kaṭavuḷum oṉṟē. paṇṭait tamiḻ ilakkiya kālattil civaṉ, oru nilattiṉ teyvamāka iṭam peṟavillai. avaṉ oru ciṟu teyvamē murukaṉukkut tantaiyāka uṇarntu muṭivāka, aintu āṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil, caiva ākama neṟi, tamiḻnāṭṭil paraviyapōtu perunteyvam āyiṉāṉ. vaṭa intiya rutraṉ, toṭakkattil, oru malaināṭṭut talaivaṉ makaḷai maṇantu koṇṭa oru malaikkaṭavuḷāvaṉ. ākavē avaṉ taṅkaḷ tava oḻukkattai mēṟkoḷvāṉ vēṇṭi - malaikaḷil ceṉṟu taṅkum yōkiyarkaḷukkuk kaṭavuḷāki viṭṭāṉ. teṉṉāṭṭiṟku avaṉ kuṭipeyarntatum, civavaḻipāṭu ciṟantu vaḷarcciyaip peṟṟu viṭṭatu. āṉāl atu nikaḻvataṟku muṉṉar, kiṭaikkak kūṭiya naṉimikap paḻaiya tamiḻppāṭalkaḷil kāṇappaṭuvatu pōl, tamiḻ nāṭṭil, avaṉ ālamarattuk kaṭavuḷākavē iruntuḷḷāṉ, oruvēḷai, ālamarattu niḻalil oru liṅkam naṭappaṭṭirukkakkūṭum. aṅṅaṉam illai eṉṟāl, avaṉ ālamar celvaṉāka ākiyirukka muṭiyātu. marutam, ilantai, nāval pōlum marattaṭikaḷilum, liṅkam vaikkappaṭṭatu. civa vaḻipāṭu peruki, ammaraṅkaḷai uḷḷaṭakkic civaṉ kōyilkaḷ kaṭṭat toṭaṅkiya aintām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟaku, avaiyellām pukaḻ peṟṟa vaḻipāṭṭiṭaṅkaḷāki viṭṭaṉa. ippōtu teṉṉāṭṭil, mikavum pukaḻ peṟṟu viḷaṅkum civaṉ kōyilkaḷil, ivaiyum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. puṉita maraṅkaḷiliruntu veṭṭappaṭṭu, immaram, ammarattaṭi liṅka vaṭivam ākiya iraṇṭiṉ iṟaittaṉmaiyum koṇṭuviṭṭa kōyil kuṭilkaḷil naṭappaṭṭu, kantu eṉappaṭum akkaḻikaḷum, liṅkamākak karutiyē naṭappaṭṭirukkak kūṭum.

nakaraṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam

oru nilattup paṇṭaṅkaḷaip piṟa nilaṅkaḷiṉ paṇṭaṅkaḷukku vilaiyākap paṇṭamāṟṟam ceytu koṇṭataṉ viḷaivākavē, toṭakkattil nakaraṅkaḷ tōṉṟalāyiṉa. kaṭaṟkarai nāṭṭiṉ poruḷām uppu, ēṉaiya nāṭukaḷil, iṉṟiyamaiyāp poruḷākiviṭṭatu. ataṉāl naṉimikap paḻaṅkālattiliruntē, uppaic cila camayam ularnta mīṉaiyum ēṟṟik koṇṭa, taḷḷāṭittaḷḷāṭic cellum kaṭṭai vaṇṭikaḷiṉ varicai, maṇ cālaikaḷaik kaṭantu, malai nāṭṭiṉ aṭivaraikkaṇṇatāṉa uḷnāṭukaḷukkuc cellalāyiṉa. uppu vaṇṭikaḷum, uppu vaṇikarum pulavarkaḷāl, aṭikkaṭi kūṟappaṭṭaṉar. "kuṉṟiṉ mītu tōṉṟum uppu vaṇikar talaivaṉ", "kuṉṟil tōṉṟum kulavumaṇal cērppa" (akam : 310:10) (kuṟippu : 'kulavumaṇal" eṉṟa toṭaraik kuva, umaṇar" eṉac coṟcitaivu ceytu avvāṟu poruḷ koṇṭuḷḷār. atu tavaṟu. kuṉṟupōl tōṉṟum, maṇalmēṭukaḷ niṟainta kaṭaṟkarai, talaivaṉē eṉpatutāṉ attoṭar uṇarttum poruḷākum. atē akattil, pālaiyil payaṇam koṇṭa uppu vaṇikar, āṅkē mūṉṟu kaṟkaḷāl ceytu koṇṭa aṭuppu uṇarttappaṭṭuḷatu. “umaṇ cāttu iṟanta oḻikal aṭuppu” (akam : 119 : 8). appākkaḷ kūṟum viḷakkat tiliruntu, paṇṭaṅkaḷ vaṇṭi vaṇṭiyāka, ōriṭattiliruntu piṟa iṭaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu terikiṟatu. kaṭalaic cārnta nilattil, kaḻinīrāl viḷainta uppai ēṟṟik koṇṭu, malaināṭṭai nōkkic cellum vaṇṭiyiṉ, ārakkālkaḷaiyuṭaiya cakkaram paḷḷattil pāyntuviṭumpōtu, taṉ uraṅkoṇṭu attaḷarcci tīrttu īrttuc cellum, perumpāram tāṅkavalla mikka valiyuṭaiya kāḷaikku ōr aracaṉ oppuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷāṉ.

"kāṉal kaḻiuppu mukantu kalnāṭu maṭukkum

āraic cākāṭṭu āḻcci pōkkum

uraṉuṭai nōṉpakaṭṭu aṉṉa eṅkōṉ”

- puṟam : 60 : 6-9





iṭaivaḻiyil murivuṟṟup pōyiṉ, āṅku utavuvataṟkākat taṉiyāka ōr accu uṭaṉ koṇṭu cellumaḷavu vaṇṭikaḷil perumpāram ēṟṟappaṭum; avvāṟu taṉiyē eṭuttuc cellum accu ‘'cēm accu” eṉappaṭum. erutukaḷ iḷaiyaṉa: nukattaṭiyil itaṟku muṉ pūṭṭi aṟiyātaṉa. vaṇṭiyilō paṇṭap potikaḷ mikap perumaḷavil ēṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mēṭu nōkki ēṟum pōtō, paḷḷam nōkkip pāyum pōtō ētēṉum ākak kūṭum atai muṉkūṭṭi aṟital iyalātu. etaṟkum taṉiyē ōr accu uṭaṉ koṇṭu cēral nallatu eṉa eṇṇum umaṇar, uḷḷa acciṉ kīḻē, vēṟōr accait taṉiyē pirittuk koṇṭu celvar eṉkiṟatu puṟanāṉūṟṟup pāṭṭu oṉṟu.

‘erutē iḷaiya nukamuṇa rāvē; :

cakaṭam paṇṭam peritu pey taṉṟē;

aval iḻiyiṉam, micai ēṟiṉum

avaṇatu aṟiyunar yār? eṉa umaṇar

kīḻmarattu yātta cēm accu”

puṟam : 102 : 1-5





uppu vaṇṭikaḷ oḻuṅkākac celvatu, iḷam ciṟārkaḷukkuk kaḷippūṭṭum kaṇkāṭciyām. iṉiya nīr niṟaintu, mikka aḻakuṭaiyatāṉa cuṉaiyil pūtta ceṅkaḻunīriṉ moṭṭu aviḻnta malarnta malarkaḷaip puṟa itaḻkaḷai nīkkiviṭṭu muḻu malarkaḷāl āṉa āṭai acaiyum alkulaiyum, pēraḻaku vāyntu aruḷ oḻukum kaṇkaḷaiyum, iṉiya iḷa nakaiyiṉaiyum uṭaiya iḷamakaḷir, pul rueṅka muḷaittuk kavartta vaḻiyiṉaiyum, neruṅka muḷvaittuc ceyta vēliyiṉaiyum, pañcu niṟainta muṟṟattiṉaiyum uṭaiya vīṭṭiṉ orupāl uḷḷa pīrkkaṅ koṭiyum, curaikkoṭiyum paṭarntu kiṭakkum, īcca marattu ilaikaḷaiyum koṇṭa kuppai mītu ēṟi niṉṟu, umaṇar vaṇṭikaḷai, oṉṟu iraṇṭu eṉa eṇṇik koṇṭirukkum kāṭci oṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟup pāṭal oṉṟil iṭam peṟṟuḷatu. "tīnīrp peruṅkuṇṭu cuṉaippūtta kuvaḷaik

kūmpu aviḻmuḻuneṟi puraḷvarum alkul,

ēntueḻil maḻaikkaṇ; iṉṉakai makaḷir,

puṉmūcu kavalaiya muḷmiṭai vēlip

pañcu muṉṟil ciṟṟil āṅkaṇ

pīrai nāṟiya curai ivar maruṅkiṉ

īttu ilaik kuppaiēṟi, umaṇar

uppoy oḻukai eṇṇum"

- puṟam : 116: 1-8





oru nilattiliruntu oru nilattiṟku uppaik koṇṭu celvatu ivvaḷavu periya aḷavil naṭaipeṟṟatu eṉṟāl, maṟṟap paṇṭaṅkaḷ, ataiviṭa mikap periya aḷavil koṇṭucellappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉa uṟutiyāka nampalām. avvāṟē, ariciyum parutti āṭaikaḷum āṟṟup paḷḷattākkup pakutiyiliruntu vaṟaṇṭa pakutikkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭaṉa. payaṟu vakaikaḷum pālpaṭu poruḷkaḷum mullaip pakutiyiliruntu naṉcey nilappakutikkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭaṉa. tiṉai, tēṉ, maṟṟum malaipaṭuporuḷkaḷ, maṟṟap pakutikaḷukkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭaṉa. ippaṇṭaṅkaḷ mullai, kuṟiñci pōlum mēṭṭu nilamum tāḻvāṉa nilapparappum iṇaiyum iṭaṅkaḷil, perumaḷavil paṇṭamāṟṟam ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. attaku paṇṭamāṟṟumiṭaṅkaḷellām perunakaraṅkaḷāka vaḷarntuviṭṭaṉa.

ākavē, vaṟaṇṭa nilamām puṉcey nilappakutiyilum, nīrvaḷam mikka naṉcey nilappakutiyum iṇaiyum, atāvatu, āṟu, taṉ iṭainilaip pōkkaik kaiviṭuttu, camanilattil amaitiyāka ōṭat toṭaṅkiya iṭattil, mutaṉmutalāka nakaraṅkaḷ eḻalāyiṉa. cōḻar talainakar maturai ākiya paḻamperum nakaraṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟattiṟku ituvē kāraṇam. innakaraṅkaḷ, parutti viḷaiyum pakutikaḷiliruntu parutti koṇṭu varappaṭṭu, eṅku āṭaiyāka neyyappaṭumō, anta iṭaṅkaḷil, iṉṟē pōl, paṇṭum, āṭai, necavukkup peyarpeṟṟirunta iṭaṅkaḷil, iṭam peṟṟu uḷḷaṉa. ivviṭaṅkaḷil vaḷarntu perukiya vāṇikam, tamiḻaracarkaḷiṉ talainakarām perumaiyai, annakaraṅkaḷ toṭakkattilēyē peṟat tuṇai purintaṉa. innakaraṅkaḷ kuṟitta viḷakkam paḻampāṭalkaḷil iṭam peṟavillai.

mukkiya tuṟaimukaṅkaḷ

ikkāla kaṭṭattil tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ mikap periya veḷināṭṭu vāṇikam, aṭutta nūṟṟāṇṭil peripuḷūs āciriyarālum, tālamiyālum virivāka eṭuttuk kūṟappaṭṭa eṇṇaṟṟa tuṟaimukaṅkaḷ iṭam peṟat tuṇaipurintatu. muntiya atikārattil eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya petta jātakā kataiyiliruntu, ki.mu. mutal āyirattāṇṭil, kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉam, cōḻa aracarkaḷiṉ mikap periya tuṟaimukamākavum, avarkaḷiṉ iraṇṭāvatu talainakarākavum tikaḻntatu eṉpatai aṟintukoṇṭōm. camaskiruta eḻuttāḷarkaḷāl pāṇṭiyariṉ nuḻaivāyil eṉum poruḷil, ‘'pāṇṭiya kavāṭam” eṉa aḻaikkappaṭṭa koṟkai, pāṇṭiyar talainakarām perumaiyai maturaiyiliruntu paṟittuk koṇṭu, pāṇṭiyar talainakar, aṇmaiyil, maturaikku māṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉat tālami kūṟum kālam varai, takka vaittuk koṇṭiruntatu. koṟkaiyiṉ mukkiyattum, paṇṭaiyōrāl peritum pukaḻappaṭṭa muttu vāṇikattiṉ nilaikkaḷamāy uṇmaiyilēyē iṭam peṟṟatu. cērarkaḷiṉ mukkiyat tuṟaimukaṅkaḷ. mēṟku āciyāvukkum, ekiptukkum, avaṟṟiṟku appālum, miḷakaiyum, maṟṟum maṇam taru uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum ēṟṟumati ceyta muciṟiyum, toṇṭiyumām.

aracarkaḷ

nākarīka vāḻvu kāṇā appaḻaṅkālattil, aracaṉuk kāṉa nirvākap paṇikaḷ mikaccilavē; āṭci muṟaiyil ' tēvaippaṭum oru cilavum, ūr aṟaṅkūr avaikaḷāl, naṉmikap paḻaiya muṟaikkēṟpa niṟaivēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyarākiya mūvēntarkaḷ, oruvarōṭoruvar toṭarntu pōriṭṭuk koṇṭiruntaṉar. appōtellām nāṭu piṭippataṟkāka eṉṟu illāmal taṅkaḷ vīrattai paṟaicāṟṟavum, piṟa aracarkaḷaik kāṭṭilum, tāmē periyavaṉ, pēraracaṉ eṉṟa nilaiyai nāṭṭavumē naṭaipeṟṟaṉa. ammūṉṟu nāṭukaḷiṉ ellaikaḷ oru nilaiyilēyē iruntaṉa. kaḷattil veṟṟi kaṇṭiruntālum ellaikaḷ māṟṟam peṟavillai. amarāvati āṟu, kāviriyōṭu kalakkum mūṉṟu mukkōṇaṅkaḷiṉ potuvāṉa muṉaiyil cantikkiṉṟaṉa. anta iṭattil, mūvēntarkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭukaḷai oru cērap peṟṟavaḷum, aṉpukkaṭavuḷ - vaḷam - vaḻaṅkum kaṭavuḷ eṉṟellām pārāṭṭap peṟupavaḷumāṉa cellāyi eṉṟa peṇ teyvattukku, oru ciṟu kōyil niṟkiṟatu. akkōyil niṟkum iṭam, eṉakku uriyatu, eṉa yārum vātāṭa māṭṭā oruvarkkē uriya iṭamāka uḷatu. avviṭattil kāviriyōṭu kalakkum, "karaipōṭṭāṉāṟu" eṉṟa poruḷ potinta peyaruṭaiya oru ciṟṟāṟu, iru nāṭukaḷukkumiṭaiyē ellai vakuttu, mēṟkuk kaṭaṟkarai varai nīṇṭirukkum cōḻa nāṭṭiliruntu pirikkiṟatu, kāviriyil akkaraiyil, paṇṭu iruntataṟkāṉa aṭiccuvaṭu iṉṟum kāṇappaṭum, ceyaṟkaiyālāṉa oru mēṭu, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷiṉ ellaiyaik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. (puṟanāṉūṟu 47 ām eṇ ceyyuṭkuk kīḻvarum koḷuvilum, maṇimēkalai, 19 ām kātai 126 ām variyilum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭirukkum, kāriyāṟṟut tuñciya neṭuṅkiḷḷi iṟanta kāriyāṟē, intak karai pōṭṭāṉāṟātal kūṭum. piṉṉark kūṟiya maṇimēkalaip pakutiyil cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ ellaikku arukil uḷḷa kāriyāṟṟiṉ karaiyil naṭaipeṟṟa pōr oṉṟu kuṟippiṭattakkatu) cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ talainakar, uṟaiyūr, āṟṟiṉ kīḻppakutiyil uḷatu. cēra nāṭṭiṉ talainakar karūr āṟṟiṉ mēṟpakutiyil uḷḷatu. pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ talainakar maturai, āṟṟiṟkut teṟkil uḷatu.

aracarkaḷiṉ talaiyāya paṇi, nāṭṭai āṭumāṭukaḷaik koḷḷaiyaṭittuc celpavariṭamiruntu kāttal, kālnaṭai kaḷ, oṉṟu, maṟavar, kaḷḷarkaḷiṉ iṉattalaivarkaḷām koḷḷaik kūṭṭattalaivarkaḷāl kaṭattic cellappaṭum. allatu, annāṭṭu aracaṉōṭu viṭutta aṟaikūvalāka, maṟṟoru nāṭṭu aracaṉāl kaṭattic cellappaṭum. ivviraṇṭil etu kuṟittup pōr mēṟkoṇṭālum, aracaṉ taṉ kulattukku uriya mālaiyōṭu, cōḻaṉāyiṉ, āttimālaiyōṭu, pāṇṭiyaṉāyiṉ vēppa mālaiyōṭu, cēraṉāyiṉ paṉaimālaiyōṭu, tāṉ kuṟittuc cellum pōrukku uriya aṭaiyāḷa mālaiyaiyum, aṇintu celvar. nāṭu kāvalai aṭuttu aracaṉukku uriya piṟitoru talaiyāya kaṭamai, tam pākkaḷ mūlam taṉ pukaḻ pāṭum pulavar pōlum iravalarkaḷai aḻaittut taṉṉaic cūḻa vaittuk koṇṭu, avarkaḷ pārāṭṭu kēṭṭu makiḻntu paricāka, nalla uṇavum, matuvum, niṟaiporuḷum koṭuttal. kīḻvarum ceyyuḷ, ciṟitu piṟpaṭṭa kālattaic cērnta tēyāyilum, aracarkaḷiṉ ikkaṭamaikaḷait teḷivāka uṇarttukiṟatu. avaiyē, akkālattum irunta uṇmaiyāṉa nilaiyākum. "kuṟaiyāta vaṇmaiyum, pakai aḻikkum pērāṇmaiyum vāynta talaiva, uṉ paṭaiyaic cērnta yāṉaikaḷ malaikaḷ pōl kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟaṉa. niṉ nāṟpaṭaiyiṉ āravārappēroli, kaṭal alaipōl muḻuṅkukiṟatu : kūriya muṉaiyai uṭaiya uṉ vēṟpaṭai miṉṉal pōl oḷi vīcukiṟatu. ittaku peru nilaiyāl ulakattup pēraracarkaḷellām uḷam oṭuṅkuvataṟku ētuvāya pērāṟṟalōṭu tikaḻkiṉṟaṉai : niṉakkō niṉ nāṭṭiṟkō varakkūṭiya kuṟṟam etuvum, illaiyāyiṟṟu; innilai, uṉ kuṭikku vaḻivaḻiyāka varum perumaiyākum. ataṉāl, ulakattu āṟukaḷ ellām, malaikaḷiliruntu vīḻntu viraintu kaṭalai nōkki aṭaivatu pōl, pulavarkaḷum iravalarkaḷum uṉṉai nōkki varuvārāyiṉar".

"āṉā īkai, aṭupōr aṇṇal!

yāṉaiyum malaiyiṉ tōṉṟum; peruma!

niṉ tāṉaiyum kaṭal eṉa muḻaṅkum; kūrnuṉai,

vēlum miṉṉiṉ viḷaṅkum ulakattu

araicu talaipaṇikkum āṟṟalai; ātaliṉ

puraitīrntaṉṟu; putuvatō aṉṟē;

..........................

malaiyiṉ iḻintu mākkaṭal nōkki

nilavarai iḻitarum palyāṟu pōlap

pulavar ellām niṉnōk kiṉarē".

(puṟam : 42 : 7-6; 19-21)





veḷināṭṭu vāṇikam ki.mu. 600 mutal ki.pi. 14 varai

tāriyas āṭciyil

ki.mu. 606-l assiriyap pēraracu kaviḻkkappaṭṭatu. atu nikaḻntatum, pāpilōṉ, āciya vāṇikat talaimaiyiṭamāka ākiviṭṭatu. yavaṉar, yūtar poyṉīṣiyar, intiyar, cīṉar, eṉṟa ulaka vaṇika iṉaṅkaḷ, taṅkaḷ vaṇikap paṇṭaṅkaḷaip , pāpilōṉiya cantaikaḷukku eṭuttuc ceṉṟaṉa. ‘ayes ceylas (Aeschylus) eṉpār ‘'ellā vakai makkaḷum vēṟaṟak kalantuviṭṭa oru peruṅkūṭṭam” (Painminikton hoclon) eṉa aḻaikkumaḷavu, pāpilōṉ nakarattu makkaḷ kalantuviṭṭaṉar. ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai toṭarntu vaḷam peṟṟirunta teṉṉintiya vaṇikar ki.mu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil, nippūril (Nippur) iruntu murāṣa-vukkum, (Murushu), avar makaṉukkum urimaiyuṭaiyatāṉa periya varttaka niṟuvaṉattiṉ vaṇikat toṭarpuṭaiya paṭṭayaṅkaḷil, vaṇikar toṭarpāṉa kuṟippukaḷaik kāṇkiṟōm. (J.R.A.S. 1917, P. 237 Kennedy) ki.mu.538l sairas (Cyrus), pāpilōṉiyap pēraracai aḻittuviṭṭāṉ. avaṉ vaḻivanta matippārnta tāriyas (Dariwas) ki.mu. 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu pōlum kārattil sesōs - ṭiriyes (Sesostrises) eṉṟa oruvaṉāl mutaṉ mutalāka veṭṭappaṭṭu, ki.mu. 15 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil 18vatu araca marapiṉar āṭciyiṉ kīḻ, mīṇṭum tiṟakkappaṭṭa cūyas kālvāyaik ki.mu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil oru paktiyait tiṟappataṉ mūlam, kaṭal, vaṇika vaḷarccikkut tuṇai purintāṉ. avaṉ, vaṭamēṟku intiyāvait taṉ pēraracōṭu iṇaittuk koṇṭāṉ. atu, intukuṣ malaiyiṉ kuṟukkākap, pālk pakutikkum, aṅkiruntu eksaiṉ (Ewxine) pakutikkuc ceṉṟa, pālaivaṉattaik, kūṭṭamākak kaṭakkum vaṇikac cāttumuṟai puttuyir peṟa vaḻivakuttu viṭṭatu. atu, kārmēṉiyaṉ (Karmanian) pālaiyait toṭṭuc celvatāki, aṅkiruntu mesapaṭōmiyā vaḻiyāka aṉṭiyōk (Antioch) nakarukkum ceṉṟatu. itaṉ mukkiya viḷaivu, paṭṭu, mutaṉmutalāka, mēṟku nāṭukaḷaic ceṉṟaṭaintatu. "kirēkkarkaḷ, paṭṭai aleksāṇ ṭariṉ paṭaiyeṭuppu mūlam aṟintu koṇṭaṉar eṉak karutappaṭṭatu. āṉāl atu ataṟku muṉpē, parṣayā mūlam, atu avarkaḷai aṭaintirukkakkūṭum." (Schoff's Periplus. P. 264.) innilavaḻi vāṇikam, tāriyasukkup piṟaku, paṭippaṭiyākak kuṟaintuviṭṭatu. ekiptai veṟṟi koṇṭa piṉṉar, aleksāṇṭar, intiyāvukkum airōppiyāvukkum iṭaiyil, periya vāṇika nilaiyamāka viraivāka vaḷarntu viṭṭa aleksāṇṭiriyāp perunakarai : uruvākkiṉāṉ. piṉṉar, ammāvīraṉ ṭayar nakaraic cūṟaiyāṭi aḻittu ataṉ paṇṭai vāṇikattaiyum pāḻpaṭutti viṭṭāṉ. itu mēṟku nōkkip perukik koṇṭirunta intiya vaṇikap perukkai ōraḷavu aṇaipōṭṭut taṭuttu viṭṭatu. aleksāṇṭar iṟappiṟkup piṟaku, asariyāvaic caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷ aṟṟuppōṉa kuḻappanilai, āṭcipuriyat toṭaṅki viṭṭatu. pārttiyāvil, oru putiya pēraracu eḻuntatu. cittiyap paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉar, pākṭriyā (Bactria) mītāṉa, tam tiṭīrt tākkutalkaḷait toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉar. innikaḻccikaḷ, nilavaḻi vāṇikattil aḻivukku vaḻivakuttu viṭṭaṉa. inta viḷaivukaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭu, ekiptiṉ nalaṉukkākac ceṅkaṭal vāṇika vaḷarccikku, arumuyaṟci eṭuttuk koḷḷumāṟu pilaṭelpisukku (Philadelphus) ki.pi. 285 - 246l, aḻaippu viṭuttār. mutal tālami, cūyas kālvāy, ōraḷavu tiṟakkappaṭṭu, vāṇikappōkku varaviṟku vaḻi ceyyappaṭṭatu. vaṇikac cāttu cellum, taṅkum iṭaṅkaḷukkum, kuṭinīrk kiṇaṟukaḷukkum vacati ceytu tarappaṭṭa, palvēṟu vaḻittaṭaṅkaḷ, kaṭalukkum, nail natikkum iṭaiyē tiṟakkappaṭṭaṉa. avvaḻittaṭaṅkaḷ muṭiyum iṭaṅkaḷilellām, tuṟaikaḷāl kaṇkāṇikkappaṭṭaṉa. taṉ muntaiya celvavaḷattaiyum pukaḻaiyum ekiptu, ōraḷavu tirumpap peṟṟu viṭṭatu. tālamiyiṉ nakarvalattil pileṭelpas nakaril intiya makaḷir, intiya vēṭṭai nāykaḷ, intiyak kākkaikaḷ, oṭṭakaṅkaḷ mītu ēṟṟappaṭṭa, intiya maṇapporuḷkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.

kirēkka iṭaittarakarkaḷum, naṭuvar iruvar kuḻu āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭa rōmum

kiṟittuva āṇṭu piṟappataṟku muntiya 500 āṇṭukaḷil, airōppāvuṭaṉāṉa, inta intiya vaṇikattil, kirēkkarkaḷ, perum iṭaittarakarkaḷāka iruntaṉar. ulakap perunāṭu kaḷukku iṭaiyilāṉa, inta virivāṉa vāṇikap paṇṭaṅ kaḷiṉ tamiḻppeyarkaḷ, kirēkkap paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉariṉ tūya kirēkka elleṉes moḻiyāl (Helenes) kaṭaṉ vāṅkap paṭṭaṉa. tiruvāḷarkaḷ sōphōkkils, (Sophocles ) arisṭōpaṉs (Aristophanes) ākiyōr eḻuttukkaḷil, avai iṭam peṟat toṭaṅkiṉa. avaiyāvaṉa, tamiḻ ariciyiliruntu “orais” (Oryza) ilavaṅkappaṭṭaiyām karuvāviliruntu, ‘'karpiyoṉ'’ (Karpion) tamiḻ iñcivēr, camaskiruta sriṅkiṭuvārā viliruntu (Sringivera) “jikki ‘peros” vālmiḷakaik kuṟikkum tamiḻ tippiliyiliruntu ‘'pepperi;” (Peperi), itu ippōtu, airōppiya moḻikaḷil, karuppu miḷakaik kuṟikkavum ākiviṭṭatu. paṇṭaikkālattil kōvai māvaṭṭattil tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭa tamiḻ vaiṭūriyattiliruntu ‘perillōs’ (Beryllos) inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakka kālattu urōmāṉiyar. avarkaḷuṭaiya, paṇṭaiya eḷiya vāḻkkai muṟaiyaiyē kaṭaippiṭittaṉar. pakaṭṭu vāḻkkaiyil avā koṇṭilar. ākavē, naṭuvar iruvar kuḻu (Consular) āṭciyiṉ toṭakka kālattil, intiyap paṇṭaṅkaḷ, urōmāpuriyai aṭaiyavillai. annāṭkaḷil, intiyā iṟakkumati ceytaṉavaṟṟil urōma nāṇayam iṭam peṟavillaiyākavē, akkālai naṭaipeṟṟa ciṟu vāṇikattilum, vāṅkiya paṇṭaṅkaḷ, urōma nāṭṭup paṇam koṭuttu vāṅkappaṭavilla.. pala nūṟu āṇṭu kālamāka iruntuvanta paḻaiya taṭattilēyē vāṇikam naṭaipeṟṟirukkak kūṭum. naṭaipeṟṟiruntatu. āṉāl, naṭuvar iruvar kuḻu āṭciyiṉ piṟpakutivarai, urōma aracu, kiḻakku nōkki virivaṭaiyavillai. tuṇivumikka urōma vaṇikarkaḷukkup palmairā (Palmyra) taṉṉuṭaiya katavu kaḷait tiṟakkavillai. jūliyas cīcarāl , aleksāṇ ṭiriyā, ki.mu. 47 il, kaikkoḷḷappaṭṭatu eṉṟālum, kaṭal vāṇikam arāpiyappaṭakukaḷil, kaṭaṟkoḷḷaik kārarkaḷiṉ piṭiyil cikkuṇṭirunta kaṭaṟkaraiyai oṭṭi naṭaipeṟṟu vantamaiyāl, akkālai vāṇikam, ciṟiya aḷavil, nilaiyaṟṟa taṉmaiyilēyē naṭaipeṟṟu vantatu. akkālattil, airōppāvukku, intiyāviliruntu, eṉṉeṉṉa ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭālum, avai. perumpālum urōmukkuc celvatillai. kirēkkattiṟkē ceṉṟaṉa. [J. R. A. S. 1904: Page : 593 - 94. Sewell] tiruvāḷar svel avar āṭcikkālattu nāṇaṅkaḷil, ituvarai orucila nāṇayaṅkaḷē intiyāvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟa uṇmai nilaiyai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu eḻuntaṉavām.

ikkāla kaṭṭattiṉ iṟutiyil, mattiya taraikkaṭal makkaḷ mītu, aṭuttaṭuttuk koṇṭa veṟṟiyum, naṭattiya koḷḷaiyum, urōmap pēraraciṉ karuvūlattiṟku oppukkāṭṭa iyalāp peruñcelvattaik koṇṭu vantu cērttaṉa. kīḻ nāṭukaḷaic cērnta, matippumikka vaṇikap paṇṭaṅkaḷ mītāṉa cuvai, orē iravil vaḷarntuviṭṭatu. ciṟṟāciyā, ciriyā nāṭukaḷai veṉṟu koṇṭāraic , ciṟappittu makkaḷ eṭutta viḻākkaḷ, makkaḷ ārpparippuk kuk kāraṇamākiviṭṭa peruñcelvattāl cuṭaroḷi vīcic ciṟappuṟṟa" (Scoffs periplus.page: 5] paḻaiya cikkaṉ, eḷiya vāḻkkaimuṟai, pakaṭṭu vāḻviṉ kavarcci muṉ niṟkamāṭṭātu, neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉpē viṭaipeṟṟuk koṇṭatu. ki.mu. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil, tūrakkiḻakku vaṇikap paṇṭaṅkaḷ, kaṇicamāṉa aḷavu, urōmai vantaṭaiyalāyiṉ”, [Warminston J. R.A.S., 1940. page 4 sweli.]

urōmap pēraraciṉ toṭakkattil

akasṭas (Augustus) ekiptai, ki.mu., 30il veṟṟi koṇṭu, intiyāvukkum, urōmap pēraracukkum iṭaiyil, oru nēriṭaik kaṭal vāṇika vaḷarccikku muyaṉṟāṉ. ki.mu. 25il, ēṟattāḻa 120 kappalkaḷ, hōrmassiliruntu (Hormus), intiyāvukkup payaṇam ceytatait tām pārttatākat, tiru. sṭirāpō (Stabo) kūṟukiṟār. [Morindle Ancient India, page 6] “intiyāviṉ palvēṟu nāṭukaḷiliruntu araciyal tūtuvarkaḷ akasṭaspāl ceṉṟaṉar. intiyat tūtuvarkaḷ, aṭikkaṭi vantatāka avaṉē kūṟukiṟāṉ” [Warmington Commerce between the Roman Empire and India. page : 35] akkālac cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ, taṉittaṉit tūtuvarkaḷai aṉuppiyatāka, tiruvāḷar vārmiṅṭaṉ kūṟukiṟār. (Warmington Commerce between the Roman Empire and India. p. 37] itu. akasṭa s kālattil intiyā, urōmuṭaṉ naṭattiya vāṇika aḷavu mikapperiya aḷaviṉatāyp peruka vaḻi ceytatu. ivvāṇikam kuṟittut tiruvāḷar vārmiṅṭaṉ avarkaḷ, “urōmukkum intiyāvukku miṭaiyilāṉa vāṇika aḷavai matippiṭṭup pārttāl, akasṭasiṉ uṇmaiyāṉa toṭakkanilai āṭcik kālattiṉ pōtē, atu intiyāvukkum iṅkilāntukkum iṭaiyilāṉa vāṇikam eppōtum iyalpāka irukkum, anta aḷavu aṭaintuviṭṭa, kuṟippiṭattakka apperunilaiyai nām kaṇṇetirākak kāṇakkūṭum. toṭakka nāḷiliruntē, intiyap paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ varukaip perukkattiṟkup pēraraciṉ paṇṭaṅkaḷaik koṭuttuc cariīṭu ceyya muṭiyātu pōyiṟṟu. ataṉ - viḷaivu paṇṭaṅkaḷukkup patilāka, urōmāṉiyar, ceṉṟāl tirumpivarāta, aṭitta nāṇayap paṇaṅkaḷai veḷināṭṭu vāṇikam ki.mu. 600 mutal ki.pi. 14 varai 179 veḷi yēṟṟiṉar. [Warmington. Page : 381 nāṇayap paṇaṅkaḷai ēṟṟumati ceyvatil, toṭakkattil oru mōcaṭi veḷippaṭaiyākavē ceyya muyalappaṭṭatu. akasṭas nāṇayattiṟkup patilāka, avaṉuṭaiya tattup piḷḷaikaḷām, kaiyas (Gaius) lūciyas (Lucius) eṉpār uruvaṅkaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭa poṉṉukkup patil muṟṟilum poṉ mulām pūcappaṭṭa nāṇayaṅkaḷ perum eṇṇikkaiyil intiyāvil kaimmāṟiṉa. uḷnāṭṭu makkaḷ, pōli nāṇayaṅkaḷiliruntu, uṇmaiyāṉa urōma nāṇayaṅkaḷai, iṉṉamum vēṟupirittu uṇaramāṭṭā, teṉ intiyāvōṭu naṭattum vāṇikattiṟkākavē attakaiya nāṇayaṅkaḷai , iṉṉamum vēṟupirittu uṇaramāṭṭā, teṉ intiyāvōṭu naṭattum vāṇikat tiṟkākavē attakaiya nāṇayaṅkaḷ taṉiyāka aṭikkappaṭṭa ṉavāka erṉasṭ (Ernst) eṇṇukiṟār" eṉak kūṟukiṟār tiruvāḷar vārmiṅṭaṉ [Warmington p. 139]. āṉāl, tamiḻarkaḷ kūrmativāyntavar eṉpatai uṟuti ceytu viṭṭaṉar. kāraṇam, aṟpattaṉamāṉa accōtaṉai mīṇṭum muyalappaṭavillai. innūṟṟāṇṭu kāla aḷavil, mēlai nāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭa vaṇikap poruḷkaḷ yāvai?

uyirvilaṅkukaḷiṉ ēṟṟumati

paṇṭaikkālattu intiyarkaḷ, uyir vilaṅkukaḷaik kaṭalvaḻiyākap, parṣiya vaḷaikuṭāviṟkum, āppirikkā viṟkum, cīṉāvukkum aṉuppi vaittaṉar. iraṇṭām tālamiyiṉ mayilkaḷaiyum, kiḷikaḷaiyum, aṉuppi vaittamaikkum avarkaḷē, perumpālum poṟuppāvar. ētaṉskkuc celyūkas (Seleucus) naṉkoṭaiyāka vaḻaṅkiya puli, cullāvum (Sulla) pāmpeyum (Pampey) kāṭcip poruḷākak kāṭṭiya ciṅkaṅkaḷ, oṟṟaik kompuk kāṇṭāmirukam, mersalas (Mercllows) nakariṉ āṭaraṅku arppaṇikkappaṭṭapōtu, kūṇṭilō kukaiyilō aṭaittuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa puli, ākiya vilaṅkukaḷ, cila vaṭa intiyāvaiyum cila teṉṉintiyā vaiyum, cērntavai, nilavaḻiyāka aṉuppappaṭṭaṉavām. āṉāl, kirēkka eḻuttāḷarkaḷ palarum, taṅkaḷ nūlkaḷil kuṟippiṭṭirukkum kuraṅkukaḷ, kaṭalvaḻiyākavē ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭum. (Warmington 147, 148, 151) aleksāṇṭa r veṟṟikkup piṉṉar, caṇṭaikaḷil perumaḷavil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa intiya yāṉai, payaras (Pyrrhus) eṉpāṉ, cila yāṉaikaḷaik ki.mu. 28l, airōppāviliruntu ittālikku iṭamāṟṟi aṉuppappaṭṭapōtu urōmāṉiyarkaḷukku mutaṉmutalāka aṟimukamākkappaṭṭatu. kārtta kiṉiyarkaḷ (Carthaginiars) avaṟṟai , āppirikka yāṉaikaḷōṭu payaṉ koṇṭu avviru iṉaṅkaḷaiyum paḻakka, intiya yāṉaip pākarkaḷai niyamittaṉarā eṉpatai, eṉṉāl uṟutiyākac colla iyalātu. āṉāl, ki.mu. 25l paṉōrmōs (Panormos) eṉum iṭattil hāsṭrupāl (Hastrubai) intiyarkaḷāl celuttappaṭṭa yāṉaikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāṉ eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. urōmap pēraracōṭu naṭattiya iraṇṭām puṉik (Punic) pōril, haṉipāl, hāsṭrupāl (Hannibal, Hasdrubal) iruvarum atuvē ceytaṉar. rapiyā (Rapia) eṉumiṭattil naṭaipeṟṟa pōril tālamiyiṉ lipiyā nāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷ, āṇṭiyō cos eṉpāṉiṉ intiyap paṭaikaḷ muṉ niṟka muṭiyavillai." (Warmington, Page 151)

intiya vēṭṭai nāykaḷ, veḷināṭṭavarāl, mikuvilaikkuriyavāka matikkappaṭṭaṉa. “horō ṭōṭas kūṟṟuppaṭi, tam kālattaic cērnta parṣiyar, pāpilōṉiyāvaic cuṟṟiyirunta nāṉku periya ciṟṟūrkaḷiṉ varuvāykaḷai, intiya vēṭṭai nāykaḷiṉ uṇaviṟku eṉṟē nitiotukkam ceytuvaittaṉar. parṣiyāvaic cērnta intiya vēṭṭai nāykaḷum, avaipōlum vēṟunāykaḷum, tālami, pilaṭelpās veṟṟi ūrvalattil kāṇappaṭṭatāka, tiru. sṭeciyās (Ctesias) kuṟippiṭukiṟār. koṭiya karaṭiyōṭu naṭattiya pōrāṭṭattil, taṉ talaivaṉaik kāppataṟkākat taṉ uyiraik koṭutta 'ṭavrāṉ' (Tawron) eṉṟa vēṭṭaināy kuṟittup pāṭiya iraṇṭu kaiyaṟu nilaic ceyyuḷkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭa ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭaic cērnta, nāṇaṟpul tāḷiṉālāṉa kaiyeḻuttuppaṭi oṉṟum kiṭaittuḷḷatu". (Warmington. Page : 149) teṉ intiya vēṭṭai nāykaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ muraṭṭuttaṉattiṟkākavē naṉku terintiruntaṉa. potuvāka avai, "katanāy" (kaṭuñciṉa nāy, viraintōṭum nāy) eṉṟē aḻaikkappaṭum. avai, caṅkiliyāl kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. avvāṟu nāy kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum vīṭṭiṉuḷ evarum eḷitil puka iyalātu. kāvalil attuṇaik kaṭumaiyāṉatu nāy, (toṭar paṭu ñamali" puṟam : 743) toṭar nāyātta tuṉṉarum kaṭinakar (perumpāṇāṟ ṟuppaṭai -125) tām kuṟittuc ceṉṟa pakaivarkaḷait tappātu veṟṟi koḷḷavalla vīrarkaḷukkut tāṉ kuṟitta vilaṅkukaḷait tappātu koḷḷum vēṭṭai nāyai uvamai kūṟumaḷavu teṉṉāṭṭu vēṭṭai nāykaḷ : ciṟantaṉavāka matikkappaṭṭaṉa. cel nā aṉṉa kaṭuviṟal cuṟṟamoṭu kēḷāmaṉṉā kaṭavulam pukku. (perumpāṇ - 139-140) karaṭi vēṭṭaiyil vēṭṭai nāykaḷ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭutalai, "uyarnta nākamaraṅkaḷ aṭarntu, kaṭantu celvataṟku aritāki viṭṭa kavartta kāṭṭu vaḻiyil, ciṟiya kaṇṇum periya ciṉṉamum uṭaiya āṇ paṉṟi, cēṟṟil vīḻntu puraṇṭataṉāl cēṟu pūcap peṟṟa mutukil puḻutiyum paṭintirukka akkōlattōṭu viraintu ōṭi, curukkavalai viritta piḷappil vīḻntu paṭṭatāka, atu kaṇṭa aḷavē, vēṭṭai nāykaḷ, kūṭṭamāka viraintu, valaiyai aḻittu, paṉṟi mītu pāyntu koṉṟu koṇṭa iṟaicci pōka, eñciya iṟaicciyaik kāṉavar koṇṭu celvar" eṉṟa naṟṟiṇai, viḷakkamākak kūṟiyuḷatu:

"pōkiya nākap pōkkarum kavalaic

ciṟukaṇ paṉṟip peruñciṉa oṟuttal

cēṟāṭu irumpuṟam nīṟoṭu civaṇa,

veḷpacip paṭīiyar, moytta vaṉpu aḻīik

kōṇāy koṇṭa koḷḷaik





kāṉavar peyarkkum ciṟukuṭi.

- naṟṟiṇai : 82 : 6-11)



vēṭṭuvar, kāṭṭil vāḻpavarākavum, viraintōṭum māṉ kūṭṭattaiyum, turattit tolaikka - valla viraiviṉaiyuṭaiya kaṭuñciṉa nāykaḷai uṭaiyavarākavum kūṟappaṭṭu uḷḷaṉar. "kāṉuṟai vāḻkkaik kata nāy vēṭṭuvaṉ'’ (puṟanāṉūṟu : 33 :1) "māṉkaṇam tolaicciya kaṭuvicaik kata nāy. vēṭṭuvaṉ". (puṟam : 205 : 8-9) vēṭṭaikkuc ceṉṟa talaivaṉ, vēṭṭai muṭivuṟṟatum, vēṭṭai mēṟkoṇṭu mūṅkiṟkāṭṭiṉuḷ nuḻaintu paravik kiṭakkum taṉ vēṭṭuvat tuṇaivarkaḷaiyum - vēṭṭai nāykaḷaiyum, vēṭṭai muṭivuṟṟatu, vāruṅkaḷ eṉa aḻaikkum kuṟippōṭu kompu eṉum ūtu karuviyai ūti oli eḻuppuvaṉ:

muntaiya nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil toṭaṅkappaṭṭuviṭṭa kiḷikaḷ, mayilkaḷiṉ ēṟṟumati inta nūṟṟāṇṭilum toṭarntatu. atil aiyamillai, kirēkkarkaḷāl ‘aspis’ (Aspis) eṉa aḻaikkappaṭum nākappāmpukaḷ, malaippāmpu kaḷ, uḷḷiṭṭa pāmpukaḷ, ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭa piṟa uyiriṉaṅkaḷām intiyāviliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa oṉpatu aṭi nīḷam pāmpu oṉṟai sṭrāpō ekiptil pārttuḷḷār. (Warmington. Page : 157)

vilaṅkutaru poruḷkaḷ

intiyāviliruntu ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭa mikamika mukkiyamāṉa poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟu tantam. kirēkkarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ nākarīkattiṉ mutirnta nilaiyil, uruvaccilaikaḷil, āṭaiyāl maṟaikkappaṭāta puṟattōṟṟap pakutikaḷukku, ataip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. muttukkaḷ, mutaṉmutalil, jūkurtaṉ (Jugurthine) pōriṉpōtu, urōmukku aṟimukam ceyyappaṭṭu, pāmpē (Pompey) eṉpavarāl, ittāliyiliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa peruṅkuviyalāl, makkaḷ naṉku aṟinta oru poruḷāka ākkappaṭṭatu. "tālamikaḷiṉ karuvūlaṅkaḷ, akasṭasāl, tirumpak koṇṭuvarappaṭṭapōtu, appēraraciṉ vīḻcciyil, avai, ellōrkkum kiṭaikkum eḷiya poruḷāyiṉa. muttukkaḷ kiṭaippatu aritākiviṭṭa cicirōviṉ (Cicero) - kālattil, iṉṟaiya nāṇayam eṭṭāyiram pavuṉ matippuḷḷa oru muttu hesōpṉaṉ (AesopwS) makaṉāl, metellā (Metelia) kātiliruntu kaḻaṟṟappaṭṭu, vaṟaṭcik kālattil oru perum paṇattokaiyai viḻuṅkiṉōm eṉṟa maṉaniṟaivu koḷvāṉ vēṇṭi, vēṇṭumeṉṟē tiṭṭamiṭṭu viḻuṅkappaṭṭatu. (Warmington - Page : 163 168169) kiḷiyōpāṭrā, taṉ matuvil, parukuvataṟku muṉṉar, muttukkaḷaik karaippāḷ eṉpatu naṉku terinta oṉṟu. arakku vaṇṇam ūṭṭappeṟṟa paruttikaḷ parṣiyāvukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. taṉṉuṭaiya kālattil pāracīka aracaṉukku, intiyarkaḷāl cevvaṇṭiliruntu peṟṟa oru vaṇṇak kalavaiyāl vaṇṇam ūṭṭappaṭṭa parutti nūl aṉuppappaṭṭatāka, stēciyās (Ctesias) kuṟittu vaittuḷḷār (Warmington Page : 178).

ekiptiya āvaṇaṅkaḷ, paṭṭaik kuṟippiṭavillai. ākavē, paṭṭu, tāriyas (Dariws), jerses (Xeroxes)kaḷiṉ pēraraciṉ mūlamākavē, mattiya taraikkaṭal pakutiyai aṭaintatu. ataṉ uṟpatti paṟṟiya cariyāṉa viḷakkattai arisṭāṭṭil koṭuttuḷḷār. paṭṭu tārkaḷil cuṟṟappaṭṭu, arisṭāṭṭil kālattiṟku muṉpē iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭatu. ṭayarilum (Tyre) maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum, uṭaikaḷākat taikkappaṭṭa nilaiyil piḷaiṉiyiṉ kūṟṟuppaṭi, "oru peṇṇiṉ uṭalai mūṭum atē nilaiyil, avaḷuṭaiya iyaṟkai aḻakai veḷippaṭuttavallatāṉa , uḷḷiruppatai veḷikkāṭṭum melliya valai nikar āṭaikaḷ neyyappaṭṭaṉa. (Schoff Perplus, Page: 264-265) kirēkka urōmāṉiya eḻuttāḷarkaḷiṭaiyē, paruttikkum paṭṭukkumiṭaiyē ciṟitu karuttukkuḻappam iruntatu. avarkaḷ, avviraṇṭaiyumē, "maram taru vilaṅkumayir", Treewool) eṉa aḻaittuḷḷaṉar. maraviṉam tarum poruḷkaḷ

intiya āṭaikaḷ, pālastīṉiyarāl, perumaḷavil iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭataṟkiṭaiyil, intiyap pañcu, aracu nūṟpu ālaikaḷaiyum, cāyat toḻiṟcālaikaḷaiyum, pēraracukaḷ, niṟuviyirunta oru cila matakurukkaḷālum ceyalpaṭṭuvanta, ekiptiṟku eṭuttuc cellappaṭṭatu. ekiptiyar, paruttiyaiyum, nār iḻaikaḷaiyum oṉṟu kalantu parutti ūṭiḻaiyākavum, nār, pāvu iḻaiyākavum, koṇṭa āṭaikaḷai neytaṉar. intiyap pañcu, ekiptil, palvēṟu puṉitap paṇikaḷukkum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. palvaṇṇam ūṭṭappaṭṭa parutti nūlkaḷ paṉōpilisil uḷḷa (Panoplis) mempis (Memphis) arukē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. annūlkaḷil orucila, avaṟṟiṉ vaṭivamaippil, intiyak kūṟukaḷaik koṇṭuḷḷaṉa."(Warmington Page 212)

ilavaṅkam, ekiptiṟkāka eṉṟē, ciṟappāka otukkappaṭṭatu pōlavē, miḷaku, pāpilōṉiyā, pāracīka vaḷaikuṭā , vāṇikaṅkaḷil, vaḻakkattiṟku mēlāṉa tēvaippaṭuporuḷāka iruntatu. ataṉuṭaiya viṟuviṟuppāṉa peruntēvai, ‘tāriyas'’ (Darius) āṭciyiṉ kīḻ, pāracīkap pēraracu virivaṭaintapōtu ēṟpaṭṭatu eṉak karutuvataṟkuk kāraṇam uḷatu. inta vāṇikam, teṉ intiyak kappalkaḷil, kaṭal vaḻiyāka naṭaipeṟṟatu. nila vaḻiyāka aṉṟu, etōppiyariṉ (Aethiopians) mēṉi niṟattaip peritum otta niṟam vāyntavar. teṟkil, pāracīkattiliruntu veku tolaivukku appāl kuṭivāḻpavar, tāriyasākku eṉṟum paṇiyātavar eṉṟa aḷavilēyē herōṭaṭṭas, (Herodotas) tirāviṭarkaḷaip paṟṟi aṟintuḷḷār, (Scoff’s Periplus . Page 213) pārttiyā (Parthia) viṉ kuṟukkāka naṭaipeṟṟa nila vāṇikamum, tālamikaḷāl ūkkam ūṭṭappaṭṭa kaṭal vāṇikamum, aṟutiyiṭṭuk kūṟamāṭṭā. āṇṭāṇṭu kālamāka ēṟkaṉavē, naṭaipeṟṟuvanta ilavaṅkam, maṟṟum maṇapporuḷkaḷiṉ vāṇikattiṉ, perumaḷavilāṉa | perukkiṟku vaḻi ceytu viṭṭaṉa. urōmukkut tēvaippaṭum miḷakai, ikkāla kaṭṭattil, poyiṉīṣiyarkaḷum, kārttikiṉiyarkaḷum vaḻaṅkivantaṉar. ki.mu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭaic cērntavarum, intiya maruttiyal - muṟaikaḷaik kaiyāṇṭavarumāṉa 'ippō kirāṭs' eṉpār, miḷakai, "intiya nivāraṇi" eṉa aḻaikkiṟār. (Warmington. Page 182) atu, kuḷir kāycca lukkum, veppakkāyccalukkum, payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. makkaḷ, marapuvaḻip paḻakka vaḻakkam kuṟitta ārāycciyil, 'herōṭōṭas' (Herodotus) avar kaḷukku, itu paṟṟik kuṟippiṭuvataṟku etuvum illai eṉṟālum, ki.mu. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭil, 'tiyōp rasṭas' (Theophrastus) atai oru maruntāka aṟintuḷḷār. ṭiyōs kōriṭas (Dioscorides) karuppu, veḷḷai, vālmiḷakukaḷukkiṭaiyilāṉa vēṟupāṭṭai uṇarntuḷḷār. (Scoff's Periplus. Page 213) iñciyum mēlai nāṭukaḷai aṭaintu maruntākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, atu ōr intiya uṟpattip poruḷ eṉpatai, mēlaināṭṭavar uṇarntu koḷḷā vakaiyil, veṟṟikaramāka maṟaittuviṭṭa arāpiya iṭaittarakarkaḷāl koṇṭu cellappaṭṭatu. (Warmington Page: 184) ki.mu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil, ēṉ, ataṟku muṉpēyum eḷḷeṇṇey, kirēkkarkaḷukku naṉku terintiruntatu. atu intiyāviliruntu aṉuppappaṭṭatu. tālamikaḷ kālattil, atu matikkattakka mukkiyattuvam uṭaiyatākiviṭṭatu. (Warmington Page : 206) kirēkkak kōyilkaḷil tēṅkāykaḷ, aticayak kāṭcip poruḷāka vaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa vātaliṉ, kirēkkattiṟkut tēṅkāykaḷum aṉuppappaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. (Warmington Page : 217) sēpōkkels (Sophocells). arici aṭaikaḷ paṟṟip pēcukiṟār. ākavē, kirēkkar, ariciyaiyum, ataṉ peyaraiyum tamiḻnāṭṭiliruntu peṟṟaṉar. palvēṟu payaṉ karuti maraṅkaḷum ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. mitriṭēṭs mītu (Mithridates) koṇṭa veṟṟi kuṟittu eṭutta viḻāvil intiyak karuṅkāli marattaik kāṭcip poruḷākak kāṭṭiṉāṉ pāmpē (Pompey). intiyak karuṅkāli maram vāṇikam neṭitu nilai peṟṟirunta oṉṟu. ituvum, maṟṟa maraṅkaḷum pāracīka vaḷaikuṭāp pakutikku, intiya vaṇikarkaḷāl, koṇṭu cellappaṭṭaṉa. karuṅkāli, nāṟkāli, mēcai pōlum taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷ kaḷukkākavum uruvaccilaikaḷukkākavum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭatu. intiyāviliruntu, kuṟippāka malapār, karṉāṭakam, tiruvāṅkūr maṟṟum vēṟu cila iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu vanta tēkku maraṅkaḷaik koṇṭu, pāracīka vaḷaikuṭāvil āṭkaḷ kappalkaḷ kaṭṭuvatai tiyōprasṭas kāṭṭu kiṟār. (Warmington Page 213-214) “maṭaraṭā'’ (Madarata) eṉa. aṟiyappaṭṭa, taitta paṭakukaḷ eṉa aḻaikkappaṭṭa, arāpiyariṉ paṭakukaḷ, teṉṉai nāriṉāl, atāvatu tēṅkāy maṭṭaikaḷiliruntu eṭutta nāriṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. (Scor’s Periplus Page : 154) ikkāla kaṭṭattil palvēṟu vakaiyaṉavāṉa intiyak kaṟkaḷ, mēlai nāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟālum inta vāṇikam; aṭutta kāla kaṭṭattilēyē periya aḷavaip peṟṟatu.

armiṉiyāvil ōr intiya vaḻipāṭṭu muṟai

mēṟku āciyāvuṭaṉāṉa inta vāṇikattiṉ oru viyattaku viḷaivu iṅkē viḷakkappaṭum. atu, armīṉiyāvil, ōr intiya vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaiyiṉ nuḻaivākum. armīṉiyāvaic cērnta ‘arasciṭē” (Arsacide) marapiṉ mutal maṉṉaṉākiya, mutalām vaḷarṣak (Valarshak) (ki.mu149. 127) kālattil, iru intiyat talaivarkaḷ, iyūpraṭs āṟṟiṉ mēṟkuk karaiyil, vāṉ (Van) eṉṟa ērikkut teṟkil, oru putiya kuṭiyiruppai niṟuvi, kiruṣṇaṉ palatēvaṉ (armīṉiya moḻiyil muṟaiyē kicaṉi (Gisani), temeter (Demeter) vaḻipāṭṭiṟkākak kōyilkaḷaik kaṭṭiṉar. itu vaṭa intiyāviliruntu vanta, āriya vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaiyiṉ perukkākumā? mika uṟutiyāka illai . kiruṣṇa palatēvarkaḷ, tirumāliṉ avatāraṅ kaḷām eṉap purāṇaṅkaḷ kūṟiṉālum, ivviru kaṭavuḷkaḷaiyum orucēra vaḻipaṭum taṉivaḻipāṭṭumuṟai, vaṭa intiyāvil ekkālattilum iruntatillai. āṉāl, paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, itaṟkāṉa eṇṇaṟṟa akaccāṉṟukaḷai aḷikkiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷil, muṉṉavaṉ māyōṉ eṉṟa peyarāl, mullai nilakkaṭavuḷāvaṉ kalappaiyaip paṭaikkalaṉākavum, paṉaiyaik koṭiyākavum, koṇṭa, veṇṇiṟak kaṭavuḷ eṉum poruḷil vāliyōṉ allatu veḷḷaiyōṉ eṉappaṭum piṉṉavaṉ toṭakkattil uḻavuttoḻil mutalil toṭaṅkappaṭṭa mullaikkum, marutattukkum iṭaippaṭṭa nilattiṉ kaṭavuḷāvaṉ. muṉṉavaṉ karuppu vaṇṇamum, piṉṉavaṉ, veḷḷai vaṇṇamum, tamiḻp puvalarkaḷiṉ viruppattiṟkuriya aṇi nalaṅkaḷām. kaṭalil vaḷarum valampuric caṅkai okkum niṟam vāynta, kolai virumpum kalappaiyaiyum paṉaikkoṭiyaiyum uṭaiyavaṉ" eṉṟum. "mācu pōkak kaḻuvappaṭṭa aḻakiya nīlamaṇi pōlum niṟam vāynta, vāṉuṟa uyarnta karuṭakkoṭi uṭaiya, eṉṟum veṟṟiyē virumpuvōṉ" eṉṟum, avarkaḷ muṟaiyē pārāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉar.

"kaṭalvaḷar purivaṉai puraiyum mēṉi,

aṭal vem nāñcil paṉaikkoṭi yōṉum,

maṇṇuṟu tirumaṇi puraiyum mēṉi,

viṇuyar puṭkoṭi viṟal vey yōṉum."

puṟanāṉūṟu : 56:3-6





"pāl pōlum veṇṇiṟam vāyntavaṉ. paṉaik koṭiyuṭaiyāṉ" eṉṟum, "nīlamaṇiyiṉ niṟam vāynta tirumēṉiyuṭaiyāṉ; āriyap paṭaiyuṭaiyāṉ" eṉṟum avarkaḷ mēlum pārāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉar.

"pāl niṟa uruviṉ paṉaikkoṭi yōṉum,

nīlniṟa uruviṉ nēmi yōṉam"

puṟanāṉūṟu : 58 : 14 - 15





ivviruvarkaḷiṉ mēṉi niṟaṅkaḷukkiṭaiyilāṉa māṟupāṭu, uvamai aṇikaḷākap paravalāka āḷap paṭṭuḷatu. eṭuttukkāṭṭukku : ‘'māyōṉ aṉṉa māl varaik kavāaṉ : vāliyōṉ aṉṉa vayaṅku veḷḷaruvi, (naṟṟiṇai : 32:7-2) irāmāvatāram tavirttu , tirumāliṉ avatāraṅkaḷil, vēṟu etuvum paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷāl kuṟippiṭappaṭavillaiyātaliṉ, ivviruvarum, teṉ intiyāvil, tirumāliṉ avatāraṅkaḷāka aṟimukam āṉavarallar). palatēvaṉ, eppōtum paṉañcāṟu paruki veṟikoṇṭu kiṭappaṉ. paṉaimalinta pakutiyil, avaṉ vaḷarccikku atu poruntum. paṉai, kaṅkai veḷiyil vaḷarvatillaiyākavē, palatēva vaḻipāṭu kaṅkaik karaiyil uruvākiyiruttal iyalātu. kaṭalukku veku tolaivil allāta, teṉṉāṭṭu āṟṟup paḷḷattākkup pakutiyilēyē avvaḻipāṭṭumuṟai tōṉṟiyirukka vēṇṭum.

palatēvaṉukku eṉak kaṭṭappaṭṭa kōyilkaḷ vaṭanāṭṭil iruppatāka nām kēḷvippaṭṭatu illai. āṉāl, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattilum, maturaiyilum avaṉukkāṉa kōyilkaḷ, kiruṣṇaṉ kōyilai aṭuttaṭuttuk kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

"vālvaḷai mēṉi vāliyōṉ kōyilum

nīlamēṉi neṭiyōṉ kōyilum”

- cilappatikāram : 5 : 71 - 72





pukar veḷḷai nākartam kōṭṭam”

cilappatikāram : 9:10.





“pala tēvaṉ tirumāliṉ paṭukkaiyām araviṉ, maṟu avatāram eṉpa. avvaravu nākarkaḷait tammōṭu iraṇṭaṟak koṇṭu nākarākiviṭavē, atuvum tirumāl vaḻipāṭṭu neṟiyil iṭam peṟṟuviṭṭatu. ivai pukār nakarattuk kōyilkaḷ, maturaiyil "mēḻi valaṉ uyartta veḷḷai nakaramum” (cilappatikāram : 14 : 9) ivveṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷ ellām, piṟkāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntatu eṉṟālum, akkāla kaṭṭattilum, vaṭanāṭṭil palatēvaṉukkuk kōyil illai. ivveḷḷaik kaṭavuḷ, teṟkiltāṉ mutaṉmutalil eḻuntāṉ eṉṟa muṭiviṟku valivūṭṭukiṟatu. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ cila cātiyariṭaiyē 'veḷḷaiyaṉ", veḷḷāṉ" eṉṟa peyarkaḷ, iṉṟum paravalāka iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. veḷḷaik kaṭavuḷ vaḻipaṭappeṟṟu, avaṉ peyar, avaṉai vaḻipaṭuvār makkaḷukku iṭappaṭṭa kālam toṭṭu vaḻakkil vantirukka vēṇṭum.) ikkōyilkaḷē allāmal kiruṣṇaṉ, palatēvaṉ ākiya iruvariṉ teyvat tirumēṉikaḷ aṭuttaṭuttu iṭam peṟṟirukkum kōyilkaḷum uḷa. attakaiya kōyilkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya, maturaikku veku tolaivil illāta tirumāliruñ cōlaikkōyil, paripāṭalil, patiṉaintāvatu pāṭṭil mika virivāka viḷakkappaṭṭuḷatu. atu, eṭuttukkāṭṭuvataṟku iyalā, neṭum pāṭṭu, mēlum nām ārāyntu koṇṭirukkum kālattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa kālattaic cērntatu. appāṭṭil viḷakkappaṭṭirukkum palatēvaṉ teyvattirumēṉi, ippōtu kāṇāmal pōyviṭṭatu. tirumāl vaḻipāṭṭāḷariṉ matippīṭṭil, palatēvaṉ, kuṟainta nilaikkut taḷḷappaṭṭuviṭṭa kālattil, akkōyil iṭittup putitākak kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkak kūṭum. iṉṟum aḻiyāmal irukkum orucila kōyilkaḷil, palatēvaṉ tirumēṉi, iṉṟum niṟkiṟatu. avai, piṟkālac camaya veṟiyarkaḷiṉ aḻiviliruntu tappiviṭṭaṉa pōlum.

anta iṇainta vaḻipāṭṭu muṟai, armīṉiyāvukku vaṭanāṭṭiliruntu ceṉṟatā, teṉṉāṭṭiliruntu ceṉṟatā eṉpatu viṭaiyiṟukka muṭiyāta nilai irukkumpōtu, atu koṭiya vaṉmuṟaic ceyal oṉṟu mūlam aḻivuṟṟup pōyviṭṭatu. kiṟittuvac camayap piracārakar, muṉivar kirikōri eṉpavar (St. Gregory) ki. pi. nāṉkāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil, puṟaccamayattiṟku etirāṉa tammuṭaiya koṭiya veṟuppu kāraṇamāka, viṣap (Vishap) eṉum iṭattilirunta intiyar kuṭiyiruppiṟku etirākak kiṟittuvap peruṅkūṭṭam oṉṟiṟkut talaimai tāṅkic ceṉṟār. ataṉ viḷaivāka nikaḻnta caṇṭaiyil, talaimai arccakarkaḷ veṭṭit taḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. teyvat tirumēṉikaḷ uṭaittu noṟukkappaṭṭaṉa. kōyilkaḷ - iṭittut taraimaṭṭamākkappaṭṭaṉa. avai niṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil mātā kōyilkaḷ, kaṭṭappaṭṭu, ciluvaikaḷ niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. āṅkuk kuṭivāḻntavarkaḷil, aiyāyirattukkum mēṟpaṭṭavar kiṟistavarāka matam māṟiviṭṭaṉar. tam camayak kōṭpāṭṭaik kaiviṭāmal viṭāppiṭiyāka irunta arccakarkaḷiṉ makkaḷ nāṉūṟṟu muppatteṭṭup pērum, kōyil paṇiyāḷarkaḷum, moṭṭai aṭikkap paṭṭu, tolaināṭukaḷukkut turattappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉar. (J. R. A. S. 1904; Page : 309 - 314. Kennedy)

tūrakkiḻakku nāṭukaḷ uṭaṉāṉa teṉṉintiyak kaṭal vāṇikam, iṉṟum toṭarntu naṭaipeṟṟuvarum makkaḷ kuṭi peyarcciyiṉ toṭakkattiṟku vaḻivakuttatu. paṭaittuṇaittalaivar, tiruvāḷar keriṉi (Gerine) avarkaḷ, kiṟittuva āṇṭu toṭakkattiṟkup pal, nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē, vaṭa intiyāviliruntum, teṉ intiyāviliruntum, vaṇikarkaḷum putitu kāṇum uḷḷaārvam mikkavarkaḷumāṉa ipperum veḷḷam, intō cīṉāvuḷ pāyat toṭaṅki, nilavaḻiyāka, varmā mūlam, attīvakaṟpattiṉ vaṭapakutiyaiyum, kaṭalvaḻiyāka, ataṉ teṉ kaṭaṟpakutiyaiyum aṭaintu, āṅkē, taṅkaḷ kuṭiyiruppukaḷaiyum, vaṇikanilaiyaṅkaḷaiyum niṟuviṉa” eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. [J. R. A. S. 1904. p. 234 - 37) paḻaiya intiyarkaḷiṉ innaṭamāṭṭam kuṟitta virivāṉa viḷakkattiṟku, mēlē kūṟiyataik kāṇka.) pirāmaṇarkaḷum pauttarkaḷumākiya āriyar, paṇṭaiya intiya nākarīkattaiyum, tamiḻar, vāṇikattaiyum iṅkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. cuvarṇa pūmiyiṉ ellaikku appāl, paṇṭaikkālattil periya vaṇika maiyamāka viḷaṅkiya mārtāṉ (Martaban) eṉum iṭattiṟku aṇittākat, "takkōlam' eṉṟa iṭattai , milintā paṉha (Milindapanha) kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. avviṭattiṉ peyar, ceṉṉaikku aṇittāka, ki.pi. pattām nūṟṟāṇṭil oru periya pōr , naṭaipeṟṟa takkōlam eṉṟa ūriṉ peyarai niṉaivūṭṭuvatāka uḷatu.

cīṉāviṉ āṉ marapup pēraracar (Han Emperor) huti (Woti) eṉpāṉ, iṉṟaiya turukkarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷākiya hiyuṅ-nu (Hiung - Nu) eṉpāraik kōpi (Gobi) pālaivaṉattiṟku appāl turattiviṭṭāṉ. ataṉpiṉṉar, airōppāvuṭaṉāṉa cīṉap paṭṭuvāṇikam, periya aḷaviṉai eṭṭiviṭṭatu. ivvāṇikam mēṟkoṇṭa vaḻikaḷil oṉṟu, cūyas vaḷaikuṭāviliruntu vanta yavaṉavaṇikarkaḷ, cīṉappaṭṭait tamiḻ iṭaittarakarkaḷiṭamiruntu peṟṟukkoḷḷum, tamiḻnāṭṭuk kaṭaṟkaraiyai oṭṭiya vaḻiyām. parmā, malēyā, cīṉak kaṭaṟkarai nāṭukaḷuṭaṉāṉa kaṭal vāṇikam, ikkāla kaṭṭattil vaḷarntu perukiṟṟu. "kiḻakkiṟku aṉuppappaṭṭa mika mukkiyamāṉa paṇṭam, miḷaku. miḷaku kuṟitta toṭarnta tēvai, urōmil eḻuvataṟku muṉpē, cīṉāvil iṭaṅkoṇṭiruntatu eṉpatu ūkikkak kūṭiyatē. malapār kaṭaṟkaraikkuk, ki.mu. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil, ēṉ, ataṟkum muṉpāka, 'jaṅ' (Jwnk) eṉappaṭum taṭṭaiyāṉa aṭippākam uṭaiya cīṉak kappalkaḷ ceṉṟuvantataṟku ituvum oru kāraṇam. (Scoff's Periplus. Page 213-14) tūrakkiḻakkiṟku, miḷaku maṭṭumallāmal naṟumaṇap pukaitaru poruḷkaḷum aṉuppappaṭṭu, paṭṭiṟkum, carkkaraikkumāka, kaimāṟappaṭṭaṉa.





tamiḻ ilakkiyattuḷ camaskiruta nākarīkattiṉ mutal nuḻaivu





camaskiruta nuḻaivu

akattiyaṉār, tammuṭaiya tamiḻilakkaṇattai iyaṟṟiya pōtu, camaskiruta nākarikam, tamiḻilakkiyattai, mutaṉmutalil pātikkat talaippaṭṭatu. tammuṭaiya malaiyiṭait tavappaḷḷiyil, uṇarccikku āṭpaṭāta aṟivu nilai kaṭanta, tavaneṟi mēṟkoḷvataik kāṭṭilum, pāṇṭi nāṭṭut tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷaik kaṟpatāl uḷavām uṇarccipūrva aṉupava vāḻkkaiyāl peritum īrkkap paṭṭa, potiyamalaiyil, iṭaṅkoṇṭirunta inta akattiyar, akattiyariṉ tapōvaṉattaic cērntavarkaḷil, mikamikap piṟpaṭṭavarāvar. piṟkālap purāṇak kataikaḷil, ivar, ivarukku muṟpaṭṭa akattiyar aṉaivaruṭaiya motta uruvamākkappaṭṭu, avvakattiyarkaḷiṉ ceyal pukaḻ aṉaittum, inta akattiyariṭattilēyē koṇṭu kuvikkappaṭṭaṉa. avar peyaraic cūḻak kaṭṭiviṭap paṭṭirukkum kataikaḷai nām nampuvatāṉāl, tamiḻilakkiyam kūṟum anta akattiyaṉār, nalluṇarvukku appāṟpaṭṭa poṟāmai, kaṭṭuppaṭuttalākāk kaṭuṅkōpaṅkaḷukku āṭpaṭak kūṭiya cātāraṇa maṉitarāvar. kataikaḷ, orupuṟam irukka, tamiḻiṉ moḻinaṭai iyalpukaḷil nuṇukkamāṉa ārāycciyai mēṟkoṇṭu akattiyaṉār, ammoḻi paṟṟiya ilakkaṇattai iyaṟṟiṉār eṉpatu, uṇmai nikaḻcciyām. avvāṟu iyaṟṟumpōtu, camaskiruta ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷellām, utāraṇattiṟkup peyarc coṟkaḷōṭu iṇaiyum ēḻu allatu eṭṭu vēṟṟumai urupukaḷ pōlvaṉa, ellā moḻikaḷukkum uḷḷa potu nilaiyām eṉa avar karutukiṟār. ulaka moḻikaḷ, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu uṟavu koḷḷāta, palvēṟu moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷaic cērntavai. aṭippaṭai amaippilēyē avai, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl, oru moḻiyiṉ collilakkaṇa amaippukkuḷ, vēṟu oru moḻiyiṉ collilakkaṇa amaippait tiṇippatu iyalātu eṉpaṉa pōlum uṇmaikaḷ akattiyaṉārkkut teriyā. camaskirutam virivāṉa , collilakkaṇa vakaikaḷaik koṇṭa taṉakkē uriya, colluru māṟṟu moḻi. āṉāl, tamiḻ, taṉi iru coṟkaḷ iṇaintē tokaic coṟkaḷai uruvākkavalla eḷiya teḷivāṉa collilakkaṇattaiyuṭaiya , oru moḻiyākum. camaskiruta muṟṟut toṭar vākkiyaṅkaḷil, collukkuc col uḷḷa , coṟṟoṭar uṟavu, accoṟkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa uṟavu uṇartta, accoṟkaḷiṉ īṟṟil iṇaikkappaṭum vikutikaḷiṉāl maṭṭumē uṇaralākum. ātaliṉ, coṟkaḷ, vākkiyattil eṅku vēṇṭumāṉālum iṭam koḷḷalām eṉpatu, ammoḻinilaiyākat, tamiḻ coṟkaḷai vēṇṭiyavāṟellām, iṭam māṟṟac ceyya iyalā, uṟutiyāṉa coṟṟoṭar nilaiyiṉaik koṇṭuḷatu. camaskiruta moḻiyiṉ ivvikutikaḷ, tam nilaiyil poruḷ uṇarttum collāka iṭam peṟa māṭṭār collīṟukaḷiṉāl ākkappaṭṭaṉavām ātaliṉ, taṅkaḷukkeṉap poruḷ illātaṉa. āṉāl, poruḷ uṇar coṟkaḷil, uṟaviṉai uṇarat tuṇaipurivaṉa ākum. ivaipōlum collīṟṟu vikutikaḷai ēṟṟuk koḷvataṉāl, vērccol, veṟum mūlamāka māṟi, am mūlamum taṉakkuttāṉē taṭam kāṇamāṭṭā mikapperiya māṟutalukku āḷākkiviṭukiṟatu. ivvakaiyil tat' eṉṟa mūlam, 'am eṉṟa īṟu ēṟṟu "tēsām" eṉa ākiṟatu. haṉ" eṉṟa viṉaivērccol peṟum pala urumāṟṟaṅkaḷil oṉṟu, "jakhāṉa eṉpatu. atupōla ṭah" eṉpataṉ urumāṟṟam, aṭṭok" eṉpatām. ivai, vikutikaḷ iṇaivāl, coṟkaḷiṉ iyalum uruvamum vēṟupaṭum moḻikaḷiṉ aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷām. tamiḻ, itaṟku māṟākat, taṉi iru coṟkaḷ iṇaintē tokaic coṟkaḷai uruvākkavalla oru colliyal muṟaiyiṉaik koṇṭa oru moḻiyām. tamiḻil poruḷ vēṟupaṭuttum colluṟuppukaḷāka varuvaṉa 1. ammoḻiyil tamakkeṉat taṉipporuḷum, taṉi āṭciyum uṭaiyavāya, ēḻām vēṟṟumai urupukaḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭum, oṭu, poruṭṭu, atu, uṭaiya, iṭai, talai, ticai eṉum ivaiyum, ivai pōlvaṉavum. tamiḻilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷ ivaṟṟaic collīṟukaḷ - collurupukaḷ eṉap poruttamuṟa aḻaippar. 2. ciṟitē urupu tirinta, oṭu, il, atu, āṟām vēṟṟumaip paṉmai uruvām, avai eṉṟa paṉmaic cuṭṭiṉ tiripu vaṭivām a eṉpaṉa. entac coṟkaḷiṉ tiripu vaṭivam eṉpatai uṇaralākā vakaiyil tirintirukkum, vēṟṟumai urupukaḷ, ai., eṉṟa iraṇṭum peyarccol, eḻuvāy, viḷi allāta vēṟṟumaikaḷil paṉmai vaṭivu peṟum camaskiruta muṟaikku muṟṟilum māṟākat tamiḻil, peyarc collukkum, ivvēṟṟumai urupukaḷukkum iṭaiyil, paṉmai kuṟikkum kuṟiyīṭukaḷ, "avarkaḷukku" eṉpatu pōl aḻaikkappaṭum uṇmai nikaḻcciyāl tam mūlaccol aṟiyāvāṟu tirintuviṭṭa ai , ku eṉṟa ivvurupukaḷum ōraḷavu taṉittaṉmai vāyntaṉavē eṉpatu uṇarappaṭum.

tamiḻum, camaskirutamum, ivvāṟu amaippu nilaiyil, muḻukka muḻukka muraṇpaṭṭiruppatāl, camaskiruta ilakkaṇa muṭipukaḷ, tamiḻilakkaṇattiṟkuc cariyākap poruntivārā. āṉāl, akattiyaṉār, camaskiruta moḻik kūṟṟuṇmaikaḷ, tamiḻilum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷa eṉak karutukiṟār. ataṉāl, camaskirutattil uḷḷatupōl, tamiḻilum ēḻu vēṟṟumaikaḷ irukka vēṇṭum eṉak karutukiṟār. vēṟṟumai urupēṟṟa camaskirutap peyarc coṟkaḷait tamiḻc coṟṟoṭarkaḷāka moḻipeyarkkiṟār. accoṟṟoṭarkaḷai, eḻuvāyp poruḷil varum, peyarccoṟkaḷ, avaṟṟait toṭarntu varuvaṉa eṉa iraṇṭākap pirittu, avviraṇṭām pirivukaḷait tamiḻ vēṟṟumai urupukaḷākak koḷkiṟār. pāṇiṉiyaip piṉpaṟṟit tamiḻil, avar, ēḻu vēṟṟumaikaḷaik kāṇkiṟār. cariyākac colvatāṉāl, uruvākkukiṟār. avar māṇavar tolkāppiyaṉār, aintiram vaḻic camaskiruta ilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷaip piṉpaṟṟi, avaṟṟai eṭṭu ākkukiṟār. kātalipiṉ' eṉpatu "kātali" eṉpataṉ vēṟṟumai urupēṟṟa peyarāyiṉ, kātali etir" eṉpatum vēṟumai urupēṟṟa peyarākātō? immuṟaiyil kaṇakkiṭṭāl, tamiḻ moḻiyil uḷḷa peyarccoṟkaḷiliruntu, peṟalākum viṉaiyeccap peyareccat toṭarkaḷ ettaṉai uḷavō, attaṉai vēṟṟumaikaḷait tamiḻp peyarkaḷ koṇṭirukka vēṇṭum.

tamiḻil camaskiruta ēḻu vēṟṟumaikaḷai iṟakkumati ceytatu pōlavē, camaskiruta ceyappāṭṭu viṉaiyaiyum avar, iṟakkumati ceytuḷḷār. ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉai ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟiyaviṉai ākiya ellā viṉaic coṟkaḷum, camaskirutattil aṭaiyum, uṟutiyāṉa col tiripē. ammoḻi, ceyappāṭṭu nilai, ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉaikaḷ, ceyappāṭṭu viṉaiyāka māṟum poḻutu, avviṉaikaḷ, "ciṅkam kollappaṭṭatu" eṉpatil uḷḷatupōl, ceyappaṭu poruḷaic, ceyviṉai mutalām eḻuvāyāka māṟṟum appaṇiyiṉaic ceykiṟatu. viṉai mutalām eḻuvāy teriyāta pōtu, allatu avviṉai mutal kuṟippiṭat tēvaiyillātapōtu, allatu ceyappaṭuporuḷ vaṟpuṟutti uṇarttat tēvaippaṭum pōtu ivvāṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu, payaṉuṭaiyatākiṟatu. ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟiya viṉaikaḷukkuc ceyappāṭṭut tirinta vaṭivaṅkaḷ tarappaṭumpōtu, itu pōlum aṭippaṭaip payaṉ etuvum kāṇappaṭuvatillai; eṉṟālum ceyappāṭṭuviṉai āṭciyāl, evvitac ciṟappup poruḷum peṟappaṭuvatillaiyākavum, camaskirutattil, ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟiya ceyappāṭṭu viṉaiyāṭci, ceyviṉai āṭciyiṉum, moḻinaṭai uṭaiyatām. ataṉpaṭi, sahpha vati eṉpatum, ‘'teṉaphīyatē'’ eṉpatum oṉṟē. piṉṉatu, vēṟu emmoḻiyil moḻipeyarppatu iyalātu eṉpatoṉṟē, vēṟupāṭu, āṅkilam camaskirutattōṭu uṟavuṭaiya moḻiyāyiṉum, "he is been” eṉpatu pōlum āṭci, āṅkilattilum poruḷaṟṟatu; nakaippiṟkuriyatu. tamiḻc ceyappāṭṭu viṉai amaippukaḷai, akattiyaṉār pukuttiyuḷḷār. iruntum, "tāṭya te” eṉpataip “paṭu” eṉṟa viṉaic collai , “aṭi” eṉpataṉ iṟantakāla viṉai yeccattōṭu iṇaittu, ‘'aṭikkappaṭṭāṉ'’ eṉa avarāl, moḻipeyarkka muṭintālum, ceyappaṭuporuḷ. kuṉṟiya ceyappāṭṭu viṉaiyiṉait tamiḻukkuk koṇṭuvara, avarāl muṭiyavillai. iraṇṭu viṉaic coṟkaḷai oru collākat tokaippaṭutti, attokaic colliṉ irumūlaṅkaḷukkum, eḻuvāy allatu viṉaimutalāka vēṟu vēṟupaṭṭa iruvarkaḷaik koḷvatu, kāraṇakāriya muṟaiyiṉaiyum, tamiḻaip poṟuttamaṭṭil, kāraṇakāriya muṟaiyiṉaiyum, tamiḻaip poṟuttamaṭṭil, kāraṇa kāriya muṟaiyiṉai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭa ilakkaṇa vitikaḷaiyum mīṟiyatākum. ātaliṉ, ‘'aṭikkappaṭum'’ eṉpatai, “aṭikka” (maṟṟoruvaṉ) aṭikkum poḻutu. eṉṟum, ‘'paṭu” tuṉpam aṉupavi eṉṟum pirittuk kāṇpatu, tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalpukku muraṇām.'’ aṭikkappaṭum” eṉpataṉ moḻinaṭaivaṭivam, attokaic colliṉ muṟpakuti, urupēlāp peyarc collāka varum, “aṭipaṭu “ allatu “aṭiyuṇ” eṉpatām. camaskirutac ceyalpāṭṭu viṉaikaḷait tamiḻil moḻipeyarkkat tēvaippaṭṭa tālum, akattiyaṉār māṇavar, tolkāppiyaṉār, tammuṭaiya ilakkaṇattai, “eḻuttu eṉappaṭuva'’ (tol: eḻuttu : 1) eṉap pōlic ceyap pāṭṭu viṉaiyōṭu toṭaṅkuvatu pōlac camaskiruta moḻiyil cintittut, tamiḻil eḻutuvārkkup peritum payaṉuṭaiyatāka amaintatālum, akattiyaṉār, icceyalpāṭṭu viṉaiyiṉaik kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷār. iyalpāṉa tamiḻppēccu naṭaiyil, entat tamiḻaṉum, ennilaiyilum mēṟkoḷḷātatum, camaskirutattiliruntu tamiḻil eḷitil moḻipeyarkka, camaskirutap paṇṭitarkaḷukkut tuṇai niṟkak kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatālum, ippōli ceyappāṭṭu viṉai, nam kālattil poruḷkaḷaic ceyappāṭṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷil viḷakkum āṅkilattil cintittu, tamiḻil eḻutak kaṟṟuk koṇṭa kāraṇattāl, eḻuttu naṭait tamiḻil, atu oru naṭaimuṟaiyākiviṭṭatu, paṭu eṉṟa, moḻi marapiṟku ēlā oru collurupu, paipiḷiṉ tamiḻ moḻipeyarppu nūliṉ ovvoru pakkattaiyum, turatiruṣṭavacamākat tamiḻp paṇṭitarkaḷiṉ ilakkiyap paṭaippukaḷaiyum pāḻpaṭuttukiṟatu.

tamiḻil, camskiruta nākarīka mutal talaiyīṭukaḷil, ivai orucilavē, aṭutta talaiyīṭukaḷ, coṟkaḷ, koḷkaikaḷ, ceyyuṭkaṟpaṉaikaḷ, purāṇa, maṟṟum piṟa kaṭṭukkataikaḷ, nampikkaikaḷ, mūṭa nampikkaikaḷ, viññāṉa, oḻukka, camayap pāṭaṅkaḷ, urainaṭai, pāvakaikaḷ, maṟṟum piṟa piṟa , kālam cellac cella, camaskiruta nākarīkattiṉ paṭaiyeṭuppu, piṟkālat tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷai maṭṭumē terinta oruvaṉukkuc cintaṉaikaḷ pōl allāmal iyaṟkaic cintaṉaikaḷaik koṇṭa taṉṉuṭaiya moḻinaṭai avaṉ tāymoḻiyallāta vēṟṟu moḻi pōlum. paḻakkamilā moḻiyākat tōṉṟumaḷavu, tamiḻilakkiyam, muḻukka, muḻukka vaṭavarkaḷiṉ ātikkattiṉ kīḻ akappaṭṭuk koḷḷumaḷavu mikap peritāki viṭṭatu.

akattiyaṉāruṭaiya , avar peyarāl, akattiyam eṉa aḻaikkappaṭum ilakkaṇam ippōtu illai. āṉāl, jamatakkiṉiyār makaṉ tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyār iyaṟṟiya, tolkāppiyaṉār eṉap payila aḻaikkappaṭum avar māṇavar iyaṟṟiya, ilakkaṇam iṉṟum uḷḷatu. atu, teḷivāka, akattiyattaiyē mūlamākak koṇṭuḷatu. ittamiḻ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ, eḻuttu, col, yāppu, ākiyaṉa, paṟṟi maṭṭumallāmal, pāṭṭiṉ karupporuḷ kuṟittum ilakkaṇam vakuppatu, ivaṟṟiṉ taṉicciṟappām. pāṭṭiṉ karupporuḷ, ilakkiya marapukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟāl, tamiḻc ceyyuḷ, camaskirutac ceyyuḷiliruntu aṟavē vēṟupaṭuvatu kaṇṭa ippirāmaṇa āciriyarkaḷ, tam ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil, ipporuḷkaḷ paṟṟiya āyviṉaiyum mēṟkoḷvatu tēvai eṉa uṇarntaṉar. tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyāriṉ nūlām. avar tamiḻp peyarāl vaḻaṅkum tol kāppiyattiṉ piṟpakutikaḷ, paṇṭait tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ, tamakkē uriya taṉi iyalpiṟ pāṭiya pāṭaṟporuḷkaḷām akam, puṟam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ovvoru nikaḻccikaḷ kuṟittum āyvu ceykiṉṟaṉa. poruḷatikāram eṉṟa tolkāppiyattiṉ īṟṟup pakutiyiliruntu, āriyarkaḷōṭu nerukkamāṉa toṭarpu koḷvataṟku muṉṉar, tamiḻar naṭattiya vāḻkkai muṟai, tamiḻ uḷḷam, āriya ilakkiyaṅkaḷukku āṭpaṭāta taṟku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻ ilakkiyam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ muḻup paṭattai varaintu koḷḷalām.

atu pōlum oru paṭakkāṭci, tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattil kāṭṭiyiruppatu pōla, āriyattukku muntiya tamiḻar nākarikam” (pre Aryan Tamil Culture) eṉṟa eṉ nūlilum, iṉi vara irukkam, paṇṭait tamiḻar (Ancient Tamilis) eṉṟa eṉ nūlilum kāṭṭiyirukkum paṭakkāṭci kaḷai, muḻumaiyākkum. -

poruḷatikārattil āriya nuḻaivu

tamiḻp pākkaḷ kuṟitta tammuṭaiya ilakkaṇattil, āriyak karuttukkaḷai iṟakkumati ceyyum kavarcci yiliruntu, tolkāppiyaṉār viṭupaṭavillai. paṇṭait tamiḻp pākkaḷiṉ marapukaḷai allatu viti muṟaikaḷai viḷakkik kūṟumpōtu, avar kālattil vaḻakkil irunta, maṟaintaṉa pōka eñciyirunta aṉaittut tamiḻp pākkaḷaiyum uṟutiyākap piṉpaṟṟiyuḷḷār. āṉālum, tām peṟṟirunta, camaskiruta ilakkiya aṟivālum, araciyal neṟimuṟaikaḷum, camutāya amaippukaḷum, teyvattaṉmai yāl vakukkappeṟṟavai eṉṟa tam nampikkaiyālum, avar aṭikkaṭi āḻttappaṭṭuḷḷār. camutāya urimaiyōṭu, pāṭṭuṭait talaivarkaḷām kalvi urimaiyum aṉupavikkum mutal mūṉṟu varuṇavattavar aṭaṅkiya nāṉku varuṇattavarkaḷāka makkaḷaip pirippatē camutāya amaippiṉ vitimuṟaiyākum eṉpatu tolkāppiyarkku uriya karuttu. āṉāl, avar kaṟṟa tamiḻ pāṭalkaḷil, āriyak koḷkaippaṭi, mikavum iḻintavarkaḷāṉa, vēṭṭaiyāṭum iṉattiṉ talaivaṉum, mīṉ piṭikkum iṉattiṉ talaivaṉum, kātaṟpākkaḷiṉ talaivarāvar. immuraṇpāṭṭai viḷakka, avar eṭuttuk koḷḷa muyaṟci mikavum kuṟaipāṭuṭaiyatu vēṇṭāviruppāṉatu. mēlum, āriyac camutāya vitimuṟaikaḷil ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭa tirumaṇa muṟaikaḷ eṭṭu uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, tamiḻp pākkaḷil iraṇṭē uḷḷaṉa. ivviru muṟaikaḷaiyum oṉṟu eṉakkāṭṭa, avar vīṇē muyaṉṟuḷḷār. tamiḻ akattiṇai oḻukkam, camaskirutak kāma oḻukkattaiviṭa meṉmaiyāṉatām; tarma artta mōkṣaṅkaḷait, tamiḻp puṟattiṇai oḻukkaṅkaḷil tiṇittuviṭa muṭiyātām eṉṟālum, uraiyāciriyar palarum vīṇē muyalvatu pōlavē, tolkāppiyarum, kātalum, pōrumē vāḻkkaiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷām eṉak karutum tamiḻar vāḻkkai muṟaiyait tarma, artta , kāma mōkṣamē vāḻkkaiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷaram eṉakkarutum āriyar. vāḻkkai muṟaiyōṭu poruttikkāṇa muyaṉṟuḷḷār. poruḷatikāram, kaṭaici atikārattil, tam kālattil tamiḻil iṭampeṟāta, āṉāl, tamakkut terinta camaskiruta eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷ uḷḷa, pala ilakkiya vaṭivaṅkaḷai vivarittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivaiyum, ivai pōlum pala iṭaṅkaḷil tamiḻukku uriyaṉa allātaṉavaṟṟait tamiḻppākkaḷ kuṟitta tammuṭaiya ilakkaṇattil pukuttiyuḷḷār. ataṉāl, tolkāppiyar ivvāṟu ceyyumiṭaṅkaḷil, avaruṭaiya uraiyāciriyarkaḷāl, avar kūṟṟiṟkut tēvaiyāṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷait tamiḻiliruntu kāṭṭa muṭiyāmal pōvatu viyappiṉait taruvatillai. tolkāppiyar, tamiḻil pukuttum, maṟṟum iru āriyak koḷkaikaḷ paṟṟi avaruṭaiya kālam kuṟitta āyviṉpōtu teḷivākak kūṟappaṭum. tamiḻil camaskirutac coṟkaḷ nuḻaivu

akattiyaṉārum, tolkāppiyaṉārum, tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku oli muṟaiyil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuviṭṭa camaskirutac coṟkaḷām tatpavac coṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟait tamiḻil pukuttiviṭṭaṉar. avvakaic coṟkaḷ iṭaikkāla uraiyāciriyarkaḷiṉ uraiyil eṭuttatāḷappaṭṭirukkum akattiyac cūttaraṅkaḷilum tolkāppiya ilakkaṇattilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ilakkaṇa vitikaḷaik kuṟikkavalla marapuc coṟkaḷil perumpālaṉa vaṟṟiṟkut tamiḻc coṟkaḷaik kāṇumaḷavu , tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiṉai akattiyaṉār, matittiruntār. kiṭaittirukkum paḻaiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil, camaskirutattiliruntu kaṭaṉ peṟṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭirukkum coṟkaḷ mikamikak kuṟaivu allatu aṟavē illai. āṉāl, kālam cellac cella oru . pāṭalil iṭam peṟṟirukkum camaskirutac coṟkaḷiṉ catavikitam, appāṭaliṉ kālattai uṇarttum aṟikuṟiyām eṉak koḷḷumaḷavu, accoṟkaḷiṉ nuḻaivu, paṭippaṭiyākap, perukik koṇṭē vantuḷatu. ivvāṟu, camaskirutac coṟkaḷ, tamiḻil mēlum mēlum iṭam peṟṟu vantaṉa eṉṟālum, tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattup perumpakutik karup poruḷāka irukkum tamiḻilakkiya, marapukaḷ, āṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu varai viṭāmal kaṭaip piṭikkappaṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉa.

akattiyar kālam

akattiyaṉār, avar māṇavar tolkāppiyaṉār ākiyōriṉ kālam etu? akattiyaṉār ilakkaṇam, ippōtu illai. āṉāl, tolkāppiyaṉār, avarai aṭikkaṭi eṭuttu āḷukiṟār. avar cūttiraṅkaḷil eṅkellām eṉṟu kūṟuvar eṉap poruḷpaṭum ‘'eṉpa'’ eṉṟa col iṭappeṟukiṟatō, aṅkellām uraiyāciriyarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar” eṉṟē poruḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiyar, tammuṭaiya ilakkaṇattai " eḻuttu eṉṟu collappaṭupavai yāvai eṉṟāl, avai, a eṉṟa eḻuttil toṭaṅki, ṉ eṉṟa eḻuttil muṭiyum muppatu eṉṟu āciriyar kūṟuvar" eṉṟa kūṟṟōṭu toṭaṅkukiṟār.

"eḻuttu eṉappaṭum,

akaram mutal,

ṉakara iṟuvāy muppaḵtu eṉpa."

(tol : eḻuttu : 1)





ākavē, akattiyaṉār tammuṭaiya ilakkaṇattait tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ muppatu eṉṟa eṇṇilēyē toṭaṅkiṉār eṉa nām koḷḷalām. eḻuttu eṉṟa - col, tamiḻ ilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷāl, kuṟippiṭattakka oru ciṟappāṉa nilaiyil āḷappaṭṭuḷatu. oliyaiyum, uruvattaiyum orucērak kuṟikkum vakaiyil, atu peyariṭappaṭṭuḷatu. muṉṉatu olivaṭivil uḷḷa eḻuttu eṉap poruḷpaṭum. olivaṭivu eḻuttu' eṉṟum, piṉṉatu nērkkōṭukaḷākavum vaḷainta kōṭukaḷākavum varaiyappaṭṭa eḻuttu eṉap poruḷpaṭum. varivaṭivu eḻuttu" eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭum. āṅkilattil olivaṭiveḻuttu, varivaṭiveḻuttu eṉṟa iraṇṭaiyum orucērak kuṟikkum iyalpuṭaiya col etuvum illāmaiyāl eḻuttu eṉṟa accollai apporuḷ kuṟikkum vakaiyil āṅkilattil moḻipeyarppatu iyalātu. akṣarā eṉṟa col camaskiruta āciriyarkaḷāl ivviru poruḷ kuṟikkum vakaiyil, āḷappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl, akṣarā eṉṟa accol, kēṭkakkūṭiya oliyai uruvākkum, kāṟṟiṉ alai atirviṉ, vāṉaveḷi aṭittaḷattiṉ mūlamām. nilaipēṟuṭaiya oli eṉakkarutum mīmāmcakar, maṟṟum viyākkarṉikāk karuttukkaḷōṭu toṭarpuṭaiyatu ākaliṉ, eḻuttu eṉṟa tamiḻccol, akṣarā eṉpataṉ moḻipeyarppu ākātu.

eḻuttukkaḷ kuṟittu akattiyaṉār kūṟum ilakkaṇap paṭiyē, tamiḻ varivaṭiveḻuttukkaḷ, urutu moḻikkākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum arāpiya eḻuttukkaḷ tavirttu, intiyāvil vaḻakkattil uḷḷa ellā eḻuttukkaḷaiyum pōlavē, ‘'pirāmi'’ eṉap peyar cūṭṭappaṭṭa, acōkaṉ kalveṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiliruntē, uruppeṟṟaṉa eṉpatai nām aṟivōm. eḻuttil vaṭikkappeṟṟa tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ, ituvarai kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭāta mikappaḻaiya mātiri vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ utāraṇaṅkaḷ, pāṇṭi nāṭṭu malaikkukaikaḷil uḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷil namakkuk kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. ikkalveṭṭukkaḷ, pittup piṭittu alaiyum makkaḷ kūṭṭattiliruntu, veku tolaivil uḷḷa, iyaṟkaiyāṉa malaikkukaikaḷil taṅkaḷai aḻiyāp pēriṉpa nilaiyām nirvāṇa nilaikkuk koṇṭu collum, amaitiyāṉa tavanilaiyil vāḻntu vanta peḷatta, jaiṉa muṉivarkaḷiṉ tūṇṭutalāl eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ikkalveṭṭukkaḷ namakkuk kiṭaittuḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttu muṟai, mikappaḻaiya mātiri vaṭivaṅkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟu, pirāmi vaṭiveḻuttukkaḷai, mikap poruttamuṟac colvatāṉāl, teṉṉintiya meḷariya vaṭiveḻuttukkaḷait, tamiḻ eḻuttu naṭaikku mēṟkoṇṭa taṟkālika mutal muyaṟciyumām. avai, taṉi mey eḻuttiṟkum, akaramoṭu iṇaintu varum uyirmey eḻuttiṟkum iṭaiyil eḷitil olippataṟku vāyppāka, mey eḻuttukkaḷ, akaramoṭu iṇaintē olikkappaṭum (meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum” (tol. eḻuttu : 13 eṉat tolkāppiyar kūṟuvatu pōla) evvita vēṟupāṭum kāṭṭavillai; atāvatu mey eḻuttai uṇartta, mēlē puḷḷi vaikkum muṟai iṉṉamum - koṇṭu varappaṭavillai. āṉāl, avai, tamiḻukkē uriya ciṟappu meyyām “ḻa” iraṭṭittavaḻi, valintum mūkkoli eḻuttām “ṉa” karam muṉ varumpōtu melintum olikkappaṭuvatum, nākkai uḷmaṭittu olittu eḻuvatumāṉa “ṟa” nakarattiṟku muṉvarum mukkoli eḻuttaik kuṟikkat taṉṉāl maṭṭumē iyalvatum, iṉṟai olippu muṟaiyil pulloli eḻuttām “na” karattiliruntu vēṟupāṭu kāṇa iyalātatumākiya iru cuḻi "ṉa" ākiyavaṟṟaik kuṟikkum varivaṭivaṅkaḷaik koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. ākak kiṭaikkak kūṭiya akaccāṉṟukaḷiṉ paṭi, ki.mu. mūṉṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil, allatu, allatu, iraṇṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil, kalveṭṭu vaṭippatil, nīṭittup payiṉṟu, aḻiyāppayiṟci peṟṟuviṭṭa veḷināṭṭiliruntu vantavarkaḷāl, tamiḻ, mutaṉmutalil, varivaṭivil eḻutappaṭṭatu eṉat terikiṟatu. tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷai eḻuti vaippataṟkut : taṉiyāṉa neṭuṅkaṇakku illāmaiyē kiṟittuva āṇṭut toṭakkattiṟku mikavum muṟpaṭṭa kālattilēyē, perumaḷavil iyaṟppaṭṭirukka vēṇṭiyavāya, tamiḻp pākkaḷ, mīḷavum peṟamāṭṭāvakaiyil, aḻintu viṭṭataṟkuk kāraṇamām. ākavē, tamiḻ eḻuttu muṟai, ellōrālum, perumaḷavil mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭiya ki.mu. mutalām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉṉar, akattiyaṉār vāḻntirukka muṭiyātu eṉa muṭivu ceykiṉṟēṉ. tolkāppiya ārāycci āciriyarum avar māṇavarum ataṟku, oru nūṟṟāṇṭu allatu caṟṟu atikap paṭiyāṉa piṟpaṭṭa kālattil vāḻntirukka vēṇṭum eṉak koḷḷumāṟu nammai vaṟpuṟuttukiṟatu. tirumaṇam, maṟṟum iṉiya nikaḻccikaḷukku ēṟpuṭaiya nāḻikai eṉum poruḷil, ōrai" eṉṟa collait tolkāppiyar āṇṭuḷḷār.

"maṟainta oḻukkattu ōraiyum nāḷum,

tuṟanta oḻukkam kiḻavōṟku illai"

(-tol : poruḷ : kaḷavu : 44)





"tīya ōraiyilum, tīya nāḷilum, puṇarcciyaik kaiviṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟa koḷkai, maṟainta oḻukkamām, kaḷavuk kālattil talaivaṉukku illai"

"ōrai" eṉṟa attamiḻc col, "avar" (Hour) eṉṟa āṅkilaccollaip pōlavē muṭinta muṭivāka horā (Hora) eṉṟa kirēkka moḻic colliliruntē peṟappaṭṭatām. "herā” eṉṟa accol kirēkka moḻiyil, ki.mu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil, "paruvam” eṉṟa potup poruḷil tāṉ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. nāḷiṉ irupattunāṉku kūṟukaḷil, ovvoru kūṟum, ēḻu kōḷkaḷil ovvoru kōḷiṉ āṭcik kīḻ, varuvatākak koḷḷappaṭṭa, ki.mu. iraṇṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ, ‘'horā’ eṉṟa accollukku, nāḷiṉ irupattu nāṉku kūṟṟil oru kūṟṟai uṇarttum poruḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. horā eṉṟa accol, tāṉ uṇarttum cōtiṭap poruṭkuṟippōṭu kimu mutal iraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil, kirēkka maṉṉarkaḷāl, āṭci ceyyappaṭṭu vanta kāntārattiṟkup payaṇam ceytatu. camaskiruta nūlāciriyarkaḷ kirēkkarkaḷiṉ jōtiṭakkalaiyaik kaṟṟuk koṇṭapōtu, atu, camaskirutattil iṭam koṇṭatu. piṉṉar, atu, teṟkil payaṇam ceytu, tamiḻil nuḻaintu viṭṭatu. “ōrai’ eṉṟa col, tolkāppiyar kālattiṉ mēlvarampākum. ki.pi. mutalāṇṭiṟku muṉṉart tamiḻil iṭampeṟṟirukka muṭiyātu eṉṟu koṇṭāl, atu, oru naṭunilai matippīṭām. tolkāppiyaṉār, tammuṭaiya ilakkaṇa nūlil, ‘ōrai eṉṟa collai āṇṭiruntālum, tamiḻarkaḷiṉ perumpakutiyiṉar, atāvatu, pārppaṉarallātār āriya nākarīkattāl aṭimai koḷḷappaṭṭu, ataṉāl kōḷkaḷ kuṟitta vāṉanūl aṟivait tolkāppiyar kālattukku mika mika muṟpaṭṭa kālattilēyē peṟṟiruntaṉar; allatu atil nampikkai - koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatai nām karuttil koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ivvāṉanūl kalaiyōṭu toṭarpuṭaiyaṉa, ēḻu nāṭkaḷaik koṇṭa vāramum, ovvoru nāḷum ‘ antāḷiṉ mutal iraṇṭarai nāḻikaiyai āṭci celuttum - kōḷāl peyariṭappaṭuvatumām. ākavē, vārattiṉ nāṭkaḷiṉ, kōḷ cārpuṭaiya peyarkaḷ tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil ki.pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai kāṇappaṭavillai.

tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattiliruntu peṟappaṭum . tolkāppiyaṉār kālattai uṟuti ceyyat tuṇaipuriyum piṟitoru ceytiyum uḷatu. akattiṇaittalaivaṉiṉ tōḻarkaḷākavum, avaṉ kātal oḻukkaṅkaḷukkut tuṇaivarkaḷākavum varuvōruḷ, pārppārkaḷaiyum kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

"pārppāṉ, pāṅkaṉ, tōḻi, cevili

cīrttaku ciṟappiṉ kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyoṭu

aḷaviyaṉ marapiṉ, aṟuvakai yōrum

kaḷaviṉil kiḷavikku uriyar eṉpa" .



"pāṇaṉ, kūttaṉa, viṟali , parattai ,

yāṇam cāṉṟa aṟivar, kaṇṭōr,

pēṇutaku ciṟappiṉ pārppāṉ, mutalā,

muṉṉuṟak kiḷanta kiḷaviyoṭu tokai it

toṉṉeṟi marapiṟ kaṟpiṟku uriyar"



"pārppār aṟivar eṉṟivar kiḷavi,

yārkkum varaiyār yāppoṭu puṇarntē"



tol. poruḷ : ceyyuḷ : 181, 182, 189





avvakaiyil, kātal nikaḻccikaḷil peruntuṇai puripavarkaḷākap pirāmaṇarkaḷ āḷappaṭuvatai, āḻntu muṭivu ceytuḷḷār. itu camaskiruta nāṭakattiṉ oru marapu ākum. akanāṉūṟṟilō, allatu puṟanāṉūṟṟilō attakaiya kuṟippu etaiyum kāṇa muṭiyavillaiyātaliṉ tolkāppiyaṉār, atai oru koḷkaiyaḷavil kūṟiyuḷḷārēyallatu, ōr uṇmai nikaḻcciyākak kūṟavillai eṉṟē nāṉ eṭuttuk koḷkiṟēṉ. puṟanāṉūṟṟil, pārppār eṉṟa col, nāṉku muṟai iṭam peṟukiṟatu; antaṇar maṟṟum avartam pulamai paṟṟi, mēlum eṭṭukkuṟippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.

"āvum āṉ iyal pārppaṉa mākkaḷum"

puṟam : 9:1





"pārppārt tappiya koṭumai"

- puṟam 34 : 3





"ēṟṟa pārppārkku īrṅkai niṟaiyap pūvum

poṉṉum puṉalpaṭac corintu”

-puṟam : 367 : 4 - 5



"anti antaṇar aruṅkaṭaṉ iṟukkum

muttī viḷakku” - puṟam 2 23 24



“pulaṉ aḻukkaṟṟa antaṇāḷaṉ”

puṟam : 126 : 17.



"yāṉē paricilaṉ maṉṉum antaṇaṉ”

- puṟam : 200 : 13.



“antaṇaṉ pulavaṉ koṇṭuvan taṉaṉē”

puṟam : 361 : 4



“kēḷvi muṟṟiya vēḷvi antaṇaṉ”

-puṟam : 361 : 4.



"ākkural kāṇpiṉ antaṇāḷar'’

-puṟam : 362 : 8



“iṟaiñcuka peruma! niṉ ceṉṉi, ciṟanta

nāṉmaṟai muṉivar ēntukai etirē”

- puṟam : 6:19 - 20



"āṉṟa kēḷvi aṭaṅkiya koḷkai,

nāṉmaṟai mutalvar,”

puṟam 26 12 13



"aṟampuri koḷkai nāṉmaṟai mutalvar'’

puṟam 93:17.



“vēta vēḷvit toḻil”

puṟam : 228 : 9





ivaiyellām avarkaḷiṉ vētappulamai, vēḷvit tī, ēṟṟuk koṇṭa koṭaivaḷam ākiyavaṟṟaik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉavēyallatu, kātalarkaḷiṭaiyē, avarkaḷ mēṟkoṇṭa tūtu paṟṟik kuṟippiṭavillai. akanāṉūṟṟil aka vēḷvi ceytaṟiyāta oru pārppāṉam, "vēḷāp pārpāṉ" (akam : 24 : 1) aracarkaḷiṭaiyē tūtuvat toḻil mēṟkoṇṭu celluṅkāl, maḻavar eṉṟa āṟalai kaḷvarāl kollappaṭṭa oru pārppāṉum, "tūtu oy pārppāṉ. maṭi veḷḷōlai... taṭintu uṭaṉ vīḻtta kaṭuṅkaṇ maḻavar" (akam : 337 : 7-11), kuṟuntokaiyil, maṇam ākā iḷaiyapārppāṉ oruvaṉiṉ uṭaimaikaḷam, "taṇṭoṭu piṭitta, tāḻka maṇṭalattup paṭiva uṇṭip pārppaṉa makaṉē! eḻutākkaṟpiṉ niṉ col" (kuṟuntokai : 156 : 3-5) kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. āṉāl, kātalariṭaiyē tūtu cellum ceyal etuvum, avaṟṟil kuṟippiṭappaṭavillai. kātalariṭaiyē, pārppaṉar tūtu cellum marapiṉait tolkāppiyaṉār, camaskiruta nāṭakattiliruntē tamiḻp pākkaḷukkuk koṇṭu vantiruppatāl, avar, muṟaiyāṉa nāṭakaṅkaḷ, camaskirutattil vaḷarntuviṭṭa piṟkālattil, vāḻntirukka vēṇṭum. pāsara (Bhasa) eṉpavartām, camaskirutap pulavarkaḷil paḻamaiyāṉavar. avar, ki.mu. mūṉṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṟpaṭṭa kālattaic cērntavarāvatu iyalātu; annāṭaka marapait tolkāppiyaṉār, tammuṭaiya ilakkaṇattil iṇaittuk koḷvataṟkut tuṇai ceyvataṟkēṟpa, teṉṉintiyāvai uṭaiya, camaskiruta nāṭakaṅkaḷiṉ, avvilakkiya marapu, cila āṇṭu kālattai eṭuttuk koṇṭirukka vēṇṭum. col, yāppu uḷḷiṭṭa ataṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷukku āṟṟiya muḻu niṟaivāṉa aṭippaṭaip paṇiyuṭpaṭa, tamiḻ nākarīka vaḷarccikku, akattiyar paṅku, kūṟip pārāṭṭattakka peruñceyalākum. āṉāl, paṇṭaiya kaṭṭukkataiyāḷarkaḷum, iṉṟaiya varalāṟṟu ārāycciyāḷarkaḷum, aṟputat tiruviḷaiyāṭalkaḷaik kaṟpittu, attūyavarai mācupaṭutta muyaṉṟuḷḷaṉar. avvakaiyil, paḻaiya tamiḻp purāṇaṅkaḷ, avar, civaṉ allatu cuppiramaṇiyariṭam tamiḻ kaṟṟār. tamiḻarkaḷukkut tamiḻaik kaṟpittār eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. akattiyar, tammiṭaiyē vantu kāṭcitarum varai tamiḻar, eḻuttaṟivavillā ūmaiyarāy iruntaṉar? eṉa nāṉ karutukiṉṟēṉ. tiruvāḷar ṭi. ār. paṇṭarkār avarkaḷ, “atuvaraik kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭātirunta teṉṉāṭ ṭiṉuḷ. tirāviṭarkaḷai nākarīka makkaḷāka māṟṟiyavāṟē, mēlum mēlum ūṭuruvic ceṉṟār.'’ eṉak kūṟukiṟār. "(Carmichal Lecturas. 1918 Page 18) tiruvāḷar, ṭi.ār. irāmakiruṣṇa cāstiri avarkaḷ, tamiḻarkaḷiṉ camutāya ilakkiya vāḻkkai muṟaiyil akattiyar pukuttiya putumai, vakutta maṟucīramaippukaḷ paṟṟip pēcukiṟār. (Proceedings and Transactions of Third Oriental Conference 1924 Page 205) ivaiyaṉaittum vaṭikaṭṭiya camayac cārpāṉa kaṭṭukkataikaḷ. makkaḷ vāḻntu vaḷarnta cuṟṟuccūḻalai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭē eḻuntamaiyāl, māṟṟavō, aḻikkavō muṭiyātatāṉa, tamakkē uriya ilakkiya marapukaḷōṭu iṇaintu, muḻumaiyāka vaḷarcci peṟṟa, ōr ilakkiyattaiyē akattiyar kaṇṭār. muḻumaiyāka vaḷarcci peṟṟa ilakkiya naṭaiyōṭu kūṭiya oru moḻiyaiyum avar kaṇṭār. ammoḻi ilakkiyam, kuṟitta ilakkaṇattai iyaṟṟiṉār. avvaḷavē, atuvallatu vēṟu illai.

akattiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē, eṇṇaṟṟa tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ iruntirukka vēṇṭum. illaiyeṉṟāl, yāppu kuṟitta ilakkaṇattai avarāl iyaṟṟiyirukka iyalātu. tiruntiya ilakkiya naṭaiyām centamiḻ kuṟittuk kūṟat tēvaiyillai. tamiḻc ceyyuḷ naṭaiyeḻunta kālattiṟkum, akattiyaṉār kālattiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa kālakaṭṭattil, puṟattiṇai, akattaṇaip pāṭalkaḷāl uruvākiya eḻucci, avvakai ilakkiyaṅkaḷai, avar kālattiṟkup piṉṉar ainnūṟu āṇṭuvaraiyum, paṇṭaiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ nilaikulaintu, camaskirutak kavitaikaḷāl īrppuṇṭu, akattūṇṭutal peṟṟup putiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟip paḻaiya kavitai muṟaikaḷaip peritum maṟaittuviṭṭa akkālam varai, koṇṭu ceṉṟatu. piṟkālap purāṇak kataikaḷ, akattiyaṉār, tamiḻait tōṟṟuvittār eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ām; tamiḻ moḻiyuḷ , inta moḻiyilakkaṇa , mutal āciriyariṉ, nalvarukaiyiṉ kīḻ naṭaipeṟṟa āriya nuḻaiviṉ perumpayaṉē, piṟkālat tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ eṉṟa uṇmaikkaru, ikkataiyuḷ aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu eṉalām. ākavē, akattiyak kaṭṭukkataik kaḷañciyattukkut tamiḻilakkiyam perumpaṅku aḷikka muṭiyum eṉa, evarum etirpārkkalām. āṉāl, akattiyak kaṭṭukkataikaḷukkut tamiḻ āṟṟi yirukkum paṅku mikak kuṟaivām eṉpatu aṟiya, ēmāṟṟam taruvatāy uḷḷatu. kuṟuntokaiyilum, naṟṟiṇaiyilum, avar paṟṟiya kuṟippu etaiyum eṉṉāl kāṇa muṭiyavillai. puṟanāṉūṟṟil, vētavēḷvi ceyvōriṉ vāḻiṭamākap potiya malaiyaik kūṟum kuṟippu oṉṟu uḷatu. "muttī viḷakkil tuñcum, poṟkōṭṭu imayamum, potiyamum pōṉṟē ." (puṟam: 2 : 23-24) akam, puṟam eṉṟa iru tokai nūlkaḷil potiyamalai, pāṇṭiyarkku uriyatāka, ēḻu pākkaḷil viḷakkappaṭṭirukka, atai, akattiyarkku uriyatākkum kuṟippu etuvum avaṟṟil illai. akattiyaṉār kālam, tokai nūlkaḷil mikap paḻaiyaṉavāya, annāṉku tokai nūl pākkaḷukku naṉimika aṇmaiyāṉatu. ākavē avai avarai makkaḷuḷ oruvarāka ōr ilakkaṇa āciriyarākap pārttatē allatu, oru riṣiyāka matikkavillai eṉṟa uṇmaiyē, itaṟkuk kāraṇamātal kūṭum.

pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉiṉ aṉpukkuriya pulavar māṅkuṭi marutaṉār (kipi. 450) avaṉaip pārāṭṭip pāṭiya maturaik kāñciyil, oru pakuti uraiyāciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyarāl, atu, akattiyaraik kuṟippatākak kāṭṭumaḷavu citaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu, neṭuñceḻiyaṉaip pārttu, "pakka malaikaḷil vīḻntu ōṭum aruvikaḷaik koṇṭa malaikku uriyavaṉē! teṉṉavarkaḷai ōṭṭiyavaṉum, aṇukuvataṟku ariya āṟṟal vāyntavaṉumākiya paḻamperum kaṭavuḷukku, aṭuttu irukka virumpuvōṉē!'’ eṉap pārāṭṭukiṟatu.

"teṉṉavaṉ peyariya tuṉṉarum tuppiṉ

toṉmutu kaṭavuḷ piṉṉar mēya

varaittāḻ aruvip poruppiṉ poruna!"

- maturaikkāñci : 40-42





ippakuti, poruḷ, teḷivu kāṇamāṭṭātu uḷatu. "teṉṉavaṉ” eṉpatu irāvaṇaṉaik kuṟikkum eṉṟāl, irāmaṉukku aṭuttavaṉāka irukka virumpiṉāṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ eṉpatu poruḷātal vēṇṭum. āṉāl, irāvaṇaṉait, teṉṉavaṉ eṉak kuṟippiṭum orē oru kuṟippu, atuvum mikavum piṟpaṭṭa pāṭṭu oṉṟil uḷatu.

"teṉṉavaṉ malai eṭukkac, cēyiḻai oṭuṅkak kaṇṭu,

maṉṉavaṉ viralāl ūṉṟa”

-tirunāvukkaracar tēvāram tirukkacci mēṟṟaḷi.





"teṉṉavaṉ" eṉpatu, pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷukkup potuvāka iṭṭu vaḻaṅkum oru paṭṭappeyar. appāṭṭu, āriyap purāṇak kataikaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil parava iṭam koṇṭuviṭṭa ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāṭappaṭṭa tāyiṉ attoṭarait , teṉ ticaik kaṭavuḷ eṉak kūṟappaṭum yamaṉai veṉṟa civaṉukku aṭutta nilaiyil irukka virumpiṉāṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ eṉṟa poruḷ tarum vakaiyil koḷḷalām. āṉāl, civaṉiṉ, ivveṟṟic ceyal, neṭuñceḻiyaṉ kālattu maturaiyil, palarālum aṟiyappaṭṭiruntatu eṉa, uṟutiyākak koḷḷamuṭiyātu. mēlē kūṟappaṭṭa iru poruḷkaḷumē, kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟa kaṟpaṉaikaḷtām. āṉāl, uraiyāciriyar nacciṉārk kiṉiyariṉ viḷakkam paṭumōcam. avar, mūlac coṟkaḷaiyum, coṟṟoṭar kaḷaiyum tām kūṟa virumpum poruḷukku ēṟṟavāṟu iṭam māṟṟip pōṭṭut, teṉṉavaṉ eṉṟa col irāvaṇaṉaik kuṟippatākak koṇṭu, akattiyar, tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ talaimaip poṟuppiliruntu, irāvaṇaṉai akaṟṟiṉār eṉak kūṟukiṟār. tam kūṟṟukku ātaravāka, tolkāppiya uraiyāciriyar iḷampūraṇar, akattiyar, irāvaṇaṉai icaiyil veṉṟār eṉakkūṟuvatai eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār. (irāvaṇaṉait tamiḻ nāṭṭaiyāḷātapaṭi pōkkiṉa, kiṭṭutaṟkariya valiyiṉaiyuṭaiya, paḻamai mutirnta, akattiyaṉ irāvaṇaṉ teṉticaiyāṇṭamai paṟṟit teṉṉavaṉ eṉṟār, akattiyaṉait, teṉticaiyuyarnta noymmaipōka, iṟaivaṉukkuc cīroppa iruntāṉ, eṉpatu paṟṟik kaṭavuḷ eṉṟār. irāvaṇaṉ āḷutal, pāyirac cūttirattu uraiyāciriyar kūṟiya uraiyāṉum uṇarka." (maturaik kāñci 40 - 42, urai) eṉṟa avar uraiyiṉaik kāṇka. nacciṉārkkiṉiyār tarum ipporuḷ viḷakkam muḻukka muḻukkap piḻaipaṭṭatu. maturaikkāñci, akattiyaṉāraik kuṟippiṭavēyillai.

cilappatikāram, paḻaiya tamiḻp purāṇakkataikaḷam, āriyap purāṇakkataikaḷum, paḻaiya tamiḻp paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum, āriyap paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷum, vēṟu pirittuk kāṇāvāṟu, orē iṉattaic cērnta piḻampu vaṭivamāka vaṭikkappaṭa villaiyāyiṉum, oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu kalakkat toṭaṅkiviṭṭa ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭaic cērnta oru pāṭṭu, intappāṭṭum, tamiḻai mutaṉmutalil tōṟṟu vittu, makkaḷukkuk kaṟpittatākak kūṟum akattiyap purāṇattiṉ mūlattaik kāṇap periya aḷavil tuṇaipuriyavillai . itu, intiraṉ makaṉ cayanta kumaraṉ, vāṉulaka āṭal makaḷ uruppaci, ākiyōrai, akattiyar capittataik kūṟuvatōṭu, akattiyar kuṟitta vēṟu oru kuṟippaiyum tarukiṟatu. atu, māṭalaṉ eṉṟa pārppaṉa yāttirikaṉ kumariyil nīrāṭac celvataṉ muṉ, akattiyar irunta malaiyai valaṅ koṇṭataik kūṟukiṟatu.

"teyva mālvarait tirumuṉi aruḷa

eytiya cāpattu intira ciṟuvaṉoṭu

talaikkōl tāṉattuc cāpam nīṅkiya





malaipparum ciṟappiṉ vāṉavar makaḷir'’

- cilampu : araṅkēṟṟuk kātai :1 - 4





"māmaṟai mutalvaṉ māṭalaṉ eṉpōṉ

mātava muṉivaṉ malaivalam koṇṭu ,

kumariyam peruntuṟai koḷkaiyiṟ paṭintu”

- cilampu : aṭaikkalakkātai : 13-15





aṭutta peruṅkāppiyamākiya maṇimēkalai, mēlē kūṟiya kāppiyamām cilappatikārak kālattaic cērntatākak koḷḷappaṭiṉum, atu, paḻaiya tamiḻilakkiya marapukaḷ ellām, camaskiruta ilakkiya marapukaḷāl, tamiḻilak’ kiyattiliruntu muḻumaiyāka veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭuviṭṭa makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai muṟai, āriya ātikkattiṉ kīḻ muḻumaiyākak koṇṭavarappaṭṭuviṭṭa makkaḷukku iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻp peyarkaḷellām, camaskirutap peyarkaḷāl iṭam koḷḷappaṭṭuviṭṭa kālattu “nikaḻccikaḷaip paṟṟiyē kūṟukiṟatu. āṉāl, intak kāppiyamum, akattiyar paṟṟiya kataikaḷil oru cilavaṟṟaiyē koṇṭuḷatu. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu, nīrvēṭkaiyuṟṟa kāntamaṉ eṉṟa cōḻa maṉṉaṉ vēṇṭa, amara muṉivaṉām akattiyaṉaṭaiya nīrkkuṭam kaviḻat tōṉṟiya kāvirip peṇṇaik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.

"kañca vēṭkaiyiṉ kāntamaṉ vēṇṭa,

amara muṉivaṉ akattiyaṉ taṉātu

karakam kaviḻtta kāvirip pāvai'’.

- maṇimēkalai : patikam. 10-12





skānta purāṇam, inta vēṇṭukōḷ viṭuttatai, intiraṉukku ēṟṟukiṟatu. camaskirutattil kāviri, kāvēri eṉa aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu. kāvēri, kavēraṉ eṉṟa muṉivaṉ makaḷākak kūṟuppaṭṭuḷatu. ikkatai, maṇimēkalaiyil, "tavānīrk kāvirip pāvai taṉ tātai kavēraṉ” (malarvaṉam pukka kātai : 55-56) "kavēra kaṉṉi” (pīṭikai kaṇṭu piṟappu uṇarnta kātai : 62) eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷatu. ikkatai; āknēya purāṇattil kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāka, tiruvāḷar. ṭākṭar. u. vē. cuvāmināta ayyar avarkaḷ kūṟukiṟār.
