(maṇi: 9:52. aṭikkuṟippu kāṇka) maṟṟoṉṟu, akattiyar āṇaiyiṭa, vāṉiṭait toṅkik koṇṭirunta acurarkkuriya kōṭṭaiyai aḻittu, av veṟṟi kuṟittut tōḷil toṭi aṇintu, toṭittōḷ cempiyaṉ eṉum peyar koṇṭa cōḻa maṉṉaṉ oruvaṉ, taṉ talainakaril kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil irupatteṭṭu nāṭkaḷ naṭaipeṟum intiraviḻāviṉ pōtu vantirukkumāṟu, intiraṉai vēṇṭikkoḷḷa, avaṉum ataṟku icaintu vantirunta nikaḻcciyaik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.

"ōṅku uyar malayattu aruntavaṉ uraippat

tūṅkaeyil eṟinta toṭittōḷ cempiyaṉ,

viṇṇavar talaivaṉai vaṇaṅki muṉ niṉṟu

maṇṇakattu eṉ taṉ vāṉpati taṉṉuḷ

mēlōr viḻaiya viḻākkōḷ eṭutta

nālēḻ nāḷiṉum naṉku iṉitu uṟaiku eṉa,

amarar talaivaṉ āṅkatu nērntatu.

- maṇimēkalai : viḻāvaṟai kātai :3-9





itaṉ poruḷ : ōṅki uyarnta malaikku uriya, ariya tavam purinta muṉivaṉ āṇaiyiṭa, vāṉil toṅkik koṇṭirunta kōṭṭaiyai aḻittal, toṭi aṇinta tōḷiṉai uṭaiya cōḻaṉ, vāṉavar. talaivaṉām intiraṉai vaṇaṅki, avaṉ muṉ niṉṟu "maṇṇulakil, eṉakku uriya ciṟanta talainakaril periyavarkaḷ virumpa viḻāk koṇṭāṭum, irupatteṭṭu nāṭkaḷilum vantu naṉku iruppāyāka" eṉa vēṇṭik koḷḷa, tēvar talaivaṉum atu nērntāṉ eṉpatām.

koṇṭāṭa maṟantuviṭṭatāl, talainakaraik kaṭal kōḷām pēraḻivukku uḷḷākkiviṭṭa intiraviḻā vanta varalāṟu itu. appāṭṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil, akattiyar kuṟitta maṟṟoru kuṟippum iṭam peṟṟuḷatu. atu, paracurāmaṉ, kṣattiriya aracarkaḷaiyellām aḻittu varumpōtu, kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattuk kāval teyvam, maṟaintu koḷḷumāṟu āṇaiyiṭa, kāntamaṉ eṉṟa aracaṉum tuyar tīrntuviṭṭatu eṉa akattiyaṉ aṟivikkum varai, talainakaraik kākkumāṟu piṉṉōṉukku āṇaiyiṭṭu, ammuṉivar tavappaḷḷiyil maṟaintu vāḻnta nikaḻcciyaik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.

"maṉmaruṅku aṟutta maḻuvāḷ neṭiyōṉ

taṉmuṉ tōṉṟal takātu oḷi nī eṉak

kaṉṉi ēvaliṉ kānta maṉṉavaṉ....

............................

amara muṉivaṉ akattiyaṉ taṉātu

tuyar nīṅku kiḷaviyiṉ yāṉtōṉ ṟaḷavum

kakantaṉ kāttal”

- maṇimēkalai: ciṟaiceykātai:25-27 maṟṟum 35-36





itaṉ poruḷ: aracar kulattai aḻitta, maḻu eṉṟavāḷ ēntiya neṭiya tirumālām paracurāmaṉ muṉ tōṉṟuvatu takātu, nī maṟaintu koḷ eṉak kaṉṉit teyvam āṇaiyiṭa, kānta maṉṉavaṉ tēvamuṉivaṉām akattiyaṉ kūṟum, tuyar tīrntuviṭṭatu eṉṟa col kēṭṭu, nāṉ varum varai kakantā! kāppāyāka eṉpatām.)

ki.pi. mutalām āyirattāṇṭiṉaic cērnta intak kataikaḷ etilum, akattiyar, teyva āciriyaṉiṭamirunta tamiḻaip peṟṟatu allatu kaṟṟataik kuṟippiṭavillai. ākavē, piṟkālattē vāḻnta akattiyaṉār, mutal tamiḻilakkaṇattai eḻutiya uṇmai nikaḻcci , mutal akattiyar, tamiḻaic civaṉ allatu cuppiramaṇiyariṭamiruntu kaṟṟut teṉṉintiya makkaḷukkuk kaṟṟut tantār eṉṟa purāṇak kataiyāka māṟiyatu. uraiyāciriyarkaḷ perukip paḻaṅkālam kuṟitta purāṇakkataikaḷaik kaṭṭiviṭṭa kipi. iraṇṭām āyirattāṇṭil nikaḻntirukka vēṇṭum. pāyiram eṉappaṭum tolkāppiya muṉṉuraiyil nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvatē, purāṇak kataikaḷil paṭumōcamāṉatu. akattiyaṉār, tammuṭaiya māṇavar, tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyārait tam maṉaivi lōpāmuttiraiyai, vitarppattiliruntu poti malaikku aḻaittuvarumāṟu paṇittār. atē camayam tam maṉaiviyai nāṉku kōl aḷaviṟkuk kuṟaivāka aṇukakkūṭātu eṉṟum eccarittār. lōpā muttiraiyum, tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyum, veḷḷam perukkeṭut tōṭum vaikaiyaik kaṭakka nēriṭṭapōtu, avaḷ veḷḷattāl aṭittuc cellātirukkat tam kaikkōlai nīṭṭi ataip paṟṟik koḷḷumāṟu vōpā muttiraiyiṭam kūṟiṉār. avvāṟē āṟṟaik kaṭakkum pōtu, veḷḷap perukku mikutiyāl kōlil, oru kōl aḷavē iṭai veḷi irukka nēriṭṭatu. avvakaiyil tiraṇatūmākkiṉi āciriyariṉ taṭaiyuttaravai mīṟiviṭṭār. kaṭuñciṉam uṭaiya āciriyar, itaik kēḷviyuṟṟutum, 'nīṅkaḷ iruvarum cuvarkkam pukak kaṭavīr allarāka" eṉac cāpam iṭṭār. atu kēṭṭa māṇavarum "nāṅkaḷ ceyyāta piḻaikku eṅkaḷaic capitta nīyum cuvarkkam pukak kaṭavīr allarāka" eṉa etirccāpam iṭṭār. tolkāppiyam irukka akataktiyam aḻintuviṭṭatai viḷakka ikkatai kūṟappaṭṭatu pōlum.





13. makkaḷ vāḻkkai ki.pi. mutal ainnūṟu āṇṭukaḷ



paḻantamiḻar muṟai iṉṉamum valuvāka naṭaipeṟṟatu



taṅkaḷiṭaiyē pirāmaṇarkaḷ vāḻat toṭaṅkiviṭṭa nilaiyilum, akattiyaṉārum, tolkāppiyaṉārum āriya nākarīkattait tamiḻ nāṭṭil pukutta muyaṟci mēṟkoṇṭuviṭṭa nilaiyilum, makkaḷil perumpakutiyiṉar, āriya nākarīkam, taṅkaḷiṭaiyē iṭam peṟātatu pōlavē vāḻalāyiṉar. paḻaṅkālattil, taṅkaḷ mūtātaiyar vāḻnta atē muṟaiyil vāḻntaṉar; kātal koṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷ, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, nampikkaikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil, muṉṉōrkaḷaik kaṇmūṭip piṉpaṟṟiṉar. atāvatu, avai ciṟitum māṟupaṭavēyillai. kīḻē koṭuttirukkum eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷ, kiṟittuva āṇṭu piṟappataṟku muntiya, eḷiya ciṟṟūr vāḻkkai eṉpatai uṇarntu koḷḷap paṭippavarkku tuṇai niṟkam. cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ kaḻittuc, camaskirutak kaṟpaṉaip pāṭalkaḷ, tamiḻar aṟivai aṭimai koṇṭu, avarkaḷiṉ, aḻakiya iyaṟkaiyōṭiyainta iṉiya pāṭalkaḷai aḻikkat talaippaṭṭapōtu kaiviṭṭuviṭṭa taṅkaḷaic cūḻa uḷḷa eḷiya iyaṟkaiyōṭiyainta iṉiya pāṭalkaḷai aḻikkat talaippaṭṭapōtu kaiviṭṭu viṭṭa taṅkaḷaic cūḻa uḷḷa eḷiya iyaṟkaik kāṭcikaḷiliruntē tam akattūṇṭu talait, tām peṟṟirunta annilaiyaic camaskiruta nākarīkattōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭuviṭṭa annilaiyilum tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷ peṟṟu vantaṉa eṉpataiyum avai uṇarttum.

malai nāṭṭil uṇavu uṟpatti

makkaḷiṉ mukkiyat toḻil, uṇavu uṟpatti. atu pala iṭaṅkaḷil viḷakkappaṭṭuḷatu. “vēṅkai maraṅkaḷ niṟainta, veppam mikka civanta mēṭṭu nilattil, kār kālattup perumaḻaiyāl īrappakkuvappaṭṭa nilattaik, kīḻmaṇ mēlmaṇṇāki naṉku puḻutipaṭumāṟu palacāl uḻutu, vitaittu, muḷaittu iḷampayirāka irukkum pōtu, kāṟṟu uḷ nuḻaiya vaḻi ceyyavum, varuttamiṉṟik kaḷai akaṟṟavum, payir naṉku taḻaippataṟku iṭam tarumāṟu, nilattu iṟukkattai nekiḻvikkavum tāḷi aṭittu, atu ceytataṉ payaṉāy, payir naṉku ceḻittu vaḷarntirukkum pōtu kaḷai akaṟṟiviṭavē ilaikaḷ taḻaittup peruki, aṇmaiyil muṭṭai iṭṭa peṇmayiliṉ aḻakiya tōkai pōlum tōṟṟam vāynta kariya taṇṭukaḷ nīṇṭu, oru cērak katirviṭṭu, katirkaḷiṉ aṭiyilum, nuṉiyilum uḷḷa maṇikaḷ orucēra muṟṟik kāytta varakai aṟukkavum, tiṉaiyaik koyyavum, eḷ kaṟuttu eḷḷiṉaṅkāy muṟṟavum, vaḷamāṉa koṭikaḷil veḷḷavaraik kottu, koyyum nilai eyta, nilattil putaittup puḷippēṟap paṇṇiya matuvaip pul vēynta ciṟiya kuṭicaikaḷil aṉaivarkkum vaḻaṅka, kotikkum naṟu neyyil vaṟukaṭalai tuḷḷa, cōṟu ākki muṭikkum ariya kāṭci oru pāṭṭil viḷakkappaṭṭuḷatu.

"veppuḷ viḷainta vēṅkaic ceñcuval

kārppeyal kalitta perumpāṭṭu īrattup

pūḻi mayaṅkap pala uḻutu, vittip,

palli āṭiya, palkiḷaic cevvik

kaḷaikāl kaḻāliṉ, tōḷtolipu nanti,

peṇmayil puṉiṟṟup peṭai kaṭuppa nīṭik,

karuntāḷ pōki, oruṅku pīḷ virintu,

kīḻum mēlum eñcāmaip palakāyttu,

vālitiṉ viḷainta putuvaraku ariyat

tiṉai koyyāk, kavvai kaṟuppa, avaraik

koḻuṅkoṭi viḷarikkāy kōṭpata mācu,

nilam putaip, paḻuṉiya maṭṭiṉ tēṟal

pulvēy kurampaik kuṭitoṟum pakarntu,

naṟuneyk kaṭalai icaippac cōṟu aṭṭu"

puṟam : 120 : 1-74





malaināṭṭil, uḻutu uṇavup poruṭkaḷai uṟpatti ceyya vēṇṭiya, iṉṟiyamaiyāta oṉṟu aṉṟu. uḻuvatu vēṇṭātē, uṇavup poruḷ kiṭaikkum nilai kīḻvarum , pākkaḷil viḷakkappaṭṭuḷatu. “uḻuvar uḻutu viḷaivikka” vēṇṭāmal, tāmē viḷaiyum uṇavup poruṭkaḷ nāṉku uḷa.. mutalāvatu, ciṉṉañciṟu ilaikaḷaik koṇṭa mūṅkil nel, iraṇṭāvatu, palā, iṉiya cuvaiyuṭaik kaṉikaḷaik kāyttut toṅkaviṭum. mūṉṟāvatu, koḻu koḻu eṉa vaḷarntirukkam, vaḷḷik koṭi, kiḻaṅkukaḷai maṇṇuḷ viṭṭirukkum; nāṉkāvatu, aḻakiya niṟam vāynta, kuraṅkukaḷ pāya, malaikaḷ, taṉṉiṭai kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum aṭaikaḷiliruntu tēṉaic coriyum” "uḻavar uḻātaṉa nāṉku payaṉ uṭaittē;

oṉṟē, ciṟiyilai vetiriṉ nelviḷai yummē;

iraṇṭē tīñcuvaip palaviṉ paḻam ūḻk kummē;

mūṉṟē, koḻuṅkoṭi vaḷḷik kiḻaṅku vīḻkkummē;

nāṉkē, aṇiniṟa ōri pāytaliṉ mītaḻintu

tiṇineṭum kuṉṟam tēṉ cori yummē”..

puṟam : 109 : 3-8





"kiḷaikaḷ tōṟum, malai mukaṭukaḷ tōṟum tēṉaṭaikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭut toṅka, palā mutalām maraṅkaḷil paḻaṅkaḷ kulaikulaiyākat toṅka, malaikaḷiliruntu aruvikaḷ mālai pōl uruṇṭōṭi vara, kollaikaḷ . tōṟum varaku, cāmai, tiṉaipōlum patiṉāṟuvakaik kūlaṅkaḷ perukiviḷaiya, immalaināṭu, ekkālattum peruvaḷam uṭaiyatu eṉa, ataṉkaṇ pallāṇṭu vāḻntu, ataip pirintu pōka nērntapōtu viyantu pārāṭṭum perumaiyuṭaiyatu”..

“piracam tūṅkap, perumpaḻam tuṇara

varai veḷ ḷaruvi, mālaiyiṉ iḻitarak,

kūlam ellām pulampuka, nāḷum

mallaṟṟu amma immalaikeḻu veṟpueṉap





pirintōr iraṅkum peruṅkal nāṭu"

- naṟṟiṇai : 93: 1-5





vēṭaṉ vāḻkkai

malai nāṭṭu vaḻiyil, iruḷil varuvatāl nērum iṭaiyūṟukaḷukku āḷākum vakaiyil iravil vāraṟka eṉṟu, ōriḷam peṇ, taṉ kātalaṉukku eṭuttuk kūṟum aṟivuraiyiṉai niṉaivūṭṭum vakaiyil, vēṭaṉ oruvaṉ vāḻkkaiyil iṭam peṟum palvēṟu nikaḻccikaḷai, oru pulavar viḷakkiyuḷḷār. "cilirttu niṟkum mayirkaḷ aṭaṅkiya kaḻuttiṉai uṭaiya āṇpaṉṟi, uṇaviṉmai yāl tōlāyvaṟṟi iḷaittuviṭṭa peṇ paṉṟiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu, muṟṟa vaḷarntu tiṉaik katirkaḷai aḷaviṉṟit - tiṉṟu aḻittuviṭṭatu kaṇṭa kāṉavaṉ, malaivaḻiyil, cellutaṟku ariya puḻai arukē patuṅkiyiruntu, ampu. eytu koṉṟa, veḷḷiya kompukaḷaik koṇṭa anta āṇ paṉṟiyai aṇi ceytu koṇṭa kariya kūntalai uṭaiya avaṉ maṉaivi, aṟuttut, taṉ cuṟṟattār vīṭukaḷukkellām ceṉṟu pakuttuk koṭukkum uyarnta malai nāṭṭukku uriya talaivaṉē kaṭuñciṉam koṇṭa kaḷiṟṟiyāṉai, puliyiṉ varukaiyai etirnōkkik kāttu niṟkum iravil, nī iṅku varutalai, nī añcukiṉṟāyallai; āṉāl, nāṉ peritum añcukiṉṟēṉ; ākavē, pāmpu aṭaṅkiyirukkum, īrampaṭṭa puṟṟaik karaṭik kūṭṭam kārmēkam pōlac cūḻntu koṇṭu, paṟittu eṭukkum, malaiyakattuc ciṟuvaḻiyil iṉi vārātē"

"piṇarccuval paṉṟi, tōlmulaip piṇavoṭu

kaṇaikkāl ēṉal kaimmikak kavartaliṉ,

kallatar aruppuḻai alkik, kāṉavaṉ

villil tanta veṇkōṭṭu ēṟṟaip

puṉaiyirum katuppiṉ maṉaiyōḷ, toṇṭik

kuṭimuṟai pakukkum neṭumalai nāṭa!

uravucciṉa vēḻam, uṟupuli pārkkum





iraviṉ añcāy; añcuval; araviṉ

īraḷaip puṟṟam kāreṉa muṟṟi,

iraitēr eṇku iṉam akaḻum

varaicēr ciṟuneṟi, vārā tīmē;”

- naṟṟiṇai : 336





āṟṟuppaḷḷattākkil

āṟṟup paḷḷattākku nilattil eḻunta oru pāṭal:

"poykaiyiṉ mīṉ tērntuṇṭa nāraikaḷ, neṟkatirppu pōril ceṉṟu uṟaṅkum. neytal varumaḷavu vaḷattāl aḻaku peṟṟa vayalil, nel aṟuvaṭai ceyyum uḻavar, mukai aviḻntu malarnta itaḻkaḷaiyuṭaiya āmpaliṉ akaṉṟa ilaiyālāṉa toṉṉaiyil, matuvuṇṭu, teḷinta kaṭalalai eḻuppum cīrāṉa olikaḷiṉ tāḷattiṟku ēṟpa āṭi makiḻum, naṉcey vaḷamikka nāṭṭiṟku uriya vēntē paḻam : uṇṇuvāṉ virumpi, ākāyattil uyarap paṟantu, malaik kukaikaḷ etirolikkap pērōli eḻuppiyavāṟē ceṉṟu, āṅkuḷḷa paḻamaraṅkaḷ, paruvam kaḻintu pōkak, kāyppu māṟiviṭṭatu kaṇṭu ēmāntu, varunti, vaṟitē mīḷum paṟavakaiḷaip pōla, varuvārkku varaiyātu vaḻaṅkuvaṉ eṉṟa uṉ pukaḻturatta vantu, uṉ pukaḻpāṭum nāṉ, vaṟitē mīḷak kaṭavaṉō ?”

"poykai nārai, pōrvil cēkkum

neytalam kaḻaṉi nellari toḻuvar

kūmpuviṭu meṉpiṇi aviḻnta āmpal

akalaṭai ariyal māntit teṇkaṭal

paṭutirai iṉcīrp pāṇi tūṅkum

meṉpula vaippiṉ naṉṉāṭṭup poruṉa!

palkaṉi nacaii, alku vicumpu ukantu,

perumalai viṭarakam cilampa muṉṉip,

paḻaṉuṭaip perumaram tīrnteṉak, kaiyaṟṟup

peṟātu peyarum puḷḷiṉam pōla,

niṉ nacaitara vantu, niṉ icainuval paricilēṉ





vaṟuviyēṉ peyarkō?"

(puṟam : 209 :1-12)





oru toḻil naṭaipeṟum nilattiṉ potuvāṉa kuṟikkōḷkaḷum, mikamika eḷiya nikaḻccikaḷum, tamiḻnāṭṭup pulavarkaḷiṭamiruntu, appulavarkaḷ, piṟkālac camaskiruta ilakkiyak kaṟpaṉaip pāṭalkaḷiṉ celvāk kiṉ aḻuttamāṉa piṭiyuḷ cikkik koḷvataṟku muṉṉar, naṉmika iṉiya pāṭalkaḷaip peṟṟiruntaṉa. ivvāṟu pāṭukiṟatu oru pāṭṭu:

"maṭai aṭaittirunta pallāṟṟāṉum māṇpuṟṟa kuḷattu nīr, maṭaitiṟantuviṭṭa vaḻi, akkuḷattiṉiṉṟum, annīrōṭu puṟam pōntu, kālvāyai aṭaintu, ceṉṟu tirumpiya, periya kompiṉaiyuṭaiya vāḷaimīṉ, akkālvāyiliruntu, cēṟupaṭṭuk kiṭakkum vayaluḷ pukuntu ōṭi, āṅku uḻutukoṇṭirukkum erumaik kaṭākkaḷiṉ kāṟcēṟupaṭṭa puḷḷikaḷaiyuṭaiya mēṟpuṟattōṭu, nilattaic cēṟupaṭa māṟimāṟi uḻum uḻavarkaḷiṉ kaik kōlukkum añcātu cerukkuṟṟu, cēṟṟiṉ mēl varappu ōramākavē ōṭi, mēṟkoṇṭu pōkamāṭṭātu taṭaiyuṟṟup pōy, avvarap paṭiyilēyē puraḷat toṭaṅkum.

"māṇ peruṅkuḷam maṭainīr viṭṭeṉak ,

kāl aṇaintu etiriya kaṇaikkōṭṭu vāḷai,

aḷḷal am kaḻaṉi uḷvāy ōṭip

pakaṭucēṟu utaitta puḷḷiveṇ puṟattuc

ceñcāl uḻavar kōlpuṭai matarip

paiṅkāl ceṟuviṉ aṇaimutal puraḷum

naṟṟiṇai : 340 8-8





āṟṟup paḷḷattākku vāḻkkaimuṟai paṟṟiya iṉṉoru ceyyuḷ itō: "cenneruppu koḻuntu viṭṭu erivatu pōṉṟa centāmarai malarkaḷiṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē, katir muṟṟiya cennelliṉ tāḷai arintu ari ariyākap pōṭṭup paṇi muṭinta uḻavar, taṅkaḷukkuk kuṭaṅkaḷaik koṇṭuvarum vaṇṭi, cēṟṟil āḻntuviṭiṉ, atupōkkac ciṟanta karumputtaṭikaḷai aṭukki iṭai yeṭukkum, pāyum puṉalāl vaḷammikka ūriṉ talaivaṉē! neṟporikaḷ pōlum, puṉkampū malarkaḷ utirntu kiṭakkum akaṉṟa nīrttuṟaikaḷ aḻaku peṟa , oḷivīcum neṟṟiyum, nalmaṇam mikka malarkaḷ cūṭiya, kāṇpataṟku iṉimai tarum tiraṇṭa kūntalum, māvaṭukkaḷ pōlum kaṇkaḷum, mārpil acaiyum muttuc caramum, ivaṟṟāl pūm nuṇṇiya aḻakum uṭaiyāḷōr iḷam parattaiyoṭu, iṭaiyaṟātu ōṭikkoṇṭē irukkum puṉalil, nēṟṟu viḷaiyāṭi makiḻntaṉai eṉṟu ūrār palarum kūṟuvar. ākavē, uṟutiyāka, nī, nāṇam iḻantavaṉē”.

"eri acaintaṉṉai tāmarai iṭaiyiṭai

arintukāl kuvitta cennel liṉaiñar,

kaḷ koṇṭu maṟukum cākāṭu aḷaṟṟuriṉ,

āykarumpu aṭukkam pāypuṉal ūra:

periya, nāṇilai maṉṟa; pori eṉap

puṉku aviḻ akaṉṟuṟaip poliya, oṉtutal,

naṟumalark kāṇvarum kuṟumpal kūntal,

māḻainōkkiṉ, kāḻ iyal vaṉamulai

eḵkuṭai eḻilnalattu orutti yoṭu, nerunai

vaikupuṉal ayarntaṉai”.

akam : 116 : 1-10





kaṭaṟkarai nilapparappil

kaṭaṟkarai nilapparappām neytal nilattiṉ mukkiyat toḻil, mīṉpiṭittal, attoḻil, pāṭal puṉaiyum tam pulamaikku ēlā iḻivuṭaip poruḷām eṉṟu pulavar karuta villai , ‘'katiriṭṭu muṟukkit tiruttamākac ceyyappaṭṭa valiya kayiṟṟāl piṉṉappaṭṭa “ periya periya mīṉpiṭi valaikaḷai, iṭipōl muḻaṅkum alaikaḷ ōyāk kaṭalil irumporuṭṭu, avvalaikaḷ niṟaiya ēṟṟappaṭṭa tōṇiyaip pākarkaḷāl piṇittuk koṇṭu cellappaṭum. aṭakkutaṟku curiya kaḷiṟṟaip pōlap, paratavar celuttuvar malarkaḷ utirntu kiṭakkum putumaṇal paranta kāṉaṟcōlaiyil, puṉṉai, taṉ nuṇṇiya makarantap poṭikaḷai; ōyātu vīcik koṇṭēyirukkum. kīḻk kāṟṟu vantu mōtuntōṟum, nāraikaḷiṉ veṇṇiṟa mutukil tūvit tūrkkum teḷinta kaṭaṟkaraikkaṇ niṟkum kaṇṭal maraṅkaḷai vēliyākak koṇṭa ūr"

"vaṭikkatir tiritta vaṉñāṇ peruvalai

iṭikkural puṇarip pauvattu iṭumār,

niṟaiyap peyta ampi, kāḻōr

ciṟaiyarum kaḷiṟṟiṉ, paratavar oyyum

pōtaviḻ, putumaṇal kāṉal puṉṉainuṇ tātu

koṇṭal acaivaḷi tūkku toṟum, kurukiṉ

veṇpuṟam ōciya vārkkam, teṇkaṭal kaṇṭal vēliya ur".

- naṟṟiṇai : 74 : 1-4 : 6-10





piṉvarum aḻakiya ceyyuḷil, oru mīṉavap peṇ taṉ uṟaviṉar, viraintu vīṭu tirumpamāṭṭār eṉpataik kūṟit, taṉ kātalaṉait taṉ maṉaiyil iravu taṅkic cellumāṟu aḻaikkiṟāḷ. "kīḻkkaṭaliliruntu eḻuntu, nalla cenniṟak katirkaḷaip parappip pakaṟpoḻutai oḷimayamākkiviṭṭa ñāyiṟu mēṟku malaiyil maṟaintuviṭum, tuṉpattait tūtu pōl muṉpōkkip piṉṉar vantu taṅkiviṭṭa tuyartaru mālaip poḻutai, oḷivīcum vaḷai aṇinta makaḷir tattam māḷikaikaḷil, etirkoṇṭu varavēṟka, mīṉ koḻuppai urukki eṭutta neyvārttu ēṟṟiya oḷiviḷakkukaḷiṉ pēroḷi vīcum nīla niṟakkaṭalil eḻum alaikaḷ mōtum karaiyiṭattē uḷḷa, āravārap pēroli eppōtum olittuk koṇṭē irukkum em pākkattil iṉṟu iruntu, emmaṉaiyil emmōṭu taṅkiyiruppiṉ, uṉakku ētēṉum kuṟapaiāṭu uḷatāmō? civanta nūlāl, vaḷaittu vaḷaittu muṭiyiṭṭuc ceyta aḻakiya valai kēṭuṟṟup pōmāṟu aṟuttuk koṇṭu ōṭiviṭṭa, etirppaṭum etaiyum kollavalla cuṟāmīṉait tam vallamaiyellām kāṭṭi, akappaṭuttik koḷḷātu em cuṟṟattār vaṟitē varuvār allar. ākavē taṅkic ceṉmō"

"kuṇakaṭal ivarntu kurūukkatir parappip

pakalkeḻu celvaṉ kuṭamalai maṟaiyap

pulampuvantu iṟutta puṉkaṇ mālai

ilaṅkuvaḷai makaḷir viyaṉnakar ayara,

mīṉniṇam tokutta ūṉ ney oṇcuṭar,

nīlniṟap parappil tayaṅkutirai utaippak

karaicērpu irunta kaleṉ pākkattu

iṉṟu nī ivaṇai āki, emmoṭu

taṅkiṉ evaṉō? teyya; ceṅkāl

koṭumuṭi avvalai pariyap pōkiya

kōṭ cuṟāk kuṟitta muṉpoṭu

vēṭṭam vāyātu emar vā ralarē”

naṟṟiṇai : 215





neytal nilattu makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai vāyppukaḷ, oru pāṭṭil viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. "nellai aṟuvaṭai ceyyum periya uḻavar, ceṅkatir parappik kāyum ñāyiṟṟiṉ veyil veppam tāṅkamāṭṭāta pōtu, teḷinta kaṭal nīril pāyntu kaḷai tīrvar. tiṇṇiya mīṉpiṭi paṭakām timilai uṭaiya valiya paratavar, uṇṭārkku uram ūṭṭum matuvuṇṭu, iṉiya kuravaik kūttiṉ tāḷattiṟku ēṟpa āṭit tiḷaippar. kaṭal alaikaḷ tūvum tūvalāl taḻaittu vaḷarntu puṉṉaiyiṉ tēṉ niṟainta malarkaḷāl āṉa mālai aṇinta āṭavar, oḷivīcum vaḷai aṇinta makaḷirkku nīrāṭalil valakkai koṭuttu utavuvar, vaṇṭukaḷ vantu moykk malarnta, kuḷirnta naṟumaṇamē nāṟik koṇṭirukkum kāṉalil taḻaittirukkum kaṭal muḷḷi mālai aṇinta vaḷai aṇinta makaḷir, vāṉaḷāva vaḷarntirukkum paṉaiyiṉ nuṅkiṉ iṉiya nīrum, potivuṟa vaḷarntirukkum karumpiṉ 225makkaḷ vāḻkkai .... cāṟum, maṇal mēṭṭil niṟkum teṉṉaiyiṉ iṉiya iḷanīrum ākiya mūṉṟu nīraiyum kalantu kuṭittuviṭṭu, maḻainīr, āṟṟunīr, ūṟṟu nīr eṉṟa munnīrām kaṭal nīril pāyntu āṭuvar".

"nel ariyum iruntoḻuvar,

ceññāyiṟṟu veyil muṉaiyiṉ,

teṇkaṭal tirai micaip pāyuntu,

tiṇtimil vaṉ paratavar

veppuṭaiya maṭṭum uṇṭu

taṇkuravaic cīrtūṅkuntu!

tūval kalitta tēmpāy puṉṉai

mel iṇark kaṇṇi milainta maintar,

elvaḷai makaḷir talaikkai tarūuntu,

vaṇṭupaṭa malarnta taṇnaṟum kāṉal

muṇṭakak kōtai oṇtoṭi makaḷir

irumpaṉaiyiṉ kuṟumpainīrum,

pūṅkarumpiṉ tīñcāṟum,

oṅku maṇal kuvavuttāḻait

tīnīrōṭu uṭaṉ virāay

munnīr uṇṭu. munnīr pāyum"

-puṟam : 24:1-16





("munnīr uṇṭu - (paṉai nuṅkiṉ nīr, karumpiṉ cāṟu, veṅkiṉ iḷanīr - ) munnīr (āṟṟunīr, ūṟṟunīr, maḻai nīr) pāyum" eṉṟa variyil aḻakiya collōviyam amaintiruppatu kāṇka)

talaivaṉai, ōr iravu taṅkit talaivikku āṟutal aḷikkumāṟu tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttum oru ceyyuḷ, neytal nilattu vāḻkkai nilai, vāḻkkai muṟaikaḷ paṟṟiya maṟṟoru kāṭciyait tarukiṟatu. mīṉ uṇṇum paciya kālkaḷai uṭaiya veṇṇiṟak kokkuk kūṭṭam cevvēḷ murukaṉ mārpil kiṭantu oḷiviṭum veṇ muttāram pōla, cevvāṉattil eḻuntu varicaiyāka paṟantu. pōmāṟu, pakaṟpoḻutai mella mellak kaḻittu viṭṭa ñāyiṟu, mēṟku malaiyil ceṉṟu maṟaintu viṭṭāṉ. mikka nāṇattaiyum melliya cāyalaiyum uṭaiya ciṟiyavaḷākiya ivaḷō, taṉ ciṟanta aḻaku keṭumāṟu, uṉ pirivukku ēṅki, aḻakiya kuḷirnta kaṇkalaṅka ōyātu aḻat toṭaṅki viṭṭāḷ. talaiva! ataṉālum, uppaṅkaḻiyil uḷḷa cuṟā mīṉ kuttiyatāl puṇṇuṟṟa kālkaḷaiyuṭaiya uṉ kōvēṟu kaḻutai, nīr niṟaintu kaṟuttut tōṉṟum nīṇṭa kaḻiyaik kaṭakkamāṭṭātu melintu viṭṭatu. ātalālum, valiya villēnti uṉ uṭaṉ varum vīrarkaḷōṭu, ivviravil cellātu, iḷaippāṟi, paṉaimaraṅkaḷ ōṅki vaḷarntirukkum veṇmaṇal parantu kiṭakkum tōṭṭaṅ kaḷil, taṉ tuṇaiyai aḻaippāṉ vēṇṭi, aṉṟil akavum avviṭattil, ciṉṉam ciṟu pūṅkottuk kaḷaiyuṭaiya neytal vaḷarntukiṭakkum, uppaṅkaḻi cūḻnta eṅkaḷ nāṭṭiṉ kaṇ, nī taṅkic celvāyāka; avvāṟu taṅkic celvatāl varuvatoru kēṭu ētēṉum uṇṭō?”

”neṭuvēḷ mārpiṉ āram pōlac ,

cevvāy vāṉam tīṇṭi mīṉ aruntum

paiṅkāl kokkiṉ niṟaipaṟai ukappa

ellai paipayak kaḻippik, kuṭavayiṉ

kal cērn taṉṟē, palkatir ñāyiṟu;

matareḻil maḻaikkaṇ kaluḻa, ivaḷē

perunāṇ aṇitta ciṟumeṉ cāyal

māṇnalam citaiya, ēṅki āṉātu.

aḻaltoṭaṅ kiṉaḷē; perum; ataṉāl

kaḻiccuṟā eṟinta puṇtāḷ attiri

neṭunīr iruṅkaḻip parimelintu acaii,

valvil iḷaiyaroṭu ellic cellātu

cērntaṉai celiṉē, citaikuvatu uṇṭō ?

peṇṇai ōṅkiya veṇmaṇal paṭappai

aṉṟil akvum āṅkaṇ,

ciṟukural neytal emperuṅkaḻi nāṭē”

akam : 120





maṇal paranta pālaiyil vaṟaṇṭa pālaiyil, vāḻkkai nilai mikavum kaṭiṉamām."tūya veḷḷai āṭaiyai virittu viṭṭāṟpōl tōṉṟumāṟu veyil virintu kāyum, kōṭaip paruvaram nīṇṭa malaiccāralil, koṭiya paciyuṭaiya cennāy, vāṭiya maraiyāvaik koṉṟu vēṇṭu maṭṭum tiṉṟu, viṭṭoḻitta eñciya iṟaicci neṭuntolaivil uḷḷa - vēṟṟu nāṭṭiliruntu, kaṭattaṟku ariya appālaiyaik kaṭantu cellum vaḻip pōvōrkku uṇṇum uṇavākum koṭumai mikka, veppam mikunta ariya vaḻi"

"mukil virittaṉa veyilavir uruppiṉ

eṉṟūḻ nīṭiya kuṉṟattuk kavāaṉ,

oypacic cennāy, uyaṅkumarai tolaicci

ārīntaṉa oḻinta miccil, cēynāṭṭu

arumcuram celvōrkku valci ākum

vemmai āriṭai

- naṟṟiṇai : 43 : 1-6





pālai, taṉakkē uriya iṉiya aḻakiya kāṭcikaḷaip peṟāmalillai. "vaḷainta ciṟakiṉai uṭaiya paṟavakaiḷiṉ uḷḷaṅkāl cuvaṭukaḷ varicai varicaiyākap poruntiya, nīrvaṟṟiya iṭaṅkaḷ tōṟum melliya nuṇmaṇal paṭintu kiṭakka, melleṉa vīcum vāṭaikkāṟṟu vaṟaṇṭu. mōtiyatāl, karumpiṉ veṇṇiṟap pū, putal tōṟum oru cēra virintu, aracaṉukku vīcappaṭum kavari pōl āṭi aḻaku ceyya, koṇṭal koṇṭalāṉa mēkaṅkaḷ nīṅkic celvatāl, māṟi māṟik kaṇ viḻittuk kāṭṭuvatupōl, ñāyiṟu tōṉṟit tōṉṟi maṟaiya, pakaṟkālam kaḻiya, mālaip poḻutoṭu irākkālam vantu cērtalum, paṉi viḻuntu kāl koḷḷum".

"koṭum ciṟai

uḷḷaṭi poṟitta variyuṭait talaiya

nīr aḻi maruṅkiṉ īrayir tōṉṟa





vaḷarāvāṭai uḷarpunaṉi tīṇṭaliṉ,

vēḻa veṇpū virivāṉa palavuṭaṉ

vēntuvīcu kavariyiṉ pūmputal aṇiya,

maḻaikaḻi vicumpiṉ māṟi ñāyiṟu

viḻittu imaippatupōl viḷaṅkupu aṟaiya

ellai pōkiya poḻutiṉ eluṟap

paṉikkāl koṇṭa paiyuḷ yāmam”.

- naṟṟiṇai : 241 : 1-10





eṉṟālum, makkaḷ peruvāriyāka, ataik kaṭantu cella vēṇṭiyiruntum, pālai, peritum , pāḻuṟṟē iruntatu. ‘'pālai vaḻiyaik kaṭantu cellum makkaḷ, aṟuttuppōṭṭa piraṇṭaik koṭi, iṭiyāl tākkuṇṭu citaṟuṇṭa paciya pāmpiṉ tuṇṭukaḷ pōla vaḻiyarukē vaṟitē vīḻntu kiṭakkum appālaik kāṭṭil, uppu vaṇikar kūṭṭam viṭṭup pōṉa kal aṭuppil, valiya villaiyuṭaiya āṟalai kaḷvar, ūṉai, maṇam uṇṭākap puḻukki uṇpar”,

“āṟucel mākkaḷ aṟutta piraṇṭai

ēṟupeṟu pāmpiṉ paintuṇi kaṭuppa

neṟi ayal tiraṅkum attam, veṟikoḷa

umaṇcāttu iṟanta oḻikal aṭuppiṉ

nōṉcilai maḻavar ūṉ puḻukku ayarum”.

akam : 179 : 5-9.





āṭumāṭukaḷiṉ mēypulamām mullai nilam

vāḻvataṟku naṉmika iṉiya iṭam mullai . "maḻai kāl iṟaṅkip peyyum maḻaip paruvattup, perumaḻai peytu ōynta kaṭaici nāḷaṉṟu, paṉainār koṇṭu pala kāliṭṭup piṉṉiya melliya uṟi, pālpāṉaiyōṭu oru kaiyil toṅka, tīkkaṭaikōl mutalām karuvikaḷai oru cēra iṭṭuc curukkiya tōl paiyaiyum, maḻaikku maṟaippākum paṉai ōlaiyālāṉa kuṭaiyaiyum mutukil kaṭṭik koṇṭu, pālvilai kūṟi viṟṟuc cellum iṭaiyaṉ, mēypulattil, ciṟuciṟu tuḷikaḷāka ōyātu viḻuntu koṇṭē irukkum maḻaiccāral, taṉ uṭaliṉ oru puṟattai naṉaittuk koṇṭiruppatum, poruṭpaṭuttāmal, kaikkōlai nilattil ūṉṟi, oru kālai ataṉ mēl vaittu, ōyvāṉa nilaiyil niṉṟavāṟē , itaḻkaḷaik kuvittu avaṉ eḻuppum vīḷai oliyaik kēṭkam āṭukaḷ, piṟarkkuriya pulam ceṉṟu mēyntuviṭāmal, avvolikku mayaṅki, avaṉaikcūḻa aṭaṅki niṟkum ammullai nilattil, iravu neṭum poḻutu āyiṉum viruntiṉar vantuviṭṭāl makiḻntu varavēṟpavaḷum, kaṇavaṉ kūṟiya coṟpiḻaiyātu il iruntu nallaṟam āṟṟum kaṟpuṭaiyāḷum, melliya cāyalum iḷamai nalam māṟā aḻakuṭaiyāḷum ākiya illattaraci vāḻkiṉṟa vīṭu uḷatu."

"vāṉ iṭuppu corinta vayaṅkupeyal kaṭaināḷ

pāṇi koṇṭa palkāl mel uṟi,

ñelikōl kalappai ataḷoṭu curukkip

paṟippuṟattu iṭṭa pāl noṭai iṭaiyaṉ,

nuṇpal tuvalai oru tiṟam naṉaippat

taṇṭukāla vaitta oṭuṅkunilai, maṭiviḷi

ciṟutalait toḻuti ēmārttu alkum

puṟaviṉatuvē, poyyā yāṇar

allil āyiṉam, viruntuvariṉ uvakkum

mullai cāṉṟa kaṟpiṉ

melliyal kuṟumakaḷ uṟaiviṉ ūrē."

naṟṟiṇai : 142





mullaiyil mikavum iṉpam tarum paruvam iḷavēṉil, "paruvavaravāl ilaikaḷellām paḻuttu utirntu pōṉa piṭavamarattuk kiḷaikaḷellām arumpalarntu niṟaintu viṭṭaṉa; putalkaḷ tōṟum paṭarntu kiṭakkum mullaik koṭikaḷ pūttukkuluṅkiṉa ; koṉṟai maraṅkaḷ, poṉ pōlum malarkaḷai īṉṟaṉa. kāyāviṉ ciṟuciṟu kiḷaikaḷil nīlamaṇi pōlum" paṉmalarkaḷ niṟaintu viṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟkellām kāraṇamāṉa kārkālam toṭaṅki viṭṭatu. peṇmāṉ, maruṇṭu maruṇṭu viḻikkum aḻakiya kaṇkaḷai uṭaiyavāya taṉ kuṭṭikaḷōṭu kaḷarnilam nōkki ōṭi viṭavē, avaṟṟiṉpāl koṇṭa āruākkātal neñcōṭu, avaṟṟait tēṭi alaiyum āṇmāṉ atō viraikiṟatu; kāṇpāyāka.'’

"ilaiyil piṭavam īrmalar arumpap,

putal ivar taḷavam pūṅkoṭi aviḻap

poṉ eṉak koṉṟai malara, maṇieṉap

paṉmalark kāyā kuṟuñciṉai kañalak,

kār toṭaṅ kiṉṟē kālai......

........................

kaḻip peyar kaḷaril pōkiya maṭamāṉ

viḻikkaṇ pētaiyoṭu iṉaṉ iruntu ōṭak

kāmar neñcamoṭu akalāt

tēṭūu niṉṟa iralai ēṟē”

- naṟṟiṇai : 1 - 15 : 6 - 10





kēṭka iṉippaṉa, mullaikkāṭṭu olikaḷ, “tēṉaṭaikaḷ kaṭṭappeṟṟirukkum neṭiya muṭikaḷaiyuṭaiya malaiccāralil kuvinta ilaikaḷai neruṅkak koṇṭa mucuṇṭaiyil, vāṉattil viḷaṅkum mīṉ kūṭṭam pōlak 'kāṭci tarumāṟu, veṇṇiṟap pūkkaḷ pūttuk kuluṅkum naḷḷiraviṉ naṭuyāmattil, āṭṭuk kuṭṭikaḷai, maḻai maṟaikkum ōlaip pāyai mutukil koṇṭavāṟē, oru cērakkoṇṭu kāttukkiṭakkum iṭaiyaṉ, iṉiya maṇam kamaḻum mullai ‘ malarkaḷait tōṉṟip pūkkaḷōṭu, vaṇṭukaḷ vantu moykkumāṟu toṭutta mālaiyai, atiṉiṉṟum nīrt tuḷikaḷ ōḻuka, talaiyil aṇintu koṇṭu, kuḷir pōkka, eḻuppiya ciṟu tīyil taṉ uḷḷaṅkaikaḷaik kāya vaittukkoṇṭē, āṭṭu maṟikaḷai viraṭṭuvāṉ vēṇṭi eḻuppum nīṇṭa oli, tiṉaippuṉattai aḻikkavarum ciṟiya kaṇkaḷai uṭaiya paṉṟiyiṉ peruṅkūṭṭattai viraṭṭuvāṉ vēṇṭi, attiṉaip puṉattaik kāttukiṭappār, avai varuvatu aṟintu ūtum, periya kompiṉ ōcaiyoṭu kalantu vantu olikkum mullaikkāṭṭiṉ iṭaiyē uḷḷa nāṭu"

"tēmpaṭu cimayap pāṅkarp pampiya

kuvaiyilai mucuṇṭai veṇpūk kuḻaiya

vāṉ eṉap pūtta pāṉāḷ kaṅkul,

maṟitturūut tokutta paruppuṟa iṭaiyaṉ

taṇkamaḻ mullai, tōṉṟiyoṭu viraii

vaṇṭupaṭat toṭutta nīrvār kaṇṇiyaṉ,

aitupaṭu koḷḷi aṅkai kāyak

kuṟunari uḷampum kāriruḷ neṭu viḷi,

ciṟutāṉi paṉṟip perunirai kaṭiya

mutaippuṉam kāvalar niṉaittiruntu ūtum

karuṅkōṭṭu ōcaiyoṭu oruṅku vantu icaikkum".

akam : 94 :1 - 11





"vaṭṭavaṭṭak kaṇkaḷaiyum kūriya vāyalakaiyum uṭaiya peṇ kākkai, iḷamaiyāl naṭuṅkum iṟakukaḷaiyuṭaiya taṉ kuñcukaḷai aṉaittavāṟē taṉ iṉak kākkaikaḷaiyum kūvi aḻaittu, kuṟukiya kālkaḷai naṭṭukkaṭṭap peṟṟu, uṇavup poruṭkaḷai niṟaiyac cērttu vaittirukkum maṉaikaḷiṉ muṉpuṟattil iṭṭuvaittirukkum, karuṇaik kiḻaṅkiṉ porikkaṟi yoṭu kalanta, cennel ariciyālāṉa veṇcōṟṟut tiraḷait teyvaṅkaḷukku iṭum paliyoṭu kavarntu uṇṇak kūṭiyirukkum"

"koṭuṅkaṇ kākkaik kūrvāyppēṭai,

oruṅkuciṟaip piḷḷai taḻīik kiḷai payirntu

karuṅkaṇ karuṇaic cennel veṇcōṟu

kuruṭaip paliyoṭu kavariya, kuṟuṅkāl

kūṟuṭai naṉmaṉai kuḻuviṉa irukkum"

- naṟṟiṇai : 367 51 - 5





potumakkaḷiṉ ivvāḻkkai viḷakkam vaṟumaiyil vāṭum oru vīṭṭiṉ paṭappiṭippōṭu muṭiyum. cōṟu ākkutal, aṟavē maṟantu pōṉamaiyāl, tēyvuṟātu kaṭṭiyatu kaṭṭiyapaṭiyē kōṭu uyarntu niṟkum aṭuppil, kāḷāṉkaḷ pūttuk kiṭakka, uṭalai vāṭṭum paciyāl varuntip, pāl vaṟaṇṭu pōkavē, tōl melintu, pāl varum tuḷaiyum tūrntu payaṉaṟṟup pōṉa mulaiyil vāy vaittuc cuvaikkuntōṟum, pāl vārāmai kaṇṭu aḻat toṭaṅkiviṭum taṉ makaḷiṉ mukattaik kāṇumtoṟum nīrāl niṟaintuviṭum kaṇkaḷākiviṭum maṉaiyōḷ'’.

"āṭunaṉi maṟanta kōṭuuyar aṭuppiṉ

āmpi pūppat tēmpupaci uḻavāp ,

pāal iṇmaiyiṉ tōlōṭu tiraṅki

illi tūrnta pollā vaṟumulai

cuvaittoṟu aḻuum taṉ makattumukam nōkki

nīroṭu niṟainta īritaḻ maḻaikkaṇeṉ

maṉaiyōḷ"

- puṟam : 164: 1-7





ciṟutoḻilkaḷ

ikkāla aḷavil, ciṟṟūrkaḷil iṉṟu kāṇappaṭum attaṉait toḻilkaḷum ceyalpaṭa vēṇṭiya aḷavukku, vāḻkkaittaram vekuvāka muṉṉēṟiviṭṭatu. it toḻil kaḷ, pulavarkaḷukku nalla uvamaikaḷāka utavalāyiṉa, ‘irum paip payaṉpaṭuttip pala karuvikaḷai vaṭittuk koṭukkum kollaṉ ulaikkaḷattil, kāycciya irumpai vicaittu aṭik kum cammaṭṭiyiṉ aṭikaḷait tāṅki urukkulaintu pōkātu niṟkum paṭṭaṭaik kallaip pakaivarāl aḻivuṟṟup pōkā oru peru vīraṉukku uvamai kūṟiyuḷḷār oru pulavar”.

“irumpupayaṉ paṭukkum karuṅkaik kollaṉ

vicaittu eṟi kūṭamoṭu porūum

ulaikkal aṉṉa vallā ḷaṉṉē”

-puṟam : 170 : 15 - 17





maruta nilattil kuḷattiṉ aṭaikaraiyil vaḷarntirukkum, aram pōlum muṭkaḷai uṭaiya pirampuk koṭi pōlum koṭiyai uṭaiya āmpaliṉ, nīr kuṟaiya nīr maṭṭattukku mēl uyarntu curuṇṭirukkum ilai, acaintu acaintu varum vāṭaikkāṟṟu vīcuntoṟum, virintu aṭaṅkum kāṭcikkuk kollaṉ ulaikkaḷattil kāṟṟu aṭikka vicaittu iḻuttuviṭum turuttiyai uvamai kāṭṭiyuḷḷār oru pulavar"

"'paḻaṉap poykai aṭaikaraip pirampiṉ

aravāy aṉṉa ammuḷ neṭuṅkoṭi

aruvi āmpal akalaṭai tuṭakki,

acaivaral vāṭai tūkkaliṉ, ūtulai

vicaivāṅku tōliṉ vīṅkupu ñekiḻum"

- akam : 96 : 3-7





tī ōṅki erintu aṭaṅkiya nilaiyil, neyyil pakkuvap paṭṭu mitakkum iṟaiccit tuṇṭukaḷukkup parutti nūṟkum peṇ, koṭṭai nīkki aṭittukkuvittu vaittirukkum paruttik kuviyalkaḷai uvamaiyākkiyuḷḷār oru pulavar,

"paruttip peṇṭiṉ paṉuval aṉṉa

neruppuc ciṉam taṇinta niṇam tayaṅku koḻumkuṟai".

-puṟam : 125 : 1-2





iraṭṭai iraṭṭaiyākak kavaitta katirkaḷaik kuṟṟip puṭaittu eṭutta varakariccōṟṟaiyum, kālnaṭaic cāṇaṅkaḷāl āṉa eru kuvintu kiṭakkum teruvil, kālattē viḷaintirukkum vēḷaiyiṉ veṇpūkkaḷai, veḷḷiya tayiril mitakka viṭṭu āycciyar ākkiya puḷik kuḻampayum vayiṟāra uṇṭu avaraik kāykaḷaik koyyum toḻilāḷiyaip paṭampiṭittuḷḷār oru pulavar.

"kavaikkatir varakiṉ avaippuṟu ākkal,

tāteru maṟukil pōtoṭu potuḷiya

vēḷai veṇpū veṇtayirk koḷīi

āymakaḷ aṭṭa ampuḷi mitavai

avarai koyyunar āra māntum"

puṟam : 215 :1-5





kāṇpavar karuttai īrkkum takutiyaṟṟatāṉa iḻi toḻilum pulavarkaḷiṉ kaviyārvattait taṇittu viṭavillai. "ūril viḻāt toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. appōtu paṟai koṭṭutal pōlum paṇi āṟṟa vēṇṭuvatu avaṉ , kaṭamai. ākavē āṅkum cella vēṇṭum. piḷḷaip pēṟṟiṟku uriya kālam. maṉaivikku vantu viṭṭatu. appōtu uṭaṉiruntu avaḷukku utava vēṇṭuvatum iṉṟiyamaiyātatu; ākavē, vīṭṭiṟkum cella vēṇṭum, itaṟkiṭaiyil ñāyiṟum maṟaiyat toṭaṅkiviṭṭāṉ. maḻai peyyavum toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. innilaiyil kaṭṭilukkuk kayiṟu piṉṉum toḻiṟkaṭamai mēṟkoṇṭu viṭṭa attoḻilāḷi, appaṇiyai viraintu muṭittu viṭṭu mēṟkūṟiya irupaṇikaḷ kuṟittu viraiyat tuṭikkum nilaiyil, piṉṉum vārkōtta avaṉ kai ūci viraiyum viraiviṉaic colla iyalātu. avviraiviṉait, taṉṉai velvār yārēṉum uḷarēl varuka eṉa vañciṉam kūṟi vantirukkum vēṟṟūr vīraṉōṭu pōriṭṭu vellat tuṭikkum, āttimālai aṇintu, avvūr vīraṉiṉ uḷḷa viraiviṟku uvamai kāṭṭiyuḷḷār oru pulavar”. “cāṟutalaik koṇṭeṉap, peṇīṟṟu uṟṟeṉap,

paṭṭa māri, ñāṉṟa ñāyiṟṟuk

kaṭṭil niṇakkum iḻiciṉaṉ kaiyatu

pōḻtūṇṭu ūciyiṉ viraintaṉṟu mātō!

ūrkoḷa vanta porunṉoṭu

ārpuṉai teriyal neṭuntaṉai pōrē"

-puṟam : 82





ūril nikaḻa irukkum viḻākkaḷai ūr makkaḷukku aṟivikkum aṟivippāḷarkaḷākap paḻaṅkālattil kuyavarkaḷum paṇipurintuḷḷaṉar. "arumpu kaṭṭiya katir pōl tōṉṟum, oḷivīcum malarkaḷaik kottuk kottāka īṉum nocci mālai aṇintu, āṟupōl akaṉṟu nīṇṭa teruvil, ūril uḷḷār yāvarum kēṭka, ‘ivvūril, iṉṉa nāḷil iṉṉa viḻā naṭaipeṟum; viḻāviṟ paṅku koḷḷa varuka' eṉa aṟivittuc cellum aṟivāl mutirnta kuyavaṉē. āmpal neruṅka vaḷarumaḷavu vaḷammikka vayalkaḷaiyum, poykaiyaiyum uṭaiya ūrukku, viḻā aṟivikkac celluvaiyāyiṉ, avvūr makaḷirai, 'kūriya paṟkaḷum akaṉṟa alkulum uṭaiyīr' eṉa avar nalam pārāṭṭi aḻaittu, 'teṟittu icai eḻuppuvataṟku ēṟpuṭaiya narampu kaṭṭiya yāḻil pāṭattakum pāṭalkaḷai iṉitāka icaikkavalla uṅkaḷurp pāṇaṉ ceyyum tuṉpaṅkaḷukkuk kaṇakkila; avaṉ uraippaṉa ellām meyyē pōl tōṉṟiṉum, aṉaittum poyyē; poyyai mūṭi maṟaittu meyyē pōl kūṟuvatil vallavaṉ appāṇaṉ. ākavē avaṉ kūṟuvatil peritum viḻippāy iruppīrkaḷāka eṉa itaiyum aṟivittuvara iyalumō? aṟivittu vara vēṇṭukiṟēṉ eṉap peṇ orutti, kuyavaṉaip pārttuk kūṟiyatāka oru ceyyuḷ :

"kaṇṇi kaṭṭiya katira aṉṉa

oṇkural noccit teriyal cūṭi,

āṟukiṭan taṉṉa akalneṭum teruvil,

cāṟueṉa nuvalum mutuvāy kuyava!

ituvum āṅkaṇ nuvaṉṟiciṉ mātō!

āmpal amaṉṟa tīmperum paḻaṉattup

poykai ūrkkup pōvō yākik,

kaikavar narampiṉ paṉuval pāṇaṉ

ceyta allal palkuva; vai eyiṟṟu

aitu akal alkul makaḷir! ivaṉ

poypoti koṭuñcol ōmpumiṉ eṉavē..

- naṟṟiṇai : 200





kuyavaṉ, oruvakaiyil kōyil pūcāriyākavum iruntu pali koṭuppatum ceyvaṉ. paḻamaiyum veṟṟic ciṟappum vāynta mūtūril parantu akaṉṟa ūr maṉṟattil, viḻāt toṭaṅkuvataṟku muṉṉar, nīla maṇi pōlum malark kottukkaḷai uṭaiya noccimālai aṇinta , uruvālum, uḷ aṟivālum mūtta kuyavaṉ, tāṉ iṭumpaliyai uṇṇumāṟu, pali uṇṇum kaṭavuḷaiyum, kākkaikaḷaiyum aḻaippataik kūṟukiṟatu piṟitoru ceyyuḷ.

"maṇikkural noccit teriyal cūṭip,

palikaḷ ārkaip pārmutu kuyavaṉ .

iṭupali nuvalum akaṉtalai maṉṟattu

viḻavuttalaik koṇṭa paḻaviṟal mūtūr".

- naṟṟiṇai : 293 : 1-4





kuyavaṉiṉ mantirak kaittiṟaṉ iṉṉamum aḻittu. viṭavillai. kuḻantaikaḷai, ‘akki’ eṉappaṭum tōl nōy paṟṟik koṇṭatum, avaṟṟiṉ tāymār, kuḻantaikaḷaik kuyavaṉiṭam koṇṭucella, avaṉ oru kucciyai, oruvakaic cemmaṇ kuḻampil naṉaittu, akkoppuḷaṅkaḷ uḷḷa pakutiyaic cuṟṟi, yāḷi eṉappaṭum oru kaṟpaṉaip pēyiṉ uruvattai varaiyac, cila nāṭkaḷukku kellām annōy maṟaintu pōyviṭum.

uṭaikaḷai veḷuttuk kañciyūṭṭal, pāṭaṟporuḷāka amaivataṟkut takutiyaṟṟa ciṟu ceyalkaḷākap pulavark niṉaikkavillai. "kūttu mutalām kaḷiyāṭṭaṅkaḷ orupāl nikaḻa, ōyātu viḻāk koṇṭāṭik koṇṭirukkum mūtūrkaḷil, āṭai kaḷaip parutti āṭai, paṭṭāṭai eṉa iṉam pirittu aḻukkup pōkat tuvaittaḷikkum toḻilaik kaiyōyvatillāmal ceyvatāl vaṟumai aṟiyātu vāḻum calavait toḻil makaḷ orutti, ciṉṉam ciṟu pūttoḻilkaḷ koṇṭa ciṟanta āṭaikku, iravil cōṟṟukkañci ūṭṭum ciṟappu oru pāṭṭil kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu".

“āṭiyal viḻaviṉ aḻuṅkal mūtūr

uṭai ōr pāṉmaiyil peruṅkai tūvā

vaṟaṉ il pulatti ellit tōytta

pukāppukaḷ koṇṭa puṉpūṅ kaliṅkam”.

naṟṟiṇai : 90 : 1-4





pāṟaiyil tiṉai ularttik kāttirukkum oru peṇṇaip pāṭiyuḷḷār oru pulavar. muṉkai niṟaiya vaḷaiyalkaḷaiyum, piṟa uṭal uṟuppukaḷil avaṟṟiṟku uriya aṇikaḷaiyum aṇintirukkum uyarkuṭi makaḷir, kariya periya malai mītu akaṉṟa pāṟaiyil centiṉai parappik kāttu niṟkiṉṟaṉar. "niṟaivaḷai muṉkai, nēriḻai makaḷir

iruṅkal viyal aṟaic centiṉai parappi"

- kuṟuntokai : 335: 1-2.





kāycciya pālil mōr iṭṭut tayir ākkuvatum ōr uvamaiyākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. "peṇmaikkuriya maṭappam uṭaiyaḷāya āymakaḷ, taṉkai viral muṉaiyāl toṭṭut teṟitta ciṟitaḷavē āṉa mōr, kuṭam niṟaiya irunta pālaṉaittaiyum tayirākkum nikaḻcci, kaḷam pukunta oru peruvīraṉ akkaḷattil niṟaintu niṟkum nāṟpaṭai aṉaittukkum uyir pōkkum nōyāki niṟkum taṉmaikku uvamaiyākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.

"maṭappāl āymakaḷ vaḷḷukirt teṟitta

kuṭappāl cilluṟai pōlap

paṭaikku nōy ellām tāṉ āyiṉaṉē"

- puṟam : 276 :3-5





kaṭuku miḷaku mutalām tāḷippup poruṭkaḷaik kalantu uṇaviṟku maṇam ūṭṭuvatum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kuykkoḷ koḻuntuvai' (puṟam : 160 : 1) kuyyuṭai aṭicil" (puṟam: 127 : 7)

ciṟuvar ciṟumiyariṉ āṭal pāṭal pōlum iṉiya poḻutupōkkukaḷ paṟpala iṭaṅkaḷil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. taccaṉ ceytu koṭutta ciṟiya kutirai pūṭṭappaṭṭa ciṟiya tēril amarntu ceṉṟu iṉpuṟal iyalātu eṉiṉum, ataik kayiṟu koṇṭu īrttuc ceṉṟu iṉpam aṭaiyum ciṟuvar ciṟumiyariṉ iṉpa viḷaiyāṭṭil iṉpam kaṇṭuḷḷār oru pulavar. taccaṉ ceyta ciṟumā vaiyam

ūrntu iṉpuṟāar āyiṉum, kaiyiṉ

īrttu iṉpuṟūum iḷaiyōr.'’

-kuṟuntokai : 1 :1 - 3





tām toṭutta malarmālaiyai aṇintu koṇṭu, mūṅkil pōlum parutta tōḷ uṭaiyēm eṉṟa tuṇivāl kaṭal nīril pukuntu āṭiya makaḷir, kaṭaṟkaraik kāṉal cōlai yil parantu kiṭakkum maṇalāl ciṟṟil kaṭṭi viḷaiyāṭum iṉpak kāṭciyaik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār oru pulavar.

"tuḷaitta kōtaip paṇaipperum tōḷiṉar,

kaṭalāṭu makaḷir kāṉal iḻaitta

ciṟumaṉai”.

- kuṟuntokai : 326 : 1-3





oḷivīcum vaḷaikaḷai aṇinta makaḷir, vaṟupaṭāta paccai nellai, avalāka iṭittu muṭittu, atu ceyyat tuṇai purinta, kariya vayiram pāynta marattāl āṉa ulakkaiyai muṟṟit talai cāyttu aḻakuṟak kāṭci aḷikkum neṟpayir niṟkum vayal varappil paṭukka vaittu viṭṭu viḷaiyāṭac ceṉṟuviṭum ciṟappaik kūṟukiṟatu oru ceyyuḷ.

“pācaval iṭitta karuṅkāḻ ulakkai

āykatir nelliṉ varampaṇait tuyiṟṟi

oṇtoṭi makaḷir vaṇṭal ayarum”.

- kuṟuntokai : 238 : 13





taṉpāl aṉpu kāṭṭuvatil oppuk kāṇamāṭṭāta taṉ tōḻiyar kūṭṭattoṭu aruviyil pāyntu āṭi- avvāṟu āṭuṅkāl, nīralaikaḷ tākkiyatāl, taṉṉuṭaiya periya, aḻakiya, aṉpoḻukum kaṇkaḷ civantu pōkak kāṭci aḷikkum celvattirumakaḷaik kāṭṭukiṟatu oru ceyyuḷ. -

“poruvil āyamoṭu aruvi āṭi

nīr alaic civanta pēramar maḻaikkaṇ".

naṟṟiṇai : 44 : 1:2





ūñcal āṭṭam ellōrukkum terinta maṟṟoru viḷaiyāṭṭu. aḻakiya kaṇkaḷai uṭaiya tōḻiyar kūṭṭam āṭṭiviṭumāṟu, kariya paṉai nārait tirittu muṟukkiya kayiṟṟai nīḷamākat toṅkaviṭṭuk kaṭṭiya ūñcal oṉṟai āṭṭik kāṭṭukiṟatu ōr aḻakiya ceyyuḷ. "peruṅkayiṟu nālum irumpaṉam piṇaiyal

pūṅkaṇ āyam ūkka"

- naṟṟiṇai : 90 : 6-7





paḷḷi ceṉṟu kalvi kallāta iḷañciṟuvarkaḷ, vēppa marattu aṭiyil, neruṅkat taḻaitta ataṉ ilaikaḷ tarum, puḷḷi puḷḷiyākat tōṉṟum niḻalil, poṟkollaṉ pāl uḷḷa poṉṉuraikalpōlum vaṭivil vaṭṭamāṉa araṅkai varaintukoṇṭu, nellik kāykaḷai vaṭṭukkaḷākak koṇṭu, pāṇṭil āṭum aḻakum ōriṭattil kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.

vēmpiṉ puḷḷi nīḻal

kaṭṭaḷai aṉṉa vaṭṭaraṅku iḻaittuk

kallāc ciṟāar nellivaṭ ṭāṭum"

- naṟṟiṇai : 3 : 2 - 4





kūrai vēynta nalla maṉaikku uriyavarākiya kuṟuntoṭikaḷai aṇinta makaḷir, taṅkaḷ maṉaiyiṉ muṉpuṟattil parappiyirukkum putumaṇalil amarntu, kaḻaṟcik kāykaḷaik koṇṭu kaḻaṅkāṭik kaḷittiruppar.

"kūrai naṉmaṉaik kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir,

maṇalāṭu kaḻaṅku".

- naṟṟiṇai : 79 : 2-3





makaḷir viḷaiyāṭṭākap pala iṭaṅkaḷilum kūṟap paṭṭiruppatu ōrai. pūñcāyk kōrai eṉum oruvakaik kōraip pullāl ceytu makarantap poṭikaḷ pūcic ceyta pāvai vaittu viḷaiyāṭu āyamoṭu, ōrai āṭātu". "oḷḷiḻai makaḷiroṭu ōraiyum āṭāy” (naṟṟiṇai 68:1-3, 155:1) “ōrai āyattu oṇṭoṭi makaḷir” (puṟam : 176 : 1)

makaḷiriṉ maṟṟoru viḷaiyāṭṭu "alliyam.” āṇ kōlamum peṇ kōlamum uṭaiyavāka iraṇṭu pāvaikaḷaic ceytu koṇṭu āṭum oruvakai viḷaiyāṭṭu.

"vallōṉ taiiya vari vaṉappu uṟṟa

allip pāvai āṭuvaṉappu ēyppak

kāma iruvar”.

puṟam : 33 : 16 - 18





kuruṭṭu nampikkaikaḷ



iṉi, makkaḷiṭaiyē nilaviyirunta kuruṭṭu nampikkaikaḷ cila. kākkai karaintāl, viruntu varum eṉṟa nampikkai, iṉṟēpōl aṉṟum iruntatu. “kaṇavaṉ pirivāṟṟāt tuṉpak kavalaiyāl melintuviṭṭa eṉ tōḻiyiṉ tōḷkaḷ, ammelivu nīṅkip paṇṭē pōl ākic ciṟappuṟuvataṟkut tuṇai ceyyum vakaiyil, kaṇavaṉ varukaiyaik karaintu muṉ aṟivittatu kākkai; annallatu ceyta kākkaikku iṭum paliyākat toṇṭi. nakarattu vayalkaḷil muṟṟa viḷainta veṇṇel arici koṇṭu cuṭucōṟṟait tiṇṇiya tērukku uriya naḷḷi eṉṉum koṭaivaḷḷaliṉ kāṭṭil vāḻum āyarkaḷiṉ pala pacukkaḷiṭam koṇṭa neyyaik kalantu, ēḻu kalattil iṭṭu vaittālum, atu ceyta naṉṟiyai nōkka, ippali ciṟitām” eṉak kūṟukiṟāḷ talaiviyiṉ tōḻi orutti.

"tiṇtēr naḷḷi kāṉattu aṇṭar

pallā payanta neyyil, toṇṭi

muḻutuṭaṉ viḷainta veṇṇel veñcōṟu

eḻukalattu ēntiṉum ciṟitu; eṉ tōḻi

peruntōḷ nekiḻtta cellaṟku

viruntuvarak karainta kākkaiyatu paliyē'’

- kuṟuntokai : 270





cakuṉaṅkaḷilāṉa nampikkai aḷavumīṟi iruntatu. paṟavaikaḷiṉ pōkku, avaṟṟiṉ kural, vara irukkum ākkam, iḻappukkaḷai aṟivittuviṭavallaṉa eṉa uṟutiyāka nampiṉar. "puḷḷum, poḻutum paḻittal" (puṟam: 204 : 10). paricil kiṭaikkātapōtu, atu tarātuviṭutta puravalaṉaip paḻikkātu, tām puṟappaṭṭu vantapōtu nikaḻnta puḷnimittam kālakkēṭukaḷaiyē paḻippar. pēykaḷ, maraṅkaḷilum iṭukāṭukaḷilum vāḻum eṉṟu nampiṉārkaḷ. pēy, taṉ kūṭṭattōṭu āṭikkaḷikkum iṭukāṭṭai oru pulavar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. (puṟam : 288: 45) pōrkkaḷattil vīrappuṇ peṟṟu iṟantu vīḻntu kiṭakkum vīrarkaḷiṉ vīrap puṇkaḷait tōṇṭi, atiliruntu perukiya ceṅkurutiyāl civantu pōṉa taṉ kaikaḷāl taṉatu mayiraik kōti muṭittu, ataṉāl kaṟutta taṉ mēṉiyum cenniṟaṅ kāṭṭum oru pēymakaḷaik kāṭṭukiṟār oru pulavar.

"porutu āṇṭu oḻinta maintar puṇ toṭṭuk

kurutic ceṅkaik kūntal tīṭṭi ,

niṟam kiḷar uruviṉ pēeyp peṇṭir".

puṟam : 62:2-4





pēymakaḷ piṇattait taḻuvi, ataṉ veḷḷiya niṇattait tiṉṉum; "pēey makaḷir piṇam taḻuuppaṟṟi, viḷar ūṉ tiṉṟa vempulāl" (puṟam : 359 : 4-5) pēy, makaḷiraip paṟṟik koḷḷum; avvāṟu pēyāl paṟṟap paṭṭa makaḷir, kutittu āṭuvar. "muruku meyppaṭṭa pulaitti pōlat tāvupu teṟikkum". (puṟam : 259:5-6) veṇkaṭukaip pukaittāl pēy ōṭi maṟaintuviṭum. "aiyavi pukaippavum" (puṟam : 98 : 15) eṉṟellām makkaḷ nampi iruntaṉar.

ōṭukiṉṟa āṟṟiṉ iṭaiyē uḷḷa, turutti eṉṟum aḻaikkap paṭum mēṭṭil, palvēṟu icaikkaruvikaḷ icai eḻuppa, āṭṭiṉ kaḻuttai aṟuttut, tiṉaiyaip pirappaṅ kūṭaiyil parappi vaittu tām veḷippaṭavantu ulāval allatu, nōykaḷait tīrkkalākāt teyvaṅkaḷ orucēra vaḻipaṭṭu ivaḷ pēyāl paṟṟappaṭṭāḷ eṉṟa muṭiviṟku * vantu niṟkum makaḷiraik kāṭṭukiṟār oru pulavar.

“maṟikkural aṟuttut tiṉaippirappu iraic,

cellāṟṟuk kavalaip pal iyam kaṟaṅkat

tōṟṟam allatu nōykku marun tāka.

vēṟṟup perum teyvam palavuṭaṉ vāḻttip

pōyk koḷīiyaḷ ivaḷ eṉa'’

kuṟuntokai : 263 : 1-5





maṉita iṟaicci, kurutikaḷai virumpuvatu pēy. ataṉāl, kaḷattil iṟavātu vīḻntu kiṭakkum vīrarkaḷiṉ puṇṇait tōṇṭat talaippaṭṭuviṭum. pēy toṭṭa puṇ, āṟuvatum illai . avvīraṉ uyir piḻaippatum ilaṉ, itil nampikkai uṭaimaiyāl avaṉ maṉaivi, pēy, taṉ kaṇavaṉ irukkum iṭattaiyum aṇṭak kūṭātu eṉpatāl, vīṭṭu vāyilil irantai, vēppantaḻaikaḷaic ceruki, yāḻ mutalām icaikaḷai eḻuppi, mella eḻuntu vīṭṭai maiyiṭṭu meḻuki, veṇ ciṟukaṭukai eṅkum tūvi, āmpal kuḻal ūti, maṇi aṭittu, kāñcippaṇ pāṭi. māḷikai muṟṟilum naṟumaṇap pukai eḻuppik kāppaṉ.

“tīṅkaṉi iravamoṭu vēmpumaṉaic cerīi

vāṅkumaruppu yāḻoṭu palliyam kaṟaṅkak,

kai payappeyarttu, mai iḻutu iḻuki,

aiyavi citaṟi, āmpal ūti,

icaimaṇi eṟintu, kāñci pāṭi,

neṭunakar varaippil kaṭinṟai pukai ik

kākkam vammō ?” -

-puṟam : 287 : 1-7





camaya vaḻipāṭukaḷ

toṉmaiyāṉa vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaikaḷ, aintāvatu atikārattil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āriyattukku muntiya tamiḻar nākarīkam (Pre Aryan Tamil Culture) eṉṟa eṉṉuṭaiya nūlilum viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṅkē eṭuttukkāṭṭiya kaṭavuḷkaḷē allāmal, vēṟu kaṭavuḷkaḷaiyum tamiḻar vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. tiṅkaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ oṉṟu. kaṭal naṭuvē, mīṉ piṭi paṭakukaḷām timilkaḷil ēṟṟappaṭṭuk kāṭci aḷikkum viḷakkukaḷ pōla, cemmīṉkaḷ oḷiviṭum vicumpiṉ uccikkaṇ, muḻumati nāḷaṉṟu, am muḻumatiyaik kaṇṭu, kāṭṭuvāḻ mayil pōṉṟa, cila vaḷaiyalkaḷ aṇinta vaḷāy eṉ viṟaliyum nāṉum ammuḻumati, vaḷavaṉ veṇ koṟṟakkuṭai pōṉṟuḷatu eṉa eṇṇiyavāṟē toḻutōm allavō? eṉṟa pulavar viṉāvil, mati vaḻipāṭu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷatu.

"munnīr nāppaṇ timilcuṭar pōlac

cemmīṉ imaikkum māka vicumpiṉ

ucci niṉṟa uvavumati kaṇṭu

kaṭci maññaiyiṉ curamutal cērnta

cilvaḷai viṟaliyum yāṉum valviraintu

toḻutaṉam allamō? ...........

vaḷavaṉ ..........

mālai veṇkuṭai okkumāl eṉavē".

puṟam : 60





("māka vicumpu" eṉum ittoṭar, puṟam : 35, 60, 270, 400, akam : 253, maturaikkāñci : 454; paripāṭal 47 ākiya pala iṭaṅkaḷil vantiruntālum, uraiyāciriyarkaḷ ataṟkāṉa teḷivāṉa poruḷai uṇarttiṉārallar).

kākkaikku uṇavu aḷittal, ovvoru vīṭṭilum, nāḷtōṟum tavaṟāmal naṭaipeṟum oru camayac caṭaṅku. "katirkaḷ eṟittalāl naṭappār kālkaḷai veppam. paṇṇumāṟu ñāyiṟu kāyum pakaṟpoḻutil, nakaril uḷḷa periya māḷikaikaḷukku varum viruntiṉarkaḷai varavēṟṟu uṇavaḷittu ōmpuvāṉ vēṇṭi, poṟṟoṭi aṇinta ammāḷikai vāḻ makaḷir, viruntiṉarkku uṇavu paṭaippataṉ muṉṉar, māḷikaiyiṉ muṟṟattil paliyākap pōṭṭa, kokku ukir pōlum cōṟṟait tiṉṟa kākkaik kūṭṭam, poḻutu maṟaiya, mīṉ aṅkāṭi pukuntu, āṅku niḻalil kuvittu vaittirukkum paccai mīṉkaḷaik kavarntu koṇṭu kaṭaṟkaraiyil viṉai oḻintu āṭik koṇṭirukkum kappaliṉ pāymarattil ceṉṟu taṅkum. itaip paṭam piṭittuk kāṭṭukiṟār oru pulavar.

"katirkāl vempak, kalkāy ñāyiṟṟut

tiruvuṭai viṉ nakar varuviruntu ayarmār

poṟṟoṭi makaḷir puṟaṅkaṭai ukutta

kokku utir nimiral mānti, eṟpaṭa

akal aṅkāṭi acainiḻal kuvitta

pacciṟāk kavarnta pacuṅkaṇ kākkai

tūṅkal vaṅkattuk kūmpil cēkkum”.

- naṟṟiṇai : 258 : 3-9





paḻaṅkālattil, periya teyva vaḻipāṭukaḷ, palikoṭuttal, teyvattaṉmai vāynta āṭal, pāṭalkaḷai oruṅkē koṇṭirukkum attaku oru vaḻipāṭu kīḻvarumāṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. “ō eṉum pēroli eḻa, veḷḷai veḷēreṉa oḷi viḷaṅka ōṭivarum aruvikaḷāl viḷakkam peṟṟa malaiccāralil, vēṅkaiyiṉ tēṉ kamaḻum ‘ malarkaḷaic cūṭikkoṇṭu, toṇṭakappaṟai eḻuppum oliyaṉ tāḷattiṟku ēṟpa, āṭavarum peṇṭirum terukkaḷil kalantu āṭi makiḻum ciṟuciṟu kuṭikaḷaik koṇṭa pākkattil murukaṉ ulāvarutalaik kūṟukiṟatu oru ceyyuḷ”.

kaṟaṅkuveḷ ḷaruvi piṟaṅkumalaik kavāaṉ,

tēm kamaḻ iṇara vēṅkai cūṭit

toṇṭakap paṟaiccīr peṇṭiroṭu viraii

maṟukil tūṅkum ciṟukuṭip pākkattu iyal muruku”.

- akam : 118 : 1-5





kuṉṟukaḷ vēlipōl nāṟpuṟamum niṟka, iṭaiyē uḷḷa ciṟṟūril, maṉṟattil niṟkum vēṅkai maraṅkaḷ, maṉa nāḷākiya nalla nāḷil pūkkat toṭaṅka, avaṟṟiṉ maṇi maṇiyāṉa arumpukaḷ malarnta, poṉ pōṉṟa malarkaḷ utirntu, akaṉṟa pāṟaikaḷai aḻaku ceyyum muṟṟaṅkaḷil, kuṟavarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ maṉaikaḷil, kuravai āṭṭattil kaitērnta mutiya makaḷiroṭu kaikōttu, ārāvāram eḻak , kuravai āṭi viḻā eṭuppatai viḷakkukiṟatu oru ceyyuḷ :

kuṉṟa vēlic ciṟukuṭi āṅkaṇ

maṉṟa vēṅkai maṇanāḷ pūtta

maṇiyēr arumpiṉ poṉvī tāay

viyalaṟai virikkum muṉṟil, kuṟavar

maṉaimutir makaḷiroṭu kuravai tūṅkum

ārkali viḻavu"

akam : 232 : 6:11.





"kaḻ nīril mīṉ ōṭum, mēl nīril kaṇpōlum karuṅkuvaḷai malarum; uppaṅkaḻi nīrāl cūḻappeṟṟa payir viḷaintu niṟkum kaḻaṉikaḷil, avaṟṟaik kāppavar paṟai aṭittu eḻuppum arittu eḻum ōcai kēṭṭup paṟavaikaḷ ōṭiviṭum. arumpukaḷ mitakkaviṭṭa kaḷḷaiyum, iṉiya tēṟalaiyum naṟavaiyum kuṭittu makiḻnta kōcar kuravai āṭi makiḻvataik kūṟukiṟatu, oru ceyyuḷ.

"kīḻnīrāṉ mīṉvaḻaṅ kuntu;

mīnīrāṉ kaṇṇaṉṉa malar pūkkuntu;

kaḻi cuṟṟiya viḷaikaḻaṉi

arippaṟaiyāl puḷ oppantu;

neruṭunīr kūum maṇal taṇkāl

meṉ paṟaiyāl puḷ iriyuntu;

naṉaikkaḷḷiṉ. maṉaikkōcar,

tīntēṟal naṟavu makiḻntu

tīṅkuravaik koḷai tāṅkuntu"

- puṟam : 396:1-9





kōcar ciṟanta vīrar; ākavē avarkaḷ. āṭiya ik kuravaik kūttu oruvakaip pōrkkūttu ākum.

itaṉiṉum koṭumai vāynta oru pōrkkūttu, piṉvarumāṟu viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. “ceṅkarumpu kaḻikaḷ mītu kāṟṟil acaintāṭum cenneṟkatirkaḷai vēyntu kaṭṭiya pantal, viḻā eṭukkum iṭam pōlap paṟpala aḻakuṭaiyatākat tōṉṟa, ōyātu nel kuṟṟum ulakkai oliyōṭu, palvēṟu olikaḷ olikkum āṅku, poṉṉāl ceyta tumpaip pūvām pōrp pūvuṭaṉ paciya paṉam tōṭṭaiyum aṇintu koṇṭu, kaṭuñciṉam mikka vīrarkaḷ kaṭal olipōl, āravārap pēroli eḻuppiyavāṟē, kuravaik kūttai, veṟi koṇṭu āṭuvar”.

“alaṅku cennel katir vēynta

āykarumpiṉ koṭik kūrai

cāṟu koṇṭa kaḷam pōla

vēṟu vēṟu polivu tōṉṟak,

kuṟṟu āṉā ulakkaiyāl,

kaliccummai viyal āṅkaṇ

polantoṭṭup paintumpai

micai alaṅkuḷaiya paṉaippōḻ ceric

ciṉamāntar veṟikkuravai

ōtanīriṟ peyarpu poṅka”

puṟam : 22 : 14-23





ki.pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil āriya nākarikam, tamiḻar uḷḷattuḷ , veḷḷam pōl perukkeṭuttup pāyntu avartam vāḻkkaiyiṉ ovvoru nilaiyaiyum māṟṟiviṭṭatu. maṟṟa ceyalpāṭukaḷōṭu, āṭal pāṭalkaḷum pātippukku uḷḷākiviṭṭaṉa, paḻantamiḻariṉ iyaṟkai yōṭiyainta eḷiya paṇṇicai, āriyariṉ kaṟpaṉaittiṟam vāynta palvēṟu paṭikaḷai uṭaiyavāya mellicaikaḷukku iṭam koṭuttuviṭṭatu. avarkaḷiṉ paḻaiya āṭalkaḷiṉ iṭattai, āriyariṉ, eḷitil viḷaṅkalākāp palvēṟu vakaiyākap pirivuṇṭa āṭalkaḷ paṟṟikkoṇṭaṉa. paḻaiya āṭal, oṉṟu, coṟkaḷiṉ tuṇai vēṇṭāmal, meyp pāṭṭiṉ mūlamākavē, kātal, vīra uṇarvukaḷai veḷippaṭuttum; allatu muḻukka muḻukka vaḻipāṭu kuṟitta āṭalākum. āriya nākarīkattiṉ iṭai nuḻaivāl, akkalaiyil, palvēṟu nilaikaḷ vaḷarntu viṭṭaṉa. āṭal pāṭal kuṟitta ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum eḻutappaṭalāyiṉa. āṉāl, annūlkaḷ, ippōtu aḻintuviṭṭaṉa. āṉāl akkalaikaḷaik kulat toḻilākak koṇṭa kalaiñarkaḷāl, avai payilap peṟuvatu kuṟitta kuṟippukaḷ palavaṟṟaic cilappatikāram tarukiṟatu. ataṉ uraiyāciriyarkaḷ, avai kuṟitta virivāṉa viḷakkaṅkaḷai aḷittuḷḷaṉar. paṇṭu āṭal nikaḻnta iṭam ūr maṉṟam. āṉāl, atu cilappatikāram mūṉṟām atikārattil kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu pōlum palnilai āṭaraṅkāka urumāṟi viṭṭatu. āṉāl, immāṟṟamellām, perunakaraṅkaḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉavē allatu ciṟṟūrkaḷil aṉṟu. nakaraṅkaḷ

urōmāṉiya nāṭṭuṭaṉ naṭattiya vāṇikam perumaḷa vil. vaḷarntuviṭṭa piṉṉar, tamiḻakattil nakaraṅkaḷ vaḷamuṭaiyavākavum, celvac ceḻippu ṭaiyavākavum uyarntuviṭṭaṉa. akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭal oṉṟil, cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ talainakaraṅkaḷ curukkamāka . viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

“uṟaviṉarkku uḷavām kēṭṭiṉaik kaḷaintu, avarait tāṅkik koḷḷavum, uṟaviṉarāy uḷḷār, kuṟaiviṉṟi uṇṇavum, ayalār, anta ayalārām nilai keṭṭuk keḻutakai uṟaviṉarāka māṟi oḻukavum virumpi, ataṟkut tuṇai ceyyum poruḷ īṭṭum muyaṟciyil ūkkam mikak koṇṭu, atil viruppamum mikuntu, āttimālai aṇipavarum, etirttārai aḻikkavalla pōrmuṟaikaḷai aṟintavarum ākiya cōḻarkku uriyatāṉa, aṟaṅkūr avai irukkum ciṟappiṉaik koṇṭa uṟaiyūrai otta peṟutaṟkariya pēraṇikalaṉkaḷai aṭaintu nallōr yāvarālum virumpattakkatāṉa ceyaṟkariya ceyalām poruḷ īṭṭum paṇiyai iṉitē muṭittu viṭṭōm. ākavē, pakaivar araṇkaḷ palavaṟṟai veṟṟi koṇṭa, pakaip paṭaikaḷōṭu eppōtum māṟupāṭu koḷḷum nāṟpaṭaiyiṉai uṭaiya , eṉṟum pukaḻ vāṭāta vēppa mālai taritta pāṇṭiyaṉukku uriya kūṭalmā nakariṉ, kālaik kaṭaivītiyiṉ palvēṟu maṇaṅkaḷum orucēra maṇakkum nalla neṟṟiyiṉaiyum, nīṇṭa kariya kūntaliṉaiyum, māmai niṟattu mēṉiyiṉaiyum uṭaiya nam kātaliyuṭaṉ, malaiyaik kuṭaintu iyaṟṟiyatu pōlum, vāṉai aḷāviya nīṇṭa māḷikaiyil, kaṭal nuraiyai aṭaittu vaittatu pōṉṟa melliya malarkaḷāl āṉa paṭukkai viritta, ōṅkiya kaṭṭilil, uyarnta viḷakkiṉ neṭuñcuṭar oḷiyil āṭavarkku uriya nalamelām uṭaiya nam mārpil, nam kātaliyiṉ mārpakattu aṇikaḷ vaṭukkaḷaic ceyyumāṟu, varivariyāṉa kōṭukaḷ niṟainta neṟṟiyiṉaiyum valimikutaliṉāl muḻaṅkum, vāyvarai vantu oḻukum mata nīriṉaiyum, kūṟṟuvaṉukku nikarāṉa āṟṟalum nilattai aṟaintu, pakaivarai aṇukac ceṉṟu kolvatil tappāta, niṉaittālē naṭuṅkappaṇṇum, toṅkum kaiyiṉaiyum uṭaiya koṭiya periya yāṉaip paṭaiyiṉaiyum, neṭiya tērppaṭaiyiṉaiyum uṭaiya cēraṉukku uriya , celvattāl ciṟanta mikap periya nakaramākiya karuvūriṉ tuṟaiyil, teḷinta nīr ōṭum kuḷirnta āṉ porunai āṟu, uyarnta karaiyil kuvittuḷḷa maṇaliṉ eṇṇikkaiyilum, palavāka, nām taḻuvi iṉpuṟuvōm; neñcē! varuvāyāka!

"kēḷkēṭu ūṉṟavum, kiḷaiñar āravum,

kēḷal kēḷir keḻīiyiṉar oḻukavum,

āḷviṉaikku etiriya ūkkamoṭu, pukal ciṟantu,

āram kaṇṇi, aṭupōrc cōḻar,

aṟamkeḻu nallavai uṟantai aṉṉa,

peṟalarum ceyviṉai muṟṟiṉam, āyiṉ;

araṇpal paṭarnta muraṇkoḷ tāṉai

vāṭā vēmpiṉ vaḻuti kūṭal

nāḷaṅ kāṭi nāṟum naṟunutal,

nīḷ irum kūntal, māa yōḷoṭu,

varaikuyiṉ ṟaṉṉa vāṉtōy neṭunakar,

nuraimukan taṉṉa meṉpūñ cēkkai,

nivanta paḷḷi, neṭum cuṭar viḷakkattu

nilam kēḻ ākam, pūṇṭup poṟippa,

muyaṅkuvam; ceṉmō ; neñcē ; varinutal

vayam tikaḻpu imiḻtarum vāypuku kaṭāttu

mīḷi moympoṭu nilaṉeṟiyāk kuṟuki

āḷkōḷ piḻaiyā añcuvaru taṭakkaik

kaṭumpakaṭṭu yāṉai, neṭuntērk kōtai

tirumā viyaṉnakark karuvūr muṉ tuṟait

teṇṇīr uyarkaraik kavaiiya

taṇāṉ porunai maṇaliṉum palavē".

-akam : 93





aracarkaḷ vāḻntatāl nakaraṅkaḷ, mukkiyattuvam peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. nakaraṅkaḷ perukavē, celvamum, aracar kaḷiṉ mukkiyattuvamum uyarntuviṭṭaṉa. tamiḻakattu mūvēntarkaḷum, paṇṭaikkālattil, vēlaiyaṟṟuk kiṭakkum nāṭkaḷil poḻutupōkkiṟkākavum, taṅkaḷ āṇmaiyai nilaināṭṭik koḷvataṟkākavum ēṉaiya aracukaḷai aṭimai koṇṭa pēraracāka uyarvataṟkākavum oruvarōṭoruvar pōr toṭuttuvantaṉar. ākavē, ikkālap pāṭalkaḷil, aracarkaḷiṉ koṭai vaḷap pārāṭṭu iṉṉamum iṭam peṟṟuḷatu eṉṟālum, avai, aracarkaḷiṉ, pōrkkaḷ veṟṟikaḷaip pārāṭṭalāyiṉa. "pakaivarai veṉṟu avartam paṭṭattu yāṉaiyiṉ poṉṉālāṉa neṟṟip paṭṭattaik kaikkoṇṭu, atu aḻittup paṇṇiya poṟṟāmarai malaraip pāṭip pukaḻum pāṇaṉ talaiyil aḻaku peṟac cūṭṭiya ciṟanta talaimai yiṉaiyum, pōril puṟam kāṭṭāk kōṭpāṭṭiṉaiyum uṭaiya periyōṉ vaḻiyil vantavaṉē! irāppoḻutu iṅkēyē uṟaṅkiviṭṭatō , eṉa aiyuṟutaṟku ēṟpap - pēriruḷ cūḻnta aṭarnta ciṟu kāṭṭaiyum, paṟai oli pōl olikkum malaiyaruvi kaḷaiyum uṭaiya muḷḷūr malaikku uriya vēntē! muḷ pōlum kūriya paṟkaḷaiyuṭaiya pāmpaiyum naṭuṅkap paṇṇum iṭiyēṟupōl pōr muracu muḻaṅka, talaimai tāṅkivanta yāṉaiyum, ataṉ mītu amarntu pōriṭṭa aracum kaḷattil oruṅkē iṟantupaṭa, aḻittaṟkariya avar nāṟpaṭaikaḷaic citaṟa aḻittu, ciṟitum poruntāta pakai, taṉ nāṭṭai aṇukavum viṭātu, taṭuttu niṟuttum ellaiyākat tikaḻum peṇṇaiyāṟu pāyum nāṭṭiṟku uriyavaṉē : uṉ pukaḻaip pāṭavallamāyiṉum, vallōmal lēmāyiṉam, uṉpāl vantu uṉpukaḻ pāṭuvōmāyiṉ, aḻikkalākāt taṉmai yarākiya uṉ kiḷaiyoṭu nīyum peruvāḻvu vāḻa, innilamicai uḷḷār ellāriṉum aṟivu oḻukkaṅkaḷil mācaṟṟa antaṇaṉākiya kapilaṉ, poruḷ vēṇṭi irantu cellum pulavarkaḷukku iṉip pukaḻvataṟku iṭamillai eṉak kūṟumaḷavu uṉ pukaḻum, avaṉ pukaḻum peruki niṟkappāṭiviṭṭāṉ. ataṉāl, ciṉam mikka cēṉaikku uriyavaṉākiya cēraṉukkuriya mēlaikkaṭalil, piṟanāṭṭup poṟkācukaḷaic cumantu varum kaṭalōṭavalla periya nāvāykaḷ ōṭiya iṭattil, vēṟu ciṉṉañ ciṟu marak kalaṅkaḷ ōṭamāṭṭātatu pōlum nilaiyiṉarāki viṭṭōmāyiṉum, emmuṭaiya vaṟumai turatta, uṉ perumpukaḻ īrttuk koṇṭuvara vantu, uṉ vaḷamaik kuṇaṅkaḷil, cilavaṟṟai nāṅkaḷum pāṭip pārāṭṭiṉam".

"oṉṉār. yāṉai ōṭaippoṉ koṇṭu,

pāṇar ceṉṉi poliyat taii,

vāṭāt tāmarai cūṭṭiya, viḻuccar,

ōṭāp pūṭkai uravōṉ maruka!

vallēm allēm āyiṉum, vallē,

niṉvayiṉ kiḷakkuva māyiṉ, kaṅkal

tuyil maṭin taṉṉa tūṅkuiruḷ iṟumpiṉ,

paṟai icai aruvi, muḷḷūrp poruna!

teṟalarum marapiṉ niṉ kiḷaiyoṭum poliya,

nilamicaip paranta makkaṭ kellām

puvaṉ aḻuk kaṟṟ anta ṇāḷaṉ,

irantu cel mākkaṭku iṉi iṭaṉiṉṟip,

paranticai niṟkap pāṭiṉaṉ; ataṟkoṇṭu,

ciṉamiku tāṉai vāṉavaṉ kuṭakaṭal

polantaru nāvāy ōṭṭiya avvaḻip

piṟakalam celkalātu; aṉaiyēm. attai;

iṉmai tūrappa, icaitara vantu niṉ

vaṇmaiyil toṭuttaṉam yāmē, muḷ eyiṟṟu

aravu eṟi urumiṉ muracu eḻuntu iyampa,

aṇṇal yāṉaiyoṭu ventukaḷattu oḻiya

arum camam tataiyat tākki, naṉṟum

oṉṉāt tevvart tāṅkum

peṇṇaiyam paṭappai nāṭu kiḻa vōyē".

puṟam. 126





pōr muracukku, allatu, kūṟuvatāyiṉ, appōr muraciṉ uṟaiyum teyvattiṉ āvikkuk, kurutippali koṭuppatē pōrākak karutappaṭṭatu. pōr muracu, ataṟkeṉa amaikkappaṭum taṉi irukkaiyil vaikkappaṭum. oru ceyyuḷ ivvāṟu kūṟukiṟatu. "muracukkuk kuṟṟamām eṉak kūṟappaṭum kuṟṟam etuvum illātavāṟu valittup piṇitta vārai uṭaiya, karumarattāl ceyyappaṭṭamai yāl, karumai niṟam kāṭṭum pakkam aḻaku peṟa, mayiliṉ taḻaittu nīṇṭa tōkai, oḷḷiya puḷḷikaḷ niṟainta nīlamaṇipōlum niṟam vāynta mālai, ākiyavaṟṟaip poṉ pōlum taḷirkaḷaiyuṭaiya uḻiñaiyoṭu aḻakuṟa aṇiyappeṟṟa, kurutippali koḷḷum vēṭkaiyuṭaiyatāṉa pakaivar uḷam naṭuṅka muḻaṅka vallatāṉa pōr muracam, nīrāṭi varuvataṉ muṉṉar eṇṇey nuraiyai mukantu vaittāṟpōl, melliya malarkaḷ kuvittu vaikkappaṭṭa kaṭṭil oḻuṅku ceyyappaṭum”. "mācaṟa vicitta vārpuṟu vaḷpiṉ,

maipaṭu maruṅkul poliya, maññai

olineṭum pīli, oṇpoṟi maṇittār,

polaṅkuḻai uḻiñaiyoṭu poliyac cūṭṭik,

kuruti vēṭkai , urukeḻu muracam

maṇṇi vārā aḷavai, eṇṇey

nuraimukan taṉṉa meṉpūñ cēkkai”.

-puṟam : 50 7-7





oppiṭṭu nōkka, koṭumaimikā, vēta vaḻi vēḷvi muṟaikaḷ iṭam peṟat toṭaṅkiviṭṭa innūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum, pāṇṭiyap peruvīraṉ oruvaṉ kuṟittup piṉvarum pōrpparaṇip pāṭalaip pāṭa, ru pulavarait tūṇṭumaḷavu, paḻaiya veṟṟippalikaḷ pāl kaḷavēḷvikaḷ pāl koṇṭa peru vēṭkai, tamiḻ aracar uḷḷattil, iṉṉamum, attuṇai āḻamāka vērūṉṟi iruntatu. “mikka āḻam uṭaiya periya kaṭalil, kāṟṟāl tākkappaṭṭa marakkalam kaṭal nīraik kiḻittukkoṇṭu ōṭuvatupōlap, pōrkkaḷiṟu kaḷam pukuntu pakaivar paṭaiyait tākkip pāḻaṭittuk kaḷattil iṭam paṇṇiviṭa, avvāṟu iṭam kaṇṭa kaḷattil naṉku tīṭṭappeṟṟu oḷivīcum ilai pōlum muṉaiyaiyuṭaiya vēloṭu pukuntu, aracaiyum aḻittup pakaivar paṭaiyaiyum pāḻceytu, avveṟṟip pukaḻ nāṭeṅkum parava, pakaivar pōr muracaik kurutip puṉalai ulai nīrākak koṇṭu, ulaiyil, tacai, mūḷai mutalāyiṉa peytu, vīravaḷai aṇinta tōḷākiya tuṭuppuk koṇṭu, tuḻāvi ākkiya uṇavaip pēykaḷukkup paṭaittu, akkaḷattil kaḷa vēḷvi ceyta, kollum pōrvalla ceḻiya".

"naḷikaṭal irum kuṭṭattu

vaḷi puṭaitta kalam pōlak

kaḷiṟu ceṉṟu kaḷaṉ akaṟṟavum,

kaḷaṉ akaṟṟiya viyalāṅkaṇ,

oḷiṟu ilaiya eḵku ēnti

araicupṭa amar uḻakki,

uraicela, muracu veḷvi,

muṭittalai aṭuppākap

puṉal kuruti ulaik koḷīit,

toṭit tōḷ tuṭuppiṉ tuḻanta valciyiṉ

aṭukaḷam vēṭṭa arupōrc ceḻiya!"

puṟam : 26:7-17





nakaraṅkaḷil parattamaiai

nēriṭai nilai, uruvaka nilai ākiya ennilaiyil poruḷ koṇṭālum, nakara vāḻkkai eṉṟāl, nākarīka vaḷarcci eṉṟē poruḷ : nākarīkattiṉ teḷivāṉa muttirai, virumpat takātatu eṉak karutappaṭātāyiṉ, parattaimai oḻukkam iṭampeṟuvatēyām. parattaiyar āṭal pāṭalkaḷil naṉku payiṟci peṟṟiruppar, kaṇkavar vakaiyil uṭaiyaṇintiruppar. aḻakuṟa oppaṉai ceytu koḷvar. "viṟalpaṭa āṭavallāḷākiya viṟali, māṇikka : maṇikkōvai eṭṭāl āṉa mēkalai aṇiyāl aḻaku peṟṟa alkulum, maṭappam poruntiya mai uṇṭa kaṇkaḷum, oḷivīcum neṟṟiyum koṇṭu", tikaḻtalaik kūṟukiṟatu oru puṟanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuḷ.

"iḻai aṇip polinta ēntu kōṭṭu alkul

maṭavaral uṇkaṇ, vāḷ nutal viṟali”

-puṟam : 89 : 1-2





eḷitil vayappaṭakkūṭiya āṭavarait taṅkaḷōṭu, āṟṟuttuṟaiyilum, kuḷap paṭittuṟaiyilum nīrāṭi makiḻumāṟu tūṇṭa vallavar parattaiyar. āṭavaṉ oruvaṉait taṉ aḻakāl mayakkavalla uḷuram paṭaitta oru parattai, avaṉ maṉaivikkuk kīḻkkāṇum iṟumānta coṟkaḷaik kūṟi aṉuppiṉāḷ. ‘'eṉ kūntalil āmpaliṉ muḻuppūvai aṇintu aḻaku ceytu koṇṭu, putu veḷḷam puraṇṭōṭum periya nīrttuṟaiyil puṉal viḷaiyāṭṭai virumpic celkiṉṟēṉ. avaḷ kaṇavaṉum, aṅkut tavaṟātu vantu, puṉal viḷaiyāṭṭil eṉṉōṭu paṅku koṇṭu viṭuvāṉō eṉa avaḷ añcuvaḷāyiṉ, koṭiya pōr nikaḻumpōtu, pōr aṟam piṟaḻātu pōriṭṭup pakaivarai aḻikkavalla eḻiṉi eṉpāṉatu periya vēṟpaṭai pōrkkaḷattiṭattē uḷḷa avaṉuṭaiya periya āṉiraiyaip, pakaivar koḷḷāvāṟu cūḻa niṉṟu kāppatu pōla, taṉ kaṇavaṉ mārpu, eṉṉai aṇukaviṭāvāṟu, taṉ tōḻiyar kūṭṭattōṭu avaṉaik kāttuk koḷvāḷāka’

"kūntal āmpal muḻuneṟi aṭaiccip

perumpuṉal vanta iruntuṟai virumpi

yām aḵtu ayarkam cēṟum; tāṉ aḵtu

añcuva tuṭaiya ḷāyiṉ, vempōr

nukampaṭak kaṭakkum palvēl eḻiṉi

muṉai āṉ perunirai pōlak

kiḷaiyoṭum kākka, taṉ koḻunaṉ mārpē'’

kuṟuntokai : 80





viḻā nikaḻum iṭaṅkaḷil āṭavarkkuk kāma vēṭkai ūṭṭik kavarntu koḷvāṉ vēṇṭip parattaiyar peruṅkūṭṭamāka vantiruppar. vāḷai mīṉkaḷ, nīril, piṟaḻntu koṇṭiruppataik kaṇṭum, avaṟṟaik kavarntu koḷḷak karutātu, akkuḷattil vāḻum nīrnāy, nāḷtōṟum tuyil mēṟkoṇṭuviṭum iṭamākiya, vāri vaḻaṅkum kaivaṇmaiyuṭaiya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉukku uriya , kōyil veṇṇiyaic cūḻa uḷḷa vayalkaḷ viḷaintu kiṭakkum, nalla niṟam vāynta, muṟṟiya taḻaikaḷāl āṉa āṭaiyai, metteṉṟa akaṉṟa alkul mēl, aḻaku uṟa uṭuttukkoṇṭu, nāṉum iṅku naṭaipeṟum viḻāviṟkuc cella vēṇṭum. putupputu varuvāykaḷai uṭaiya ūraṉ, eṉṉai aṅku, akkōlattil kāṇpaṉāyiṉ, eṉṉai varaintu koḷḷātu viṭuttuppōvatu aritiṉum aritākum". "vāḷai, vāḷiṟ piṟaḻa, nāḷum

poykai nīrnāy vaikutuyil ēṟkum,

kaivaṇ kiḷḷi, veṇṇi cūḻnta

vayalveḷ ḷāmpal uruva neṟittaḻai

aitu akal alkul aṇipeṟat taii,

viḻaviṟ celīiyal vēṇṭum, maṉṉō !

yāṇar ūraṉ kāṇunaṉ āyiṉ

varaiyā maiyō aritē".

naṟṟiṇai- 390 : 1-8





parattai, āṭavar tēṭum veṟiyōṭu terukkaḷil varumpōtu, maṉaiviyar, tattam kaṇavarai viḻippōṭu kāttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. talaiviyai, avaḷ tōḻi ivvāṟu eccarikkiṟāḷ. "kaḷḷamilā nōkkamainta aḻakiya kaṇkaḷ, mayirccāntaṇintu maṇam ūṭṭappeṟṟa kūntal, parutta tōḷkaḷ, oḻuṅkuṟa vaḷarnta veṇpaṟkaḷ, tiraṇṭu neruṅkiya tuṭaikaḷ ākiya ittaku ciṟappukaḷāl oppuyavarvaṟṟa pēraḻakiyākiya parattai aḻakiya taḻaiyāṭai uṭuttum viḻā nikaḻ kaḷam polivu peṟa vantu niṉṟa viṭṭāḷ. avaḷ pārvaiyiṉiṉṟum nam kaṇavaraik kākka, tōḻimīr; eḻumiṉō eḻumiṉ!"

"maṭakkaṇ, takarak kūntal, paṇaittōḷ,

vārnta vāl eyiṟṟuc, cērntu ceṟi kuṟaṅkiṉ

piṇaiyal antaḻait taiit , tuṇai ilaḷ,

viḻavukkaḷam poliya vantuniṉ ṟaṉaḷē

eḻumiṉō eḻumiṉem koḻunark kākkum"

- naṟṟiṇai : 170:1-5





āṭavarai aṭimai koḷḷum parattaiyariṉ muyaṟci paṟṟiya maṟṟoru kāṭci itō; ‘'ūril viḻāvum muṭintu viṭṭatu, muḻaviṉ muḻakkamum aṭaṅki viṭṭatu; inniyiail ivaḷ yātu karutiṉaḷō eṉṟu kēṭpāyē yāyiṉ, kūṟukiṟēṉ kēḷ. oru nāḷ taḻai āṭai uṭuttu, attaḻai āṭai acaiyum alkulai uṭaiyavaḷāyt teruviṉkaṇ ipparattai ceṉṟāḷ. avvaḷavē, paḻaiya veṟṟikaḷ pala koṇṭa malaiyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāri, avvōrikku uriya nakariṉ oppaṟṟa periya teruvil veṟṟikkaḷippōṭu pukuntāṉāka , atukaṇṭa ōriyiṉ makkaḷ oṉṟutiraṇṭu āravārap pēroli eḻuntuviṭṭatu. atu kēṭṭatum, ārāyntu koṇṭa, ciṟanta vaḷaiyalkaḷai aṇinta aḻakiya mēṉiyai uṭaiya ivvūr makaḷirellām, taṅkaḷ kaṇavaṉmārkaḷai, avaḷ kaṇṇil paṭāvāṟu kāttuk koṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl ivarkaḷum naṉmai aṭaintārkaḷ. naṭantatu itutāṉ”,

“viḻavum uḻantaṉṟu muḻavum tūṅkiṉṟu;

evaṉkuṟit taṉaḷ kol eṉṟi yāyiṉ,

taḻai aṇintu alamarum alkul teruviṉ

iḷaiyōḷ iṟanta aṉaittiṟkup paḻaviṟal

ōrik koṉṟa oru perum teruvil

kāri pukkanērār pulampōl,

kalleṉ ṟaṉṟāl ūrē, ataṟkoṇṭu

kāval ceṟiya māṭṭi, āytoṭi.





eḻilmā mēṉi makaḷir -

vīḻu māntaṉar, tam koḻunaraik kāttē".

naṟṟiṇai : 320





aḷavukku mīṟiya parattaiyar oḻukkam, illaṟa vāḻkkaiyil periya pūcalukku vaḻivakuttuviṭṭatu. ūṭal eṉappaṭum, kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyarkaḷukkiṭaiyēyāṉa ciṟu pūcal, marutattiṇai cārnta pāṭalkaḷiṉ karupporuḷākiviṭṭatu. kīḻ varum pāṭṭu atu kuṟittatu. "veḷḷiya neṟkatirai aṟuvaṭai ceyyum uḻavar muḻakkum taṇṇumai olikku añci, avvayalil aṭaṅkiyirunta paṟavaikaḷ ellām eḻuntōṭi neruṅka amartalāl, vayal mītu tāḻa vaḷaintirukkum kiḷaikaḷaiyuṭaiya maruta marattiṉ, pūṅkottukkaḷ utirāniṟkum, iravalarkkuk koṭaip poruḷākat tērkaḷaiyē vaḻaṅkum vaḷḷa lākiya virāṉ eṉpāṉukkariya ciṟanta iruppaiyūr pōṉṟa eṉ aḻakellām keṭuvatāyiṉum keṭuka; eṉṉai neruṅka uṉṉai viṭuvēṉallēṉ. neruṅkaviṭṭāl eṉvāy uṉṉai vilakkiṉum, eṉ kaikaḷ uṉṉai aṇaittuk koḷḷum. āṉāl, nīyō parattaiyiṉ mārpāl mācupaṭṭa cantaṉam pūcappaṭṭa mārpiṉai uṭaiyāy; apparattai taḻuviyatāl vāṭiya mālaiyiṉai uṭaiyāy; attakaiya uṉṉait toṭuvatu, toṭattakātaṉa eṉak kaḻittuviṭṭa tāḻi mutalām kalaṅ kaḷai eṭuttu āḷvataṟku oppākum. ākavē eṉ maṉaikku vāraṟka; uṉṉai aṇaitta apparattai neṭitu vāḻka"...

"veṇnel arinar taṇṇumai verīip

paḻaṉap palpuḷ iriyak, kaḻaṉi

vāṅkuciṉai marutat tūṅkutuṇar utirum

tērvaṇ virāaṉ iruppai aṉṉa eṉ

tolkaviṉ tolaiyiṉum tolaika; cāra

viṭē eṉ; viṭukkuva ṉāyiṉ, kaṭaiik

kavavukkai tāṅkum; matukaiyam kuvavumuṟai

cāṭiya cāntiṉai ; vāṭiya kōtaiyai,





ācil kalam kaḻīi yaṟṟum;

vāral; vāḻiya! kavaiiniṉ ṟōḷē”.

naṟṟiṇai : 350





kaṇavaṉ piḻaiyai, maṉaivi, orōvaḻi maṉṉittu, avaṉai ēṟṟuk koḷvatum uṇṭu. “pāṇaṉē! arukē varuka! nalla aṇikalaṉkaḷai aṇinta eṉ maṉaivi, cuṟṟattār aṉaivarum kūṭiyiruntu ōmpa, mutal cūl uṭaiyāḷāki makavu īṉṟu, nam kuṭivaḷara vaḻiceytu, neyyōṭu kalanta veṇciṟukaṭukām vitaikaḷai nam māḷikaiyokum viḷaṅkap pūca, pāyalil paṭuttiruntavaḷai neruṅki aḻakiya kūntalai uṭaiya mutupeṇṭumākit tuyilā niṉṟaṉaiyē! eṉṉē niṉ ciṟappu! eṉap palappala kūṟip pārāṭṭi makavu īṉṟu māṇpuṟṟa avaḷ aḻakiya vayiṟṟai, eṉ kaikkuvaḷai malarāl taṭavik koṭuttu ētō cintaṉaiyil āḻntēṉ pōl ciṟitu poḻutu aṅku niṉṟirunta eṉṉai, mella nōkki, mullaiyiṉ arumpukaḷ pōlum paṟkaḷ tōṉṟa ciṟitē nakaittup piṉṉar, veṭkam vantuṟavē, nilamalar pōlum kaṇkaḷait taṉ kaikaḷāl mūṭikkoṇṭu makiḻnta kāṭci, eṉakku nakai taruvatāy iruntatu. atu kuṟittu nakaittu makiḻa pāṇaṉē! varuka”.





“vārāy pāṇa! nakukam, nēriḻai karumpuṭaik kaṭuñcūl nam kuṭikku utavi, neyyō ṭimaikkum aiyavit tiraḷkāḻ viḷaṅkunakar viḷaṅkak kiṭantōḷ kuṟukip putalvaṉ īṉṟeṉap peyarpeyarttu, avvarit titalai alkul mutupeṇ ṭākit, tuñcutiyō? mel añcil ōti ; eṉap paṉmāṇ akaṭṭil kuvaḷai oṟṟi uḷḷiṉeṉ uṟaiyum eṟkaṇṭu, mella mukai nāṇ muṟuval tōṟṟit takaimalar uṇkaṇ putaittu uvan tatuvē'’

- naṟṟiṇai : 370</poem>}} talaiviyiṉ ciṉam perumpālum taṇivatu illai. kaṇavaṉ vāyil vēṇṭip pāṇarnaṇpaṉait tūtu pōkkiṉum, atu payaṉ aḷippatillai . ciṉam māṟā oru maṉaiyāḷ kūṟuvatu itu: pāṇaṉē! makappeṟṟataṉāl uṟṟa vālāmai nīṅka eḻuppiya pukaiyum paṭintu, putalvaṉukkut tīṭṭum maiyum iḻuki, āṭaiyum aḻukkup paṭintuḷḷatu. cuṇaṅkāl aḻaku peṟṟa iḷaiya koṅkaiyiṉ iṉiya pāl peruka, atu karappap putalvaṉai aṇaittuk koṇṭamai yāl tōḷum muṭai nāṟṟam vīcukiṟatu. ākavē, ivai pōlvaṉavaṟṟāl tūymai keṭṭup pōkāmal putup polivōṭu iruppavarum, nalla pala aṇikaḷai - aṇintavarum ākiya parattaiyar cērikkaṇ tēril tirintu koṇṭirukkum uṉ talaivaṉukku, nāṉ takutiyuṭaiyavaḷ allaḷ. ataṉāl, poṉkampi pōṉṟa narampukaḷaik koṇṭa yāḻil eḻum icaikku ēṟpa, iṉkural eṭuttup pāṭutalil nī vallavaṉē āyiṉum, karuti vantataip peṟuvāṉ vēṇṭi, eṉṉait toḻutu pāṭuvatu ceyyaṟka kuḷirnta nīrttuṟaikaḷāl niṟainta nalla ūrukku uriyōṉākiya uṉ talaivaṉai iṅkiruntu koṇṭu ceṉṟu viṭuvāyāka. palarālum pārāṭṭap peṟum takutiyuṭaiyatākiya eṉ maṉaiyil iruntu nī pāṭātavāṟu, teruvil neṭumpoḻutu niṟkumāṟu tēril pūṭṭappaṭṭirukkum kutiraikaḷum annilaiyai veṟuttuk kural eḻuppukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, nāṉ virumpāta nilaiyil, payaṉil coṟkaḷai mēlum kūṟik koṇṭu niṟka vēṇṭām".

”neyyum kuyyum āṭi, maiyoṭu

mācupaṭ ṭaṉṟē kaliṅkamum; tōḷum

titalai meṉmulait tīmpāl piliṟṟap

putalvaṟ pullap puṉiṟunā ṟummē;

vāliḻai makaḷir cērit tōṉṟum

tērōṟku ottaṉem allēṉ; ataṉāl

poṉpuṉai narampiṉ iṉkural cīṟiyāḻ

eḻāal, vallai āyiṉum; toḻāal,





koṇṭu cel, pāṇa! niṉ taṇtuṟai ūraṉai

pāṭumaṉaip pāṭal kūṭātu, nīṭunilaip

puraviyum pūṇnilai muṉikuva;

virakil moḻiyal; yām vēṭṭatu ilvaḻiyē”.

- naṟṟiṇai : 380.





pōṟṟā oḻukkam uṭaiyarākiya parattaiyariṉ piṭikkuḷ akappaṭṭuk koḷḷum āṭavar ceyyum tavaṟukaḷukku aḷavē irātu eṉpatu nōkkik, kaṇavarai ēṟka maṟukkum maṉaiviyariṉ piṭivātattiṉ niyāyattaṉmai uṇarappaṭum parattaiyar oḻukkam koṇṭa kaṇavaṉāl puṟakkaṇikkap paṭṭāḷ oruttikku avaḷ tōḻi kūṟuvatu, itu; “aṉṉai pōlum aṉpuṭaiyāy! naṟiya neṟṟiyaiyuṭaiya arivaiyē! kātil kuḻai aṇintu mārpil mālai cūṭi, kaiyil toṭi aṇintu viḻāk kaḷattil tuṇaṅkai āṭikkoṇṭirukkum talaivaṉaik kaippaṟṟik koṇṭuvantu viṭalām eṉṟu, eṉ aṟiyāmaiyāl ācaippaṭṭu, viḻākkaḷum nōkkic ceṉṟu koṇṭiruk kumpōtu, nammiṭam ayalāṉ pōl naṭantukoḷḷum avaṉ, neṭiya periya terukkōṭiyil, oru vaḷaivāṉa iṭattil vēṟu oru vaḻiyākat tiṭumeṉa vantu etirppaṭa, avaṉai, “maṉaiyāḷaip pirintu parattaiyar piṉ tiriyum uṉṉaik kaṇṭittuk kēṭpār yārēṉum uḷarō? allatu oruvarumē ilarō'’ eṉṟu nāṉ kēṭka, avaṉ atupaṟṟi ētum karutātāṉ pōl, eṉṉaip pārttu, ‘uṉ neṟṟip pacalai naṉimiku aḻaku” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. pakaivarālum pārāṭṭattakka perum ciṟappuṭaiyāṉ eṉpatai maṟantuviṭṭu, “naṇpa! nī oru nāṇili” eṉak kaṭintu kūṟiviṭṭēṉ”

"aṟiyāmaiyiṉ aṉṉai ! añcik

kuḻaiyaṉ, kōtaiyaṉ, kuṟumpait toṭiyaṉ:

viḻavayar tuṇaṅkai taḻuukam cella,

neṭunimir teruvil kaipuku koṭumiṭai,

notuma lāḷaṉ katum eṉat tākkaliṉ,





'kēṭpōr uḷarkol? illai kol? pōṟṟu' eṉa

'yāḷatu pacalai', eṉṟaṉaṉ; ataṉ etir'

“nāṇilai eluva!” eṉṟu vanticiṉē;

ceṟunarum viḻaiyum cemmalōṉ, eṉa,

naṟunutal arivai! pōṟṟēṉ;

ciṟumai perumaiyiṉ kāṇātu, tuṇintē".

- naṟṟiṇai : 50





tuṇaṅkaiyāvatu, āṭavarum peṇṭirum paṅku koḷḷum oruvakai āṭal; āṭuṅkāl, kaikaḷai vaḷaittu oruvaraiyoruvar taḻuvikkoḷvar. atu, makkaḷaip pōlavē, pēykaḷukkum potuvāṉatu. ”kaṇṭārai uḷam naṭuṅkap paṇṇum naṭaiyum, kaṇṭārkku accam ūṭṭum uruvamum uṭaiya pēymakaḷ. ceṅkuṟuti tuḻāviya kūriya nakaṅkaḷaik koṇṭa viralkaḷāl kaṇkaḷait tōṇṭi uṇṇappaṭṭuviṭṭa muṭai nāṟṟam uṭaiya kariya piṇattiṉ talaiyait toṭiyaṇinta kaiyil ēntik koṇṭu, pakaivarkku accam varumāṟu, etir ceṉṟu aḻikkum pōrk kaḷattaip pāṭit tōḷ kuluṅka appiṇattait tiṉṉum vāyuṭaiyavaḷāyt tuṇaṅkaik kūttu āṭā niṟkum".

"urukeḻu celaviṉ, añcuvaru pēymakaḷ,

kuruti āṭiya kūrukirk koṭuviral,

kaṇtoṭṭu uṇṭa kaḻimuṭaik karuntalai

oṇtoṭit taṭakkaiyiṉ ēnti, veruvara

ēṉṟu aṭu viṟaṟkaḷam pāṭittu, tōḷpeyarā

niṇamtiṉ vāyaḷ tuṇaṅkai tūṅka".

tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai 57-56





yāḻ icaikkum pāṇarum kūttu āṭum kūttarum kūttiyarum, naṭaṉamāṭum viṟaliyarum oṉṟukalantē vāḻvar. oruvarukku oruvar tuṇaipurintu koḷvar. avarkaḷ, perumpālum pēraracarkaḷālum, kuṟunilat talaivar kaḷālum pēṇappaṭuvar. avarkaḷil oru kuḻuviṉar, orumuṟai, periya villāḷaṉākiya oru kuṟunilat talaivaṉaic ceṉṟu kaṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷuḷ talaivaṉ, pāṇaṉ. ākavē, avaṉ ēṉaiyōrai nōkki, ‘'nāṉ pāṭukiṟēṉ, viṟali; nīṅkaḷellām muḻaviṉai muḻakkuṅkaḷ, yāḻkaḷil paṇṇicai eḻuppuṅkaḷ ; kaḷiṟṟiṉ toṅkum kaipōlum tūmpu eṉappaṭum peruvaṅkiyattai icaiyuṅkaḷ; ellari eṉappaṭum calliyai vāciyuṅkaḷ. ākuḷi eṉappaṭum ciṟupaṟaiyai aṟaiyuṅkaḷ; patalai eṉappaṭum orukaṇ mākkiṇaiyiṉ oru pakkattai mellak koṭṭuṅkaḷ; namatu toḻil uṇarttum aṭaiyāḷak kōlām matalai mākkōlai eṉ kai tāruṅkaḷ'’.

"pāṭuval; viṟali! ōrvaṇṇam, nīrum

maṇmuḻā amaimiṉ; paṇyāḻ niṟumiṉ;

kaṇviṭu tūmpiṉ kaḷiṟṟuyir toṭumiṉ;

ellari toṭumiṉ; ākuḷi toṭumiṉ;

patalai orukaṇ pai eṉa iyakkumiṉ;

matalai mākkōl kaivalam tammiṉ".

puṟam : 152 : 13-18





pāṭṭuṭait talaivaṉ, avarkaḷukku iṟaicci kalanta uṇavum, iṉiya matuvum, poṉṉum niṟaiyat taruvaṉ eṉpatu kūṟat tēvai illai. pāṇaṉ kūṟṟil kūṟuvatāṉāl, “vēṭṭaiyil tāṉ eytu koṉṟa māṉiṉ niṇattōṭu kūṭiya iṟaicciyām pakkuvamākap paṇṇappaṭṭa uṇavōṭu, urukkiya āṉ neypōlum matuvaiyum tantu, taṉ malaiyil kiṭaitta kalappillāta poṉṉoṭu, palvakai maṇikkuviyalkaḷaiyum, vāṅkik koḷḷuṅkaḷ eṉa vaḻinaṭaiyil vāri vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ”.

"vēṭṭattil

tāṉ uyir cekutta māṉniṇap puḻukkōṭu

āṉ uruk kaṉṉa vēriyai nalkit,

taṉ malaip piṟanta tāvil naṉpoṉ

paṉmaṇik kuvaiyoṭum viraiik koṇmeṉac

curattiṭai nalki yōṉē” (puṟam : 152 : 25-30)





kaṭaṟkarait tuṟaimukaṅkaḷ kiṟittuva ūḻiyiṉ toṭakkattil, urōmāpuriyōṭu naṭaipeṟṟa vāṇikattil mikapperiya vaḷarcciyōṭu, kaṭaṟkarai nakaraṅkaḷum mikavum, mukkiyattuvam uṭaiyatāka uyarntuviṭṭaṉa. avvāṟē ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkaraiyil vaṭakōṭiyil kuṟippiṭakkūṭiyatu, piṟkālattil mallai eṉṟum, mēlum piṟpaṭṭa kālattil makāpalipuram eṉṟum aḻaikkap paṭṭatāṉa māvilaṅkait tuṟaimukamām. viḷaiyāṭum kūṭṭamākiya ciṟanta toṭi aṇinta makaḷir, paṉṟikaḷ uḻutu cēṟākkiya maṇṇaik kiḷaṟiṉāl ataṉ kaṇ pulāl nāṟum āmai muṭṭaikaḷaiyum, tēṉ nāṟum āmpal kiḻaṅkukaḷaiyum peṟum maṇvaḷamum, iḻum eṉum oli ōyātu olikkumāṟu nīrai veḷippaṭuttik koṇṭē yirukkam matakukaḷaik koṇṭa nīrvaḷamum uṭaiya perumaimikka māvilaṅkai".

ōrai āyatta oṇtoṭi makaḷir

kēḻal uḻuta iruñcēṟu kiḷaippiṉ,

yāmai īṉṟa pulavunāṟu muṭṭaiyait

tēṉ nāṟu āmpal kiḻaṅkoṭu peṟūum

iḻum eṉa olikkum puṉalam putaviṉ

perumā vilaṅkai "

-puṟam : 176 : 1-6





ittuṟaimukam maṟṟumōr iṭattil, maṇam nāṟum malarkaḷaiyuṭaiya curapuṉṉai , akil, cantaṉam ākiya maraṅkaḷai nīrāṭum tuṟaiyil nīrāṭum makaḷirkaḷiṉ tōḷkaḷukkut teppam ākit tuṇaipuriyumāṟu, karaikaḷaik kutti aḻikkum peruveḷḷam aṭittuk koṇṭuvantu tarum āṟṟuvaḷam mikkatum, aḻikkalākā āṟṟal vāynta tolperum nakarām ilaṅkaiyiṉ peyarait tāṉ tōṉṟiya pōtē peṟṟatumākiya māvilaṅkai" eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

naṟuvī nākamum, akilum, āramum

tuṟaiyoṭu makaḷirkkut tōḷ puṇai ākiya





porupuṉal tarūum pōkkarum marapiṉ

toṉmā ilaṅkaik karuvoṭu peyariya naṉmā

vilaṅkai”

- ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭai : 116 - 120





kāviri kaṭaloṭu kalakkumiṭattil, tollūāḻik kālattiliruntē pukaḻ peṟṟirunta pukār allatu kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉam iṭam peṟṟuḷatu. ākavē, atu pala pāṭalkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. appukārt tuṟaimukam “ mēlē viritta pāyaic curuṭṭa vēṇṭāmalum, ēṟṟiya pāraṅkaḷai iṟakkiviṭa vēṇṭāmalum, āṟṟu mukattut tuṟaimukattuḷ pukunta periya kalattiliruntu paṇiyāṭkaḷ, paṇṭaṅkaḷai, iṭainilai nakarukkuk koṇṭupōyc cērkkum” ciṟappuṭaiyatākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.

"mīppāy kaḷaiyātu, micaipparam tōṇṭātu,

pukāarp pukunta perumkalam, takāar,

iṭaippulap peruvaḻic coriyum”

puṟam : 30 : 11-13





pūkkaḷ utirntu parantu kiṭakkum akaṉṟa tuṟaikaḷai yuṭaiya kāviriyākiya periya āṟṟiṉ perukkeṭuttu varum veḷḷa nīr, nuṇmaṇal tiraḷumāṟu mēṭākkiya veṇ maṇaṟ kuviyalkaḷaiyum, putuvaruvāykaḷaiyum uṭaiya ūrkaḷai uṭaiya celvavaḷam mikka cōḻa vēntarkaḷāl purakkappaṭum ulakamellām pārāṭṭum naṉmai mikka nalla pukaḻ uṭaiyatāṉa nāṉmaṟaikaḷām paḻam nūlkaḷai aḷitta mukkaṇ celvaṉ kōyil koṇṭirukkum ālamuṟṟam eṉṉum iṭattil aḻakuṟa eṭukkappaṭṭa poykaikaḷaic cūḻntirukkum pūñcōlaikaḷil, maṉaiyuṟai makaḷir, aḻakuṟac ceyyappaṭṭa maṇaṟ pāvaikaḷai vaittu viḷaiyāṭum tuṟaiyiṉai uṭaiyatum, makarak koṭikaḷai ucciyil koṇṭa vāṉaḷāva uyarnta matilaiyum, muṭi aṟiyappaṭāvāṟu mika uyarnta māḷikaikaḷaiyum uṭaiya pukār” eṉṟum, pūkkaḷ malarntu maṇakkum nīṇṭa uppaṅkaḻikaḷiṉ, naṭuvaṇ, perumpukaḻ vāynta kāviriyiṉ karaikkaṇ uḷḷa nakaram" eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.

pūviri akaltuṟaik kaṉaivicaik kaṭunīrk

kāvirip pēriyāṟṟu ayirkoṇṭu īṇṭi

ekkar iṭṭa kuppai veṇmaṇal

vaippiṉ yāṇar vaḷaṅkeḻu vēntar,

ñālam nāṟum nalaṅkeḻu nal icai

nāṉmaṟai mutunūl mukkaṇ celvaṉ

āla muṟṟam kaviṉpeṟat taiiya

poykai cūḻnta poḻilmaṉai makaḷir

kaicey pāvai tuṟai

makara neṟṟi vāṉtōy puricaic,

cikaram tōṉṟāc cēṇuṇar nalil

pukā ar"

-akam : 181 : 11-22





pūviri neṭuṅkaḻi nāppaṇ; perum peyark

kāvirip paṭappaip paṭṭiṉam.

akam : 205 : 17-12





iraṇṭu pāṭalkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭirukkum, puṟantai eṉappaṭum puṟaiyāṟu, kiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkaraiyil uḷḷa maṟṟoru tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉam, periya alaikaḷ, oliyōṭu, iyakkamum aṭaṅkiyirunta, mēkam cūḻnta iravil, koḻutta mīṉkaḷaip piṭikkum paratavar, paranta kaṭalil, taṅkaḷ mīṉpiṭi timilkaḷil, kaṭaliṉ iruḷum nīṅkumāṟu ēṟṟiyirukkum viḷakkukaḷ, pōril puṟam kāṭṭāk kōṭpāṭṭiṉai uṭaiya vēntaṉ pācaṟaikkaṇ uḷḷa, oyātu āṭikkoṇṭēyirukkum iyalpuṭaiyavāya, pōryāṉaikaḷiṉ aḻakiya mukattil pūṭṭiyirukkum mukapaṭaṅkaḷiṉ oḷḷiya cuṭarpōlat tōṉṟum iṭamākiya, pāṭivarum iravalarkaḷaip piṟariṭam cellāvāṟu vaḷaittuk koḷḷavalla kaivaṇmai vāynta kōmakaṉākiya, kutiraikaḷ pūṇṭa ciṟanta tērppaṭaiyuṭaiya periyaṉ eṉpāṉukku uriya, malar virinta kottukkaḷaik koṇṭa puṉṉai maraṅkaḷ niṟainta cōlaikaḷāl cūḻappaṭṭa puṟantai” eṉṟum, ‘'kaḷ uṇṭu makiḻntirup pavaṉum, nalla tēr uṭaiyavaṉum ākiya periyaṉ eṉpāṉukku uriya , kaḷ maṇakkum poṟaiyāṟu” eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.

“peruntirai, muḻakkamoṭu iyakku avin tirunta

koṇṭal iraviṉ iruṅkaṭal maṭutta

koḻumīṉ koḷpavar iruḷ nīṅku oṇcuṭar

ōṭāp pūṭkai vēntaṉ pācaṟai

āṭiyal yāṉai aṇimukattu acaitta

ōṭai oṇcuṭar oppat tōṉṟum

pāṭunart toṭuta kaivaṇ kōmāṉ

pariyuṭai naṟṟērp periyaṉ, viriyiṉar

puṉṉaiyam kāṉal puṟantai muṉtuṟai”

- akam : 100 : 5- 13





‘'naṟavumakiḻ irukkai, naṟṟērp periyaṉ

kaḷkamaḻ poṟaiyāṟu”

- naṟṟiṇai : 131 : 7-8





pāṇṭiyariṉ mukkiya tuṟaimukam, koṟkai. atu muttukkaḷ viḷaikiṉṟa paranta kaṭalaiyuṭaiya koṟkai nakarattu muṉpuḷḷa tuṟaimukam ; “muttuppaṭu parappiṉ koṟkai muṉ tuṟai” (naṟṟiṇai : 23 : 6) eṉṟum, vīram ceṟinta pōril valla pāṇṭiyarkaḷ, aṟaneṟi keṭātu kākkum aḻakiya koṟkaip peruntuṟaiyil koḷḷum muttu ‘'maṟappōrp pāṇṭiyar aṟattiṟ kākkum koṟkaiam ‘peruntuṟai muttu” (akam : 27 : 9-10) eṉṟum; vīcum alaikaḷ koṇṭu vantu kuvikkum, kuḷirnta iṉiya oḷiyiṉaiyuṭaiya muttukkaḷ. ūrpavar. virumpumāṟu naṭakkum kutiraikaḷiṉ kāl vaṭukkaḷai maṟaikkum nalla tēraiyuṭaiya pāṇṭiyaratu koṟkai. ivartirai tanta īrṅkatir muttam. kavarnaṭaip puravik kālvaṭu tapukkum naṟṟēr vaḻuti koṟkai ' (akam : 130 : 9-11) eṉṟum, veṟṟiyē kāṇum pōril valalavarkaḷāy pāṇṭiyaratu, pukaḻ mikka ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya koṟkai : muṉ tuṟaiyil kiṭaikkum oḷivīcum muttukkaḷ: "viṟal pōrp pāṇṭiyaṉ, pukaḻmali ciṟappiṉ koṟkai muṉtuṟai avir katir muttam" (akam : 207:3-5) eṉṟum, kaṭal nīrp parappil ceṉṟu, pala mīṉkaḷaiyum piṭippavar, avaṟṟuṭaṉ cērakkoṇṭa muttuc cippikaḷai, nārāl vaṭittu eṭukkappaṭṭa kaḷḷiṉ, makiḻcci mikka vilaiyākak koṭukkum. mikka pukaḻ vāynta koṟkai", " parappil paṉ mīṉ koḷvar mukanta cippi , nārari naṟaviṉ makiḻnoṭaik kūṭṭum pēricaik koṟkai (akam : 296 : 8-10) eṉṟum, viḷaṅkum periya kaṭalil, etirttāraik kollum cuṟāmīṉkaḷai nīkkiviṭṭu valampuric caṅkukaḷai muḻkik koṇarnta periya mīṉpiṭi . paṭakukaḷai uṭaiya paratavar, piṭitta accaṅkukaḷiṉ kalle ṉum oliyiṉai muḻukka āravāram mikka koṟkai nakarattār varavēṟkak karaicērvar eṉṟum pārāṭṭap paṭṭuḷatu.

ilaṅku irum parappiṉ eṟicuṟā nīkki,

valampuri mūḻkiya vāṉtimil paratavar

olittalaip paṇilam ārppak kal eṉak

kalikeḻu koṟkai etirkoḷa iḻitarum".

-akam : 350 : 10-13





cērarkaḷiṉ mukkiya tuṟaimukam muciṟi . "cērarkku uriya cuḷḷiyākiya pēriyāṟṟiṉ veḷḷiya nuraikaḷ citaṟi vilakumāṟu, yavaṉarkaḷ koṇṭuvanta aḻakiya vēlaip pāṭṭāl ciṟappuṟṟa marakkalam, poṟkācukaḷōṭu vantu miḷakup potikaḷōṭu mīḷum, vaḷam mikka muciṟi".

cēralar,

cuḷḷiyam pēriyāṟṟu veṇnurai kalaṅka,

yavaṉar tanta viṉaimāṇ naṉkalam

poṉṉoṭu vantu kaṟiyoṭu peyarum

vaḷamkeḻu muciṟi'.





mīṉ viṟṟu vilaiyākap peṟṟa nellaik kuvittu, annellait tōṇikaḷil ēṟṟivantu maṉaikaḷil niṟaittu maṉaikaḷil kuvintukiṭakkum miḷakup poti ‘kaḷaip pēroli mikka kaṭaṟkaraiyil tāṟumāṟākap pōṭṭu vaippar. kalaṅkaḷil vanta poṟkācukaḷai uppaṅ kaḻikaḷil niṟkum tōṇikaḷil karai cērppar. malaipaṭu poruḷkaḷaiyum kaṭalpaṭu poruḷkaḷaiyum oṉṟu kalantu, vantu pukaḻ pāṭuvārkku vaḻaṅkuvar. kaḷvaḷam mikkatum, poṉmālai aṇinta cērarkku uriyatumāṉa, muḻaṅkum kaṭalpōl muracu muḻaṅkum muciṟi . ‘'mīṉ toṭuttu nel kuvaii,

micai ampiyiṉ maṉai maṟukkuntu;

maṉaik kuvai iya kaṟi mūṭaiyāl

kalic cummaiya karaikalak kuntu;

kalam tanta poṟparīcam -

kaḻittōṇiyāl karai cērkkuntu;

malaittāramum kaṭaltāramum

alaippeytu varunarkku īyum -

puṉalam kaḷḷiṉ, polantārk kuṭṭuvaṉ,

muḻaṅku kaṭal muḻaviṉ muciṟi”

puṟam : 343 : 7-10





kaṭaṟkarai vāḻ makkaḷ, kaṭalil kalaṅkaḷ kaviḻntu pōvatai aṟintiruntaṉar. ataṉāltāṉ aṭuttuvarum pāṭṭil, aḻakiya uvamai iṭam peṟṟuḷatu. “pāṇaṉ kaiyil uḷḷa, yāḻukku uriya ilakkaṇap paṇpukaḷōṭu kūṭiya nalla yāḻ, aḻakiya vaṇṭukaḷ pōla im eṉum icai eḻum, nī vaḻakkamāka varum teruvil, nī varuvatai etir pārttu uṉ mārpai, muṉpu tamakku uriyatākap peṟṟirunta, ciṟanta aṇikaḷai aṇintirunta parattaiyar palarum, nī kaiviṭṭuviṭṭa kavalaiyāl, kaṇkaḷiliruntu veppam mikunta kaṇṇīr corintavāṟē, koṭiya puyal kāṟṟu kaṭumaiyāka vīcuvatāl tuṉpuṟṟiruntapōtu, kaṭalil tām ēṟiyirunta marakkalam kaviḻntuviṭa, mēlum kalakka muṟṟut tāmum oru cērak kaṭalil vīḻntu āṇṭu mitantu vanta oru palakaiyai, vīḻtta aṉaivarum orucērap paṟṟik koṇṭu, tām tām taṉittaṉi iḻuppatu pōla, uṉ kaikaḷaip paṟṟi, avaravar nattam pakkam iḻukka, avariṭai akappaṭṭu nī varuntiya varuttattai nāṉ kaṇkūṭākak kaṇṭēṉ: kaṇṭa eṉṉāl yātu ceyya iyalum?"

"kaṇṭaṉeṉ; makiḻna! kaṇṭu evaṉceykō?

pāṇaṉ kaiyatu paṇpuṭaic cīṟiyāḻ

yāṇar vaṇṭiṉ immeṉ imirum

ērtaru teruviṉ etircci nōkki, niṉ

mārputalaik koṇṭa māṇ iḻai makaḷir,

kavalē muṟṟa ceytu vīḻ arippaṉi ,

kāl ēḻuṟṟa paitaṭu kālaik

kaṭalmaram kaviḻnteṉak kalaṅki uṭaṉpiṟappu

palar koḷ palakai pōl

vāṅka vāṅka niṉ tūṅku añar nilaiyē"



naṟṟiṇai : 30





inta atikārattil eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa pala ceyyuḷkaḷilum, iyalpāṉa allatu kaṟpaṉait tiṟamāṉa intiyak kalaiyiṉ muḻu mutal mūlamām ciṟappiyalpukaḷait taṉiyē kāṇalām. intiyak kalaiyiṉ nōkkam, toṭakka nilaiyil aḻaku paṭuttuvatē ; oṉṟaip pārttu, ataip piṉpaṟṟuvataṉṟu. kalaiñaṉ, ilakkiyap poruḷākiviṭṭa iyaṟkaip poruṭkaḷai, 'iṟṟu' eṉal pōl, uḷḷatu uḷḷavāṟē kūṟiviṭuvatai virumpavilla. māṟākat, taṉ kaiyil kiṭaitta apporuḷai aḻaku ceyvataṟkāṉa aḷaviṟku ellaiyē illai. eṭuttuk kaiyāṇṭa poruḷiṉ ovvoru ciṟu kūṟum, iyaṟkaiyōṭu kūṭiya tākat teḷivākap pulappaṭavalla, aṇikaḷiṉ ciṟukūṟu kaḷāl aḻaku ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. ākavē, ceyyuḷ naṭaiyil, ovvoru peyarccollukku muṉpum, oru peyaraṭaic col koṭukkappaṭum. avvāṟu koṭukkap paṭum iṭai oru toṭarāka amaintuviṭiṉ, attoṭaril iṭam peṟṟirukkum ovvoru peyarccolliṉ muṉṉum oru peyaraṭai koṭukkappaṭum. immuṟai , kirēkkak kalaik koḷkaikaḷil payiṟci peṟṟirukkum ōr uḷḷattaik kuḻappattiṟku uḷḷākki, āṅkilattiṟku moḻi peyarppatai mikak kaṭiṉamākkum vakaiyil, muḻuc ceyyuḷaiyum nōkkumpōtu, ōviyaṅkaḷāl oppaṉai ceyyappaṭṭa vāyil kōpuram pōl tōṉṟum varai toṭarntu naṭaipeṟum. teṉṉintiyak kalaiñarkaḷiṉ uḷḷam, veppa maṇṭalak kāṭukaḷil, ilaikaḷ malarkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ taṅkutaṭaiyaṟṟa vaḷamaikku aṭimaippaṭṭup pōṉataṉ viḷaivāṉa, avai paṟṟiya āḻnta cintaṉai, akkālattiya intiyak kalai vaḷarccikkut tūṇṭukōlāy niṉṟatu. caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam 1920



tirunelvēli, teṉṉintiya caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, limiṭeṭ,



talaimai nilaiyam : 154, ṭi. ṭi. kē. cālai, āḷvārpēṭṭai, ceṉṉai - 18.

appar accakam, ceṉṉai - 600 106.





inta miṉṉūlaip paṟṟi


uṅkaḷukku immiṉṉūl, iṇaiya nūlakamāṉa, vikkimūlattil iruntu kiṭaittuḷḷatu[1].

inta iṇaiya nūlakam taṉṉārvalarkaḷāl vaḷarukiṟatu. vikkimūlam patiya taṉṉārvalarkaḷai varavēṟkiṟatu. tāṅkaḷum vikkimūlattil iṇaintu mēlum pala miṉṉūlkaḷai aṉaivarum paṭikkumāṟu ceyyalām.

mikunta akkaṟaiyuṭaṉ meyppu ceytālum, miṉṉūlil piḻai ētēṉum iruntāl tayakkam illāmal, vikkimūlattil immiṉṉūliṉ pēccu pakkattil terivikkalām allatu piḻaikaḷai nīṅkaḷē kūṭa cari ceyyalām.

ippaṭaippākkam, kaṭṭaṟṟa urimaṅkaḷōṭu (potukaḷa /kuṉu -Commons /GNU FDL )[2][3] ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, inta uraiyai nīṅkaḷ maṟṟavarōṭu pakiralām; māṟṟi mēmpaṭuttalām; vaṇika nōkkattōṭum, vaṇika nōkkamiṉṟiyum payaṉpaṭuttalām

immiṉṉūl cāttiyamāvataṟku paṅkaḷittavarkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu:
